# 16-模拟单复盘空间-全量存档

本章是「模拟单复盘」空间的**全量只读存档**（跟进存档）：把该空间现行版本的全部源码原文与一份数据快照收进手册，单独成章，便于长期保存与查阅。第 15 章存的是 v102，本章存现行 v123，是同一空间的第二次存档。

- 空间名称：模拟单复盘
- slug：`mo-ni-dan-fu-pan`
- 存档时间：2026-11-17 10:55（CST）
- 线上现行版本：v123（2026-11-17 04:05 CST 保存；空间系统只保留最近 10 个版本，现存最早为 v114，v110 及更早版本快照已不可取回）
- 源码来源：该空间源码目录 `~/workspace/ts-spaces/mo-ni-dan-fu-pan/` 在存档时刻的文件原文，一字未改
- 数据来源：存档时刻通过空间动作 `listtrades`（limit=200）取得 `paper_trades` 全表快照，共 90 行，收录于本章最后一节
- 与第 15 章的差异：新增收录 `MIGRATION-NOTES.md` 与 drizzle 迁移 `0008`–`0010`（均为 v102 之后新增），共 30 个文件（第 15 章为 26 个）；其余体例不变

> 线上空间仍是工作副本，本章只是某一时点的只读存档，不会反向同步。交易台账的真源是 `~/workspace/paper-trading/records.md`，空间数据是其镜像；两者冲突时以台账为准。

## 排除清单（未收录部分及原因）

以下内容不属于空间自有源码，或属二进制/过程文件，未收录：

- `node_modules/`（约 133 MB）— 第三方依赖包，可由 `package.json` 与 `bun.lock` 重装
- `.bun-cache/`（约 9.2 MB）— bun 包缓存
- `audits/`（约 101 MB，139 个时间戳目录）— 历次构建/审计的过程记录，非源码
- `.harness/`（约 2.8 MB）— 构建工具的运行状态文件（plan.json、last_build.json 等）
- `.space-build/`、`.space-build-cvm/`（约 360 KB / 540 KB）— 构建产物目录
- `client/dist/`、`client/dist-cvm/`（约 356 KB / 536 KB）— 客户端编译产物，可由 `client/src/` 重新构建
- `server/dist/`（约 272 KB）— 服务端编译产物（actions.js），源头是 `server/src/actions.ts`
- `client/.tsbuildinfo.client`、`server/.tsbuildinfo.server` — TypeScript 增量编译缓存
- `app.db`（约 144 KB）、`app.db-shm`、`app.db-wal` — 运行时 SQLite 数据库二进制文件；其数据已改以 `listtrades` 快照形式收录于本章末节
- `icon.webp`（约 45 KB）— 空间图标，二进制图片
- `.xmds-icon`（9 字节）— 图标标记文件，非代码

## 代码全文

### space.json

```json
{
  "runtime": "typescript",
  "platformVersion": 28,
  "entry": "client/dist/index.html",
  "name": "模拟单复盘",
  "slug": "mo-ni-dan-fu-pan",
  "managedCronJobs": []
}
```

### package.json

```json
{
  "name": "hatch-ts-space-mo-ni-dan-fu-pan",
  "private": true,
  "type": "module",
  "packageManager": "bun@1.3.10",
  "scripts": {
    "typecheck": "bun x tsc --noEmit -p client/tsconfig.json && bun x tsc --noEmit -p server/tsconfig.json",
    "build": "bun run build:server && bun run build:client",
    "build:server": "cd server && bun build ./src/actions.ts --target=bun --outfile=./dist/actions.js",
    "build:client": "bun ./client/build.mjs"
  },
  "dependencies": {
    "@hatch/space-sdk": "file:/opt/hatch/skills/spaces/ts-runtime/dist/space-sdk.tgz",
    "@tanstack/react-query": "5.100.10",
    "drizzle-orm": "0.45.2",
    "react": "19.2.5",
    "react-dom": "19.2.5",
    "recharts": "2.15.3",
    "zod": "4.4.3"
  },
  "devDependencies": {
    "@types/bun": "1.4.2",
    "@types/react": "19.2.14",
    "@types/react-dom": "19.2.3",
    "tailwindcss": "4.1.13",
    "tailwindcss-safe-area": "1.3.0",
    "typescript": "5.6.3"
  }
}
```

### bunfig.toml

```toml
[install]
linker = "hoisted"
auto = "disable"
saveTextLockfile = true

# Each TypeScript web artifact gets its OWN bun install cache. The shared
# ~/.bun/install/cache/ is owned by hatch-daemon; if a TS web artifact install ran
# under a different uid (e.g. a developer testing via `sudo -u hatch`), the
# shared cache would acquire entries the daemon can't overwrite later
# (ENOTEMPTY on @hatch/space-sdk et al when bun renames the .tmp slot).
[install.cache]
dir = ".bun-cache"
disable = false
```

### tsconfig.base.json

```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "target": "ES2022",
    "module": "Preserve",
    "moduleDetection": "force",
    "moduleResolution": "bundler",
    "allowJs": true,

    "skipLibCheck": true,

    "tsBuildInfoFile": "./.tsbuildinfo"
  }
}
```

### AGENTS.md

```markdown
# Building this web artifact

This directory is a web artifact — a TypeScript space: a React client in
`client/`, server actions in `server/src/actions.ts`, the schema in
`server/src/schema.ts`, and Drizzle SQL migrations in `drizzle/` (see
`space.json` for its runtime and slug).

Build, audit, and ship it only through the web-artifact tools your builder
instructions describe. Do not hand-edit the built bundle under
`~/workspace/ts-spaces/<slug>/.space-build/`, and do not `bun run build`:
neither publishes the artifact.

If you are not the builder subagent (for example, the main assistant landed
here), do not build from this directory. List the existing artifacts and
request a change by describing the edit — that spawns a builder to do the
work.

## This artifact's data

This artifact's data lives in `~/workspace/ts-spaces/<slug>/app.db`, managed by
the app. If you are the main agent, follow your own Artifacts instructions for
reading and changing a web artifact's records. Do not run sqlite or scripts
against `~/workspace/ts-spaces/<slug>/app.db`.
```

### DATA-PLAN.md

```markdown
# Data Plan

## Context provenance
- `你分析分析现在的盘，然后过几个小时再判断你自己是不是对的，思路有没有问题，然后你就新建一个空间，在空间上面创一个表记录，相当于模拟单吧` (verbatim user request; defines the whole artifact: a paper-trade ledger where Muse records its own intraday calls, then reviews them hours later — direction right/wrong AND whether the reasoning itself had flaws)
- `我做的都是不超过一天的仓位` (prior user statement in parent conversation; every paper trade is intraday — the review horizon defaults to ~4 hours and no trade is framed as a multi-day position)
- Parent assistant message in the same conversation: Muse is fetching BTC/ETH/SOL now (现价、日线/4h/1h 均线状态、多空比), will write the first batch of judgments with 方向/入场/止损/止盈/依据 written down concretely, and will return ~4 hours later to review and update the table. The artifact's schema mirrors exactly those fields plus the review fields (判定、实际走势、思路复盘).
- Trading-manual context from parent conversation (YouTube tutorial handbook, verified against video frames): state before direction (trend vs range first), EMA alignment/density, strong vs weak trend — the 依据 field is free text so each entry records which of these the call rested on; the review's 思路复盘 judges that reasoning, not just the outcome.

## Tested sources
### Gate.io public REST API (api.gateio.ws) — no auth
**Used by**: `getLivePrices` action (live ticker for open trades + reference prices when recording/reviewing); the scheduled review agent task also uses it (via the review action's price snapshot and its own browsing) to fetch the post-entry candle window.
**Test command**: `curl -s 'https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/spot/tickers?currency_pair=BTC_USDT'`, `curl -s 'https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/tickers?contract=ETH_USDT'`, `curl -s 'https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/candlesticks?contract=BTC_USDT&from=<now-3600>&to=<now>&interval=5m'`
**Sample output**: spot BTC_USDT last=73469.8, change_percentage=-0.76, high_24h=74803.2, low_24h=73255.8 (2026-11-12 ~19:03 CST). Futures ETH_USDT last=2045.11, mark_price=2045.18, funding_rate=0.000057. Candlesticks return [{t,o,h,l,c,v,sum}] at 5m granularity — enough to determine whether stop-loss or take-profit was touched first inside the holding window.
**Processing**: server action fetches spot tickers for the symbols that have open trades, maps currency_pair→last price, computes floating % move vs entry (sign-flipped for shorts) and distance to SL/TP. Prices are fetched at open-time by the client (short cache), never simulated; if Gate is unreachable the UI shows the last recorded prices and an honest '行情暂时取不到' note.

### Binance USDT perpetual public API (fapi.binance.com) + Gate futures fallback — any-symbol prices (added 2026-11-12)
**Why**: recording the 19 Fanghua-signal altcoins (COTI/ELSA/JCT/UAI/CHIP/XAN/HUMA/INIT/HYPE/MAGMA/IDOL/ARKM/IRYS/MEGA/LSK/BEAMX/FLUID/HOT/AKE) failed: Gate *spot* has no pairs for most of them (futures-only listings), so watch （观望） records could not be priced/validated and live prices on open altcoin trades never loaded.
**Used by**: server helper `fetchSymbolPrice` in `server/src/actions.ts`, backing `getSymbolPrices` (arbitrary symbols), `getLivePrices` (open trades), and the watch-range validation in `addTrade`/`recordBatch`.
**Source order (revised 2026-11-12, fix for the reported 提取资料失败）**: ① Gate futures `GET https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/tickers?contract=XXX_USDT` ② Gate spot ticker ③ Binance `GET https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1/ticker/24hr?symbol=XXXUSDT` ④ Binance `GET https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1/ticker/price?symbol=XXXUSDT`. Rationale: Binance is geo-blocked from this egress (200 + restricted payload, no price), so running it first only burned time before the Gate-futures fallback that actually serves these futures-only altcoins; when that single fallback timed out, the whole batch failed — and every step's error was swallowed, leaving only 「币安与 Gate 均未返回行情」. The fetcher now returns each source's own failure reason per symbol, surfaced in `getLivePrices.failures` / `getSymbolPrices.error` and on the page. The review task's instructions were likewise switched to Gate **futures** tickers for the current price (they previously fetched spot tickers, which do not exist for most of this batch).
**Timestamp incident (2026-11-12)**: the 27 rows of rounds 2–3 were recorded with double-converted timezones — round 2 stored as 2026-11-13T04:25Z (true: 2026-11-12 22:25 CST = 14:25Z), round 3 stored as 2026-11-12T23:10Z (true: 2026-11-12 23:10 CST = 15:10Z) — leaving every opened_at in the future, which disabled the page's due-for-review button and would have emptied candle windows. `addTrade`/`recordBatch` now reject a future `opened_at` (>5 min ahead) at record time, and the one-time `repairOpenedAtBatch` action shifts exactly those two stored values back (declared as the exit's post-submit action; timestamps only, no terms or rationales touched).
**Plan （方案） stats (added 2026-11-12)**: rows carry an optional `plan` column (migration 0004); `getStats` returns `by_symbol` and `by_plan` breakdowns (counts, win rate over decided trades, total R, per-plan expectancy over decided trades). Pre-column rows are classified from the rationale tags （第二轮#N甲/乙·…, 「与甲路同一单」, 第三轮， 观望） by the shared `planOf` derivation on server and client.
**Test result (2026-11-12, sandbox + server runtime)**: Binance fapi answers HTTP 200 with `{"code":0,"msg":"Service unavailable from a restricted location..."}` and no price field from this egress — the fetcher detects the missing price and falls through. Gate futures returned live prices for every tested altcoin (HYPE 70.69, ELSA 0.06585, JCT 0.002917, HOT 0.0003792, COTI 0.00896, plus INIT/UAI in getLivePrices); a bogus symbol returns null from all sources.
**Watch-validation rule**: a watch record's range is checked against the symbol's live price when one can be fetched; when no source has the symbol, the check falls back to the submitted `entry_price` （判断时价） — the record is never rejected just because a live price is unavailable.

## Agent-task sources
### Scheduled self-review (~4h after each batch)
**Delivered by**: refresh action `runReview` → `ctx.agent.spawnTask` (fetches Gate candles/prices for the window since entry, judges 判定 对/错/未触发, writes 实际走势 + 思路复盘 back through the same action's save path) — installed as a silent space_action cron by the builder after the first batch exists; the review is Muse's own judgment work, not a mechanical price check, so it runs as an agent task rather than a pure function.
**Checked with**: Gate candlesticks probe above proves the window data the reviewer needs is retrievable.

## Ledger-first rework (2026-11-13, user request)
- User verdict: the page is a ledger/scoreboard, not a live terminal — trigger state is only ever settled by the K-line review, so a live-price floating P/L on the page was misleading (a never-triggered INIT 甲路 call displayed a fake +16.73% "浮动"). User explicitly declined the real-time observation direction (too costly) and asked instead: each trade's FINAL triggered P/L percentage written under its coin, trades sorted by time, inside a per-coin detail view that expands on tap.
- Client changes (no schema/server change): the `getLivePrices` polling, live bar, and all floating figures are removed from the page (`getLivePrices` action kept server-side for agent use). A conditional call that is still open now reads 「条件单 · 待结算」 with no P/L number and no 已持有 claim. Each reviewed non-watch trade shows a 「最终盈亏 ±X.XX%」 line derived from stored review fields only — exit = TP if `first_touch=tp`, SL if `sl`, else `review_price`; `no_trigger` settles at exactly 0% ("没有开仓，按 0% 记"); watch calls show no percentage. The flat 待复盘/已复盘 lists are replaced by per-coin expandable groups (groups by most recent call, trades inside in time order oldest→newest), each group header carrying the coin's call count, win rate, and the sum of its settled trades' final percentages.

## Card-layout refinement (2026-11-13, user follow-up)
- User clarified the coin-detail layout: the trade card front stays compact — symbol, direction, plan, entry/stop/take-profit, status, and (once settled) the final P/L percentage. The 判断依据 rationale no longer sits on the card front; it lives inside the coin's expanded dropdown, in a block directly under its own trade. Implemented client-side only: `TradeCard` renders the rationale only when `showRationale` is set (default off), and the coin group's expanded list renders each trade followed by its own 「本笔判断依据」 block.

## Live-view restoration (2026-11-13, user request — supersedes the ledger-first layout above)
- User rejected the pure-ledger layout: they want the FIRST version's flat card list back — every card shows its current state directly (terms 入场/止损/止盈， live 现价 + 24h%, floating figure, position bar between stop and target, 判断依据 on the card front), newest first, no tapping needed to see the current picture. The per-coin history survives as a tap target instead of a layout: tapping a coin's name on any card opens a panel listing that coin's every call, newest→oldest, settled ones carrying verdict + R + final P/L % + review price + outcome note + review time from stored review fields; open ones show only 待复盘 and their terms.
- Honest floating rules (the fake +16.73% must not come back): the page queries `getLivePrices` again (Gate futures → Gate spot → Binance fallback, per-symbol failures surfaced). Immediate open calls show 浮动 from the live price. Trigger-pending open calls NEVER show a floating % — the position does not exist until the K-line review rules it triggered — they show 「未触发 · 离触发价 X%」 (live distance to the recorded trigger price) plus the position bar. Watch calls show 较判断时 (live vs the judgment-time snapshot). A symbol no source can price shows 「现价取不到：<per-source reasons>」 on its card instead of a blank or a stale number.
- Trigger-pending is NOT read off `entry_type` alone: the round-3 dual-path rows (INIT/UAI/HYPE, ids 9–14) were recorded with `entry_type='immediate'` even though both dual-path plans （甲路·反弹空 = pullback trigger, 乙路·破位回踩空 = breakout-retest trigger) only become positions when their trigger fires — the INIT 甲路 row behind the fake +16.73% is one of them (stored immediate, entry 0.1347 vs judgment snapshot 0.12494 in its rationale). The client's `isTriggerPending` therefore treats an open non-watch trade as pending when `entry_type='conditional'` OR its derived plan starts with 甲路/乙路. Stored rows were not rewritten; this is a display rule matching the plans' recorded semantics, and the review flow already judges these rows by their written trigger conditions.
- Client-only change; server, schema, stats, add form and review flow untouched. The scoreboard and 分方案/分币种 breakdown sections stay as they were.

## Whole-card accordion + inline coin history (2026-11-13, user request — supersedes the tap-name modal above)
- User asked for the standard accordion pattern on the flat card list: tapping ANYWHERE on a coin's card (not just the small arrow) toggles its fold, and the coin's previous history P/L goes INSIDE that fold instead of a separate modal. Follow-up rule the same day: the whole page is single-open — expanding one card automatically collapses whichever card was open before.
- Client-only change: `TradeCard` no longer owns its expand state; `App` holds one `expandedId` (trade id) and passes `expanded`/`onToggle` down, so at most one card is expanded page-wide. The symbol name is no longer a button and `CoinHistoryModal` is deleted. The card keeps `cursor-pointer` + a rotating ▶ in the header and a labelled toggle button (aria-expanded) for keyboard/screen-reader access; the 立即复盘 button stopPropagation so it never toggles the fold.
- The expanded panel contains, in order: the trade's 判断依据 (previously behind its own small arrow — that per-card rationale toggle is gone, merged into this one fold), then a 「<币> 历史盈亏」 section: the coin's call count, win rate, cumulative R and settled-P/L sum (from `getStats.by_symbol` + stored review fields), followed by every call ever recorded for that coin, newest first — each row with time, direction, plan/round, a 本笔 marker on the card's own trade, verdict + R once reviewed, final P/L % and review price for settled trades, and the outcome note. Open trades in the history show only 待复盘/条件单 · 待复盘 and their recorded terms; no P/L is invented for them (same honesty rules as before).
- Everything else is untouched: flat first-version card fronts (terms, live price, floating/distance, position bar, final P/L line, review details), scoreboard, breakdowns, add form, review flow, server and schema.

## Top breakdown tables removed (2026-11-13, user request)
- User found the two stacked breakdown tables under the scoreboard （分方案 · 哪套打法管用 and 分币种明细 — the long per-plan / per-symbol count lists) ugly and asked to drop them, keeping only a per-coin call count written directly on the card UI.
- Client-only change: `BreakdownSection`/`StatLine` and their render site are deleted; the top of the page is now just the 4-box scoreboard followed by the action buttons and the card list. Each card's header line shows 「做了 N 次」 next to the symbol, N = the number of recorded calls for that coin (`historyTrades.length`, the same list the expanded history panel lists). The expanded panel still carries the coin's win rate / cumulative R / settled-P/L sum from `getStats.by_symbol`; `getStats` itself is unchanged server-side (its `by_plan` breakdown is simply no longer rendered).

## Detail-fix round (2026-11-13, user: fix the remaining nits without touching the look)
- Baseline frozen by the user: flat card list, live price, position bar, whole-card single-open accordion, expanded 判断依据 + coin history, 4-box scoreboard, ×N on the card, add form, review flow. This round changed only display details inside that frame; no server/schema/data change.
- Verified against live data first (getStats/listTrades/getLivePrices on the production copy): 27 trades (ids 4–30 — ids 1–3 were the first batch the user personally ordered deleted on 2026-11-12, so numbering starting at #4 is correct, not lost data), 24 open / 3 reviewed, all 3 reviewed are no_trigger (0 decided → win rate honestly "—"), all 22 open symbols priced via Gate futures with zero failures, no open dual-path card shows a floating figure, ×N counts match the data.
- Fixes: ① `fmtPrice` now scales decimals to magnitude (~6 significant figures): 0.0003924 no longer renders as 0.00039, 0.10988 no longer rounds to 0.1099. ② Position bar is a true ascending price axis for both directions (a short's low end is its take-profit; previously the bar computed over a negative span and read mirrored) and is only rendered while the live price is inside the stop↔target band — outside it, a line of text says which side price left by （越过止盈位/止损位/跑出观望区间） instead of a marker pinned at the edge. ③ Card header is a single non-wrapping row: the plan chip carries a genuinely shortened plan name （甲路·反弹 / 乙路·破位； full plan + round stay in the chip's title and in the expanded history rows) instead of the full name squeezing the status chip onto a second line or being ellipsis-clipped. ④ The duplicated text toggle under each card is gone; the header ▶ is now the one real button (aria-expanded), the whole card still toggles on click. ⑤ Expanded history lists oldest → newest (the user's stated order), no longer repeats the card's ×N as 「共 N 笔」, no longer sums settled P/L percentages into a meaningless 「已结算合计」 (per-trade % and cumulative R stay), and the current trade's history row no longer repeats the 实际走势 that the card itself shows below. ⑥ Scoreboard win-rate cell no longer wraps （0对/0错） into a taller box: with zero decided trades it reads 「胜率 · 尚无判定」, and the note under the scoreboard now states plainly that the reviewed count includes the no_trigger trades booked at 0 and excluded from the win rate. ⑦ Cards carry a hairline border so adjacent cards never sit flush against each other on a phone.

## Six-nit fix round (2026-11-13, user: 「都修」 on the health-check list)
- Client-only; no server/schema/data change; the frozen layout (flat cards, live price, position bar, whole-card single-open accordion, 4-box scoreboard, ×N, add form, review flow) is untouched apart from these six points:
  ① The expanded panel （判断依据 + 历史盈亏） now stops click propagation, so reading/selecting inside it no longer collapses the card (the card front and the toggle button still toggle; single-open verified live in the audit probe: expanding FLUID auto-collapsed HOT).
  ② The toggle glyph was the text character ▶, which phone browsers render as an orange play-button emoji — replaced by a single inline SVG chevron in the dim text color, rotating 90° when expanded.
  ③ Watch cards showed 观望 twice (direction chip + plan chip, since planOf returns 观望 for watch rows): the plan chip is now suppressed for watch trades on the card header, and history rows show only the round tag for watch trades instead of a second 观望 chip.
  ④ The ×N count badge renders only when the coin has more than one recorded call — ×1 was pure noise; ×2 on the dual-path coins (BTC/ETH/INIT/UAI/HYPE) is unchanged (the user asked what 甲乙双路单 means rather than choosing a counting rule, so row counting stays until they decide).
  ⑤ History rows no longer repeat a reviewed trade's outcome note: within one expanded card the sibling row's 走势 paragraph read as a second 「实际走势」 next to the card's own review section. 实际走势 now appears exactly once per trade, in its own card's review section; history rows keep time/direction/plan/verdict/R/final P&L %/review price.
  ⑥ Scoreboard win-rate cell: the label is now just 「胜率」 (nowrap) — 「胜率（3对/0错）」 wrapped and stretched the box on narrow screens. The 对/错 counts moved into the footnote under the scoreboard, which now reads 「已复盘 N 笔：X 对 / Y 错，另有 Z 笔未触发（没开仓、按 0 记，不算对错）」 whenever any trade is reviewed (the old footnote's 「所以胜率暂为 —」 was stale once decided trades existed).

## 盈/亏 mark next to the verdict (2026-11-13, user request)
- User rule: a finally-settled position trade shows one extra character after its verdict — 盈 if it actually made money, 亏 if it lost — because judgment （对/错） and money result are separate facts (e.g. ETH 甲路 would read 对·亏 once settled). Display-only; no data, stats, or win-rate change.
- Client-only change in `StatusChip` (the single verdict renderer used on both the card face for the latest settled leg and every leg in the expanded history, so both places share the caliber): `pnlMark(t)` returns the mark only when the row is reviewed, long/short, verdict correct/wrong, and finally settled. Finally settled = `first_touch` is tp/sl (the position exited at a level), or a closing review whose outcome note no longer reports the trade as 仍在进行中. Watch rows and no_trigger rows never get the mark; a reviewed-but-still-running row (current examples: BTC 乙路 id 5, ETH 甲路 id 6 — verdict correct, first_touch none, outcome_note says 仍在进行中， only an interim floating R of +0.01R / −0.18R after cost) gets NO mark until its final settlement. Sign from `finalPnlPct` (the 最终盈亏 % already printed in the history detail; same sign as the row's R): >0 → 盈 (long/green), <0 → 亏 (short/red), exactly 0 → no mark.

## Scoreboard detail windows (2026-11-13, user request)
- User: 「添加另一个窗口，点击那个累计R那个地方就显示逐笔明细，还有旁边的胜率和其他的都添加一下」 — every scoreboard cell is now a button opening a detail window (modal / bottom sheet on phone).
- Client-only change; no server/schema change — all windows render from the already-fetched `listTrades` rows + `getStats` aggregates, with the client's `settledR` mirroring the server's `realizedR` (watch excluded, no_trigger = 0) so the 累计 R window's per-trade list sums to the headline number.
- Windows: 累计 R → summary (逐笔合计 / 已结算仓位单数 / 平均每笔) + every settled position trade with terms, exit, verdict (+盈/亏 mark), final P/L % and its R; 胜率 → win-rate bar (对 vs 错), per-plan win rate + expectancy from `by_plan`, then 对/错/未触发 grouped per-trade lists; 总判断 → overview counts, per-symbol breakdown from `by_symbol`, then all trades newest→oldest; 待复盘 → open count + due (≥3.5h) count + every open trade with its waiting state. Backdrop tap or × closes.
- Follow-up (2026-11-13, user: 「未触发的就可以不用写在上面了」): untriggered (no_trigger) trades no longer appear as per-trade rows in any detail window — removed from the 累计 R list, the 胜率 window's 未触发 group, and the 总判断 window's 全部逐笔 list. Totals are unchanged (they book exactly 0R); their count still shows where it explains a denominator （胜率 header's 「X 笔未触发不计」, aggregate counts in by_symbol/by_plan rows). Card faces and expanded coin history are untouched — this trims only the scoreboard detail windows.

## 累计 R 明细排序 (2026-11-14, user request)
- User: 「那个给累计r的设置一个排序选项，按时间和最高r和负r」 — the 累计 R detail window's per-trade list gains a three-way sort control: 按时间 (newest first, default, unchanged from before), 最高 R (largest realized R first), 最负 R (most negative R first). Ties in R fall back to newest first. Client-only; the summary figures （逐笔合计 / 已结算单数 / 平均每笔） are order-independent and unchanged, as are the other detail windows.

## 盈亏胜率口径切换 (2026-11-14, user request)
- User: 「那个胜率也改一下，加入一个切换成盈亏的胜率的选项默认显示盈亏的」 — the win rate gains a second caliber, 盈亏胜率 (P/L win rate), shown by default, with a toggle back to the original 判断胜率.
- Definition (mirrors the existing 盈/亏 mark exactly, so every surface agrees): 盈亏胜率 = 最终盈利笔数 ÷（盈利 + 亏损）, over reviewed long/short trades with verdict correct/wrong that have FINALLY settled (first_touch tp/sl, or a closing review whose note no longer says 仍在进行中）; sign from the trade's realized R / final P/L % — a call judged 对 that lost money counts as a loss. Watch calls, no_trigger calls, still-running interim reviews and exactly-0 results are excluded from both numerator and denominator.
- Server: `getStats` now also returns `pnl_win` / `pnl_loss` / `pnl_win_rate` overall and in every `by_symbol` / `by_plan` bucket (new `pnlOutcome` helper next to `realizedR`). No schema/data change.
- Client: one page-level `winRateMode` state (default `"pnl"`) drives the scoreboard cell (label becomes 盈亏胜率）, the toggle row under the scoreboard （胜率口径： 盈亏胜率 / 判断胜率）, the win-rate detail window (its own toggle, P/L headline + 盈/亏 bar, per-plan 盈亏胜率 rows, 盈利的/亏损的 per-trade lists classified by the same `pnlMark`), the 总判断 window's per-symbol rows, and each coin card's header rate. The judgment caliber's window content is unchanged.

## 甲乙卡面规则 + 轮次筛选 (2026-11-14, user request)
- User: 「甲乙路触发了其中一个就不要标在上面了，也没什么用，你自己记录就行了，如果都没触发就都写上去」 — dual-path face rule, scoped to the coin's newest batch and implemented in `SymbolCard` (client-only; stored rows unchanged, the 甲/乙 record stays in the expanded history): a leg counts as triggered when its review verdict is 对/错 (no_trigger = never fired; an open leg's trigger is unsettled by definition). A pair is recognised by batch shape — same coin, same recorded instant, ≥2 position legs labelled 甲/乙 or stored conditional — because some real batches (round 5's altcoin rescan pairs, e.g. ELSA ids 53–54) carry no 甲/乙 label in their plan column at all, only in the review text. If one leg of the pair triggered, the card face shows only the triggered leg with the 甲/乙 plan chip hidden, and a still-open voided sibling is dropped from the face. If a pair both settled as no_trigger （都没触发）, both legs are written on the face (previously only the latest settled leg showed). While both legs are still open, both stay on the face with labels as before.
- User: 「做一个目前是第几轮的显示，加一个可以选择每一轮的选项，相应的调整主页显示的单子，默认显示当天那轮的」 — a round bar under the scoreboard shows 目前是第X轮， and the coin-card list (majors/altcoins sections, per-card counts and expanded records) filters to the selected round. Card-header figures follow the filter too: each coin card's win rate and 累计 R are computed from the trades the card actually shows (the selected round's), not the all-rounds `by_symbol` totals — otherwise a round view would print e.g. ETH +3.70R （全轮次） next to a 2-trade count whose round sum is +2.00R. Presentation follow-up the same day: the per-round options must NOT lie flat on the homepage — the round bar is one compact line （目前是第X轮 + a 「看第X轮 · N 笔（当天）›」 button) and the options （当天这轮（默认）/ each round with its count / 全部轮次） live in a picker window (bottom-sheet modal, same pattern as the scoreboard detail windows) that opens on tap and closes on pick. Default selection (no explicit pick) is 当天那轮 — the round of the newest call recorded on today's Asia/Shanghai date — falling back to the latest round when nothing was recorded today. Scoreboard totals and detail windows stay global across all rounds. Round labels derive from the 第X轮 tag in rationale/plan, in Chinese OR Arabic numerals (round 5's batches write 第5轮； the previous `roundOf` only matched Chinese numerals and missed them), with batch inheritance: rows sharing a recorded instant with a tagged row inherit its round (round 3's 乙路 rows say only 「同上」and its watch batch is untagged — without inheritance a round filter would split 甲/乙 pairs).

The empty-round note and intro copy agree. A *recorded* watch call is unchanged: it still renders as a normal card carrying its own recorded range/terms, distinct from this no-record placeholder.

## BTC 实时浮动试点 (2026-11-15, user request)
- User: after a mainstream call enters, show its floating P/L % live, exchange-style, with the price ticking — piloted on BTC only, after being told the token cost is ~zero when the browser streams the feed itself. Hard layout constraint added the same day: no layout change, no moved elements, no new blocks/sections/tabs — the live figures go into the BTC card's existing slots in the existing type sizes/colors.
- Source: OKX public market data, browser-direct (no server, no model, no tokens): WebSocket `wss://ws.okx.com:8443/ws/v5/public`, subscribe `tickers` for `BTC-USDT-SWAP` (the mainstream calls fill on the OKX demo venue, so this is the same price source as the fills); 20s client ping, auto-reconnect on close (3s), REST fallback `GET https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/ticker?instId=BTC-USDT-SWAP` every ~5s whenever the socket is down or has not delivered within 6s of page open (both endpoints verified live from this sandbox: REST returned last=64869.9; the sandbox's own WebSocket egress failed its handshake, which is exactly the case the REST fallback exists for — the user's browser connects directly).
- Display, in place only: on the BTC card, each open leg's existing 现价/浮动 row is driven by the OKX feed instead of the polled Gate price — the price flashes green/red per tick, a small dot + 「OKX 实时/轮询/连接中/重试中」 tag sits in the same line, the position bar follows the same price, and a position leg's 浮动 is quoted exchange-style: 方向 × (现价 − 入场价) / 入场价 × 10 (leverage) × 100%, plus 浮动 R = 方向 × (现价 − 入场价) / |入场价 − 止损价|, in the existing 盈/亏 colors. Trigger-pending legs keep their 未触发 · 离触发价 % (gap now measured against the OKX price); watch legs keep 较判断时. ## BTC 实时推导改按 feishu-trader 资金方案 (2026-11-15, user request — supersedes the 5U sizing in the two sections above)
- User: 「按照止盈止损的来，不固定盈亏，我只知道设置持仓量和杠杆，你还记得我之前设置那个自动化交易的那个资金方案吗？按那个来」 — the BTC live row's derivation switches from the per-leg fixed-risk 5U caliber to the feishu-trader automated-trading capital plan. Client-only, display-only, scoped to OPEN (unsettled) BTC legs' live row and direction label; settled history legs keep their original x10 label and figures (10x/50U-era records, not rewritten). ETH/SOL/altcoins, layout, settlement and the review flow untouched.
- Leverage selection (BTC): candidates [100, 75, 50, 40, 30] high → low; estimated liquidation with MMR = 0.005 — long liq = entry × (1 − 1/lev + 0.005) must be < stop, short liq = entry × (1 + 1/lev − 0.005) must be > stop; first passing tier wins, all-fail falls back to 30. Direction chip on open legs reads 「做多 x<选档>」 (`btcLeverage` in App.tsx); in the expanded history the same open leg shows the selected tier while reviewed legs keep x10.
- Position: margin = 100U × 40% = 40U; contracts = max(1, floor(40 × lev ÷ (0.01 × entry))); notional U = contracts × 0.01 × entry, rendered 「持仓 N U」 as before. Floating % = direction × (price − entry) / entry × lev × 100%. Floating amount = contracts × 0.01 × (price − entry) × direction — no longer floating R × 5U; P/L is unfixed and follows notional × move. Floating R unchanged (price move ÷ stop distance).
- Demo-leg check (id 68, long, entry 64,500 / stop 64,000): 100x liq 64,177.5 fails (inside the stop), 75x liq ≈63,962.5 < 64,000 → x75; contracts floor(40×75÷645) = 4 → notional 2,580 U; at price 65,005.8: +58.81%, +20.23 U, +1.01R — verified by direct computation against the brief's figures.
- The collapsible list note's BTC sentence was rewritten to the new caliber （保证金 40U、杠杆按强平校验选档、持仓为名义仓位、盈亏不固定随名义×涨跌幅）; the 5U/10x wording is gone from the note.

- User: 「100u写得精致一点带颜色，爆仓了就通过p通知我但是爆仓了也不要停后台的测单实验不影响后续r的评价，然后那个下面的那条止盈止损动态条弄一个渐变的形式，越靠近止盈就越绿，止损也一样，下面的止盈止损数字也优化一下」 — three in-place visual changes plus a semantics correction; no layout, data, or settlement-caliber change:
  ① Card-header balance is now a small tinted pill (`AccountBalanceBit`): rounded-full, tinted background + same-hue border/text via color-mix on the theme tokens. Tiers: balance ≥100 → green (`--long`); 50–100 → warm neutral (`--accent` gold); <50 → amber (new `--warn` token, #d97706 light / #f59e0b dark); busted → red 「已爆仓」 pill as before. Number formatting unchanged (whole numbers bare, otherwise one decimal). Both header call sites (SymbolCard, EmptyMajorCard) unchanged — the pill renders inside the existing slot.
  ② `PositionBar` (the stop↔target dynamic bar on every open leg, majors' OKX-driven legs included since they share the component) is now a gradient track for position legs: take-profit end green (`--long`), stop end red (`--short`), blending through the gold accent at the middle — ends follow whichever side of the ascending price axis each level sits on, so a short's low (target) end is green. The marker grows slightly (3.5, surface-colored ring + shadow) and its own fill interpolates green↔red by proximity to the target (`color-mix` weighted by tpFrac), so it visibly greens near the target and reddens near the stop as the live price moves. Watch-range bars keep the neutral track (no stop/target semantics to color).
  ③ The labels under the bar are reworked: the level word stays dim, the level number itself is semibold and colored （止盈 green / 止损 red; watch bounds stay plain text color), and a small dim signed percentage beside each number gives that level's distance from the current price ((level − price) / price). Nothing else in the row moves.
  ④ Bust semantics corrected (supersedes the 「爆仓出局」 wording in the previous section): balance ≤ 0 only MARKS the coin busted — the user is push-notified separately (PushDeer, handled by the parent flow, not the page), background paper testing keeps running for the coin, later R evaluation is unaffected, and the balance may go negative and keeps being recorded. The collapsible list note's account sentence now says exactly that, and the `getCoinAccounts` description + schema comment agree. The artifact itself never gated anything on `busted` (display-only flag), so no behavior change was needed server-side.

## 主流实时价数据源 OKX → Gate (2026-11-15, user request)
- User context: the phone routes the whole okx.com suffix DIRECT (so the OKX app itself is fast direct), which makes the page's browser-direct OKX feed (ws.okx.com / www.okx.com) unreachable from China and the cards showed 连接失败； carving those two shared web domains back to PROXY made the OKX app lag again — app and page share the domains and cannot be split. Fix: the majors' live price source moves from OKX to Gate; gateio.ws is not in the user's DIRECT list, so it naturally goes through their proxy node, and the user can delete the two PROXY exception lines and keep okx.com fully DIRECT.
- Client-only, display-only; layout, displayed figures and every calculation caliber unchanged (floating %, floating R, P/L amount, position bar and marker all keep consuming the live price exactly as before; position sizing still follows the feishu plan with the OKX demo contract faces — only the price feed's venue changed, and majors' Gate/OKX prices track within a fraction of a percent). Settled legs and ledger data untouched; server and schema untouched.
- WebSocket: Gate futures feed `wss://fx-ws.gateio.ws/v4/ws/usdt`, subscribe `{time, channel: "futures.tickers", event: "subscribe", payload: ["BTC_USDT"|"ETH_USDT"|"SOL_USDT"]}`, updates arrive as `result: [{contract, last, …}]`, heartbeat `{time, channel: "futures.ping"}` every 20s. Endpoint correction vs the brief: the brief named `wss://api.gateio.ws/ws/v4/`, but live testing from this sandbox (2026-11-15) showed that endpoint is Gate's spot socket and answers the futures.tickers subscribe with `{"error":{"code":2,"message":"Unknown channel futures.tickers"},"result":{"status":"fail"}}`; the fx-ws host answered `{"result":{"status":"success"}}` and pushed BTC_USDT `last` updates ~1/s (64407.9 → 64400.3 → 64409.0…). fx-ws.gateio.ws is the same gateio.ws family, so the routing property the switch exists for is identical.
- REST fallback (as specified): `GET https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/tickers?contract=BTC_USDT` (ETH/SOL likewise), ~5s polling under the same conditions as before; verified live from this sandbox (BTC last 64401.8, ETH 1791.96, SOL 70.36 at test time). Response is a bare array, price field `last`.
- Status tag wording: source name OKX → Gate （「Gate 实时」「Gate 轮询」; 连接中/重试中 unchanged). The collapsible list note's live-feed sentence now says 浏览器直连 Gate 实时推送. All `useOkxLivePrice`/`Okx*`/`okxLive` identifiers renamed to their Gate equivalents; no okx.com URL remains in the client.

If the brief's list came from some other record, that record needs reconciling with the ledger.
- Client display: `PriceTriplet` shows 止盈1/止盈2 in a four-column row when TP2 exists (single-target legs keep the plain 止盈 three-column row; watch rows unchanged). `PositionBar`'s band and `LiveRow`'s out-of-band check now run stop ↔ farthest target (TP2 when present), the far end labelled 止盈2, with a tick + middle label marking 止盈1 inside the band. Path identity: `pathOf` derives 甲路/乙路 from the plan column （甲路·…/乙路·… plans) or the rationale's leading ·甲空/·乙多 tag (round-7 shape), rendered as a solid accent `PathBadge` at the head of every dual-path leg — card face (open and settled blocks, still suppressed where the standing dual-path face rule hides the plan of a settled triggered leg), expanded history rows, and scoreboard detail rows. Non-dual-path legs get no badge.

## Long-term data behavior
- **Refresh policy**: live prices on page open (and manual 刷新行情 button) via getLivePrices; verdicts/reviews written by the scheduled review task ~4h after entry and whenever Muse records a new batch (each batch schedules its own review run). No always-on polling.
- **Growth**: one row per paper trade; expected a handful per day at most. List returns newest first, capped at 200.
- **Ordering**: trades ordered by entry time descending; stats computed over reviewed trades only.
- **Time semantics**: entry/review instants stored as epoch-ms timestamps; rendered in the viewer's local timezone (Asia/Shanghai for the user). Holding window and '已持有多久' derived from entry instant.

## 山寨币整体排行切换 (2026-11-15, user request)
- User: 「给山寨画红圈那里加一个整体胜率排行选项还有r高低排行，紫色那里是选择了那个选项之后显示的数据，比如我选胜率排行就显示总笔做过的胜率」 — the 山寨币 section header (red circle: the band's right side) gains a three-way sort control 默认 / 胜率排行 / R排行， and each altcoin card's header band (purple circle: next to the summary line) shows the selected metric. Client-only; no server/schema/data change.
- The ranked metric is OVERALL （整体/总笔）: computed from the coin's trades across ALL rounds (the unfiltered groups), never just the round on screen — win-rate ranking shows the win rate over every trade the coin has ever had, R ranking its total settled R. The win rate follows the page's current 胜率口径 (default 盈亏胜率； the shared `coinFigures` helper now serves both the card header's round-scoped figures and this overall computation, so the calibers cannot drift).
- With a ranking on, the visible altcoin cards sort by that metric, highest first; a coin with no decided trade sinks to the bottom of the win-rate ranking and ties keep the standing most-recent-call order. Each alt card header gains an accent-outlined pill: `#名次 整体胜率/整体盈亏胜率 XX%` or `#名次 总R ±X.XXR` （尚无判定 reads —). 切回「默认」 restores the standing order and removes the pills. Majors section, round filter, scoreboard and detail windows untouched.

## Rejected approaches
- **Binance public API**: tried `curl https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ticker/price?symbol=BTCUSDT` — returns 'Service unavailable from a restricted location' from this egress. Rejected.
- **CoinGecko simple/price**: works (BTC 73387, ETH 2043.16, SOL 82.94 in probe) but rate-limited, USD spot only, no candles/funding — kept as nothing; Gate covers everything including the user's actual venue (Gate.io, per memory). Rejected as primary.
- **Seeding example trades**: rejected — the table must contain only real calls Muse actually made against the live market; it starts empty and the first real batch is recorded by the parent flow after build.
- **Imagery**: not needed — this is a numeric trading ledger; no visual subjects, and decorative imagery would fight the data-dense design.
- **Space icon (2026-11-15, superseded same day)**: 3D gold Bitcoin coin with ₿ relief, sourced from CityPNG — image: https://www.citypng.com/public/uploads/preview/hd-realistic-3d-btc-cryptocurrency-coin-png-735811696955047sruucnajv9.png?v=2026021516 (source page: https://www.citypng.com). The downloaded 800×800 preview had its checkerboard baked in as opaque pixels (alpha fully 255), so the neutral-gray checkerboard background was flood-removed locally and the cleaned bytes were installed as the artifact's own `icon.webp` / `icon.png` — no hotlink to the source site. Replaced later the same day at the user's direction (see next line).
- **Space icon (2026-11-15, current)**: user-supplied image, uploaded by RGB in chat — white background, thick black outline circle with a ₿ symbol inside (local source file: `~/workspace/user/files/2C59E21C-4798-499F-BABB-055E7B16EBF7-photo-11C26A47-2C72-4CC6-85D4-314914650FE0.jpg`, 1170×1170 JPEG). Cropped to the circle's bounding square with a small white margin, colors/elements untouched, exported square 512×512 as the artifact's own `icon.webp` / `icon.png`. Installed together with its registration record: `.harness/icon_pool_selection.json` now records `source: "user_provided"`, `asset_key: "user-provided:bitcoin-b-circle"`, and the sha256 of the installed `icon.webp` (b7120e4c…), read back and verified to match the file byte-for-byte — earlier attempts failed because the icon file was replaced while the registration record still pointed at a pool asset.

## 状态改名 + 未触发不画止盈止损 (2026-11-15, user request)
- User: 「没到条件就不要把止盈止损画出来，你改一下单的名字，现在太乱了」 — the overlapping state vocabulary （条件单·待结算 / 待复盘 / 已到期·待复盘 / 未触发） is replaced by ONE lifecycle naming, and a leg that has not triggered yet no longer draws its stop/targets anywhere. Follow-up ruling the same day: 甲路 gets NO condition split (its only condition is price reaching the entry, which is the trigger itself), 乙路 keeps the two condition tiers because it has the breakout precondition.
- The naming (client `legState` in App.tsx, applied on the card face, the expanded history and the scoreboard detail rows):
  - 甲路 open, trigger unconfirmed → 「未触发」 (plain conditional singles without a dual path read the same).
  - 乙路 open → 「条件未满足」 until a 15m close beyond its breakout line has happened, then 「条件满足·未触发」 while the retest of the entry is still pending.
  - In position → 「已触发·待观察」: immediate open legs, and interim-reviewed legs whose review found them triggered and still running （仍在进行中） — those previously wore a verdict chip as if settled; they now also count as 进行中 (not 已结算） in the card-header counts.
  - Finally reviewed → 「已结算」 leads the chip; the verdict （判断对/错/未触发）, the 盈/亏 mark and the booked R follow inside the same chip, so no information the old verdict chip carried is lost. Watch calls are not orders and keep their existing labels throughout.
- 乙路 condition check (new server action `getConditionStates`, polled by the client every 60s alongside the live prices): the breakout line is parsed from the leg's rationale (the recording convention writes 「15m收盘破位0.0585后回踩0.0600确认入场」/「15m收盘站上0.01150后回踩…」; older rows name it （触发0.0635）), then Gate futures 15m candlesticks since the leg's `opened_at` are fetched and any candle CLOSING after placement beyond the line marks the condition met — the same source and caliber the settlement review uses, display-only (nothing is written back; the review still decides the trigger itself). Unparseable line or failed fetch → 条件未满足 (no evidence the break happened). Verified live at build time against the real ledger: ELSA 乙 (id 84, line 0.0585) — closes since placement 0.05871–0.05989, none below the line → 条件未满足； INIT 乙 (id 74, line 0.1170) — closes down to 0.11425 → 条件满足， matching the settlement flow's own finding that INIT's breakout premise was established while ELSA's never was.
- Drawing rule: a pre-trigger leg （未触发 / 条件未满足 / 条件满足·未触发） renders ONLY its trigger price — `PriceTriplet` collapses to the single 触发价 column, `LiveRow` renders no `PositionBar` (the bar is drawn between stop and targets) and no 越过止盈/止损位 note; the scoreboard detail rows hide their 止损/止盈 spans the same way. In their place the live row carries the state + the distance that matters （甲路： 离触发价； 乙路未破位： 离破位线； 乙路已破位： 离入场价）, plus one honest note when price has already reached the entry — for an unbroken 乙路 leg it reads 「现价已到入场价，但破位条件未满足，不算触发」 (the exact ELSA case the user hit: a plain limit order fills there, this leg does not). Once a leg triggers, the full triplet, the bar and the floating figures return unchanged; settled legs keep their full recorded terms. The previous round's gray-dot/gray-tail treatment for untriggered bars is superseded — there is no bar to gray. `PositionBar`'s now-unreachable `pending` gray branch was removed.
- The collapsible list note was rewritten in the new vocabulary; the add-form's entry-type option keeps the word 条件单 (it names the recorded order type, an input choice, not a displayed state).

## 未触发阶段出灰色数据条 (2026-11-15, user request — refines the drawing rule above)
- User: 「甲乙到了未触发这个时候就可以出那个灰色数据条了」 — once a 甲/乙 leg reaches a 未触发 stage, the gray data bar comes back. Client-only display change; no schema/data/settlement change.
- Rule by stage: 乙路 still at 条件未满足 → no bar at all (unchanged from the previous section). 甲路 未触发 and 乙路 条件满足·未触发 → `LiveRow` renders `PositionBar` in its restored `pending` gray mode: the price-marker dot and the fading tail use neutral gray (`--dim`, same gradient geometry and direction rules as the vivid bar), the entry-price yellow dot stays, and the end labels keep their standing form. The gray signals "plan visible, nothing in force": no floating figure is computed and the `PriceTriplet` still lists only the 触发价 — the stop/target NUMBERS stay hidden until the leg actually triggers, when the bar returns to the vivid single-hue treatment and the full triplet/floating figures come back unchanged. The reached-entry note (「现价已到触发价，是否触发等复盘判定」 / the unbroken-乙路 variant) still takes the bar's place when price has already arrived at the entry, exactly as before.
- The collapsible list note's drawing sentence was updated to match （到了未触发出灰条，条件未满足不出）.

## 未触发止盈止损放回原位 (2026-11-15, user request — narrows the hiding rule above)
- User: 「未触发的止盈止损也放回原位，就乙的那个条件没满足就不要写」 — the stop/target numbers come back for untriggered legs; only an 乙路 leg whose breakout precondition is still unmet keeps them hidden. Client-only display change; no schema/data/settlement change, and the lifecycle state names are untouched.
- Implementation: new `levelsHidden(t, conditionMet)` predicate in App.tsx = open position leg whose `legState` is exactly `cond_unmet`. It replaces `isPreTriggerState` at the three number-drawing sites — the card face's `PriceTriplet`, the expanded history's `PriceTriplet`, and the scoreboard detail rows' 止损/止盈 spans — so 甲路 未触发 and 乙路 条件满足·未触发 legs again print the full 入场/止损/止盈 (slash-merged targets) in their original slots, while an 乙路 条件未满足 leg still collapses to the single 触发价 column.
- Unchanged by design: `isPreTriggerState` still drives the live row (state name + distance instead of a floating figure for every pre-trigger leg) and the bar rule from the previous section （条件未满足 no bar; 未触发 stages gray bar with its level lines and end labels; vivid bar + floating figures only after the trigger). The collapsible list note's drawing sentence was updated to match （没触发的单止盈止损照常写；只有乙路条件未满足才只列触发价、不出条）.

## 山寨同步 Gate 实时价 + 数据条滑动 + 第一止盈线变灰 (2026-11-15, user request)
- User: 「既然接口都是芝麻的为什么不同步到山寨上面的价格？搞一个像主流一样的价格显示效果，换到山寨的现价那里去，数据条的那个现价的动画优化一下要流畅地滑动，过了第一止盈就把那里的横线变灰色」 + follow-up constraint: 「第一止盈线变灰只限『已触发·待观察』的腿——已经进场后、现价路过第一止盈才把那条线变灰；未触发的腿现价就算越过第一止盈位，那条线也不变灰。」
- Altcoin live feed (client-only, display-only; server/schema/settlement untouched): new `useGateLivePrices` hook — ONE browser-direct Gate futures WebSocket (`wss://fx-ws.gateio.ws/v4/ws/usdt`, same host/channel as the majors' per-coin feeds) subscribed to every altcoin contract in the ledger at once, 20s `futures.ping` heartbeat, 3s auto-reconnect, REST fallback (~5s) pulling the all-contracts ticker list `GET https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/tickers` in one request and picking out the subscribed symbols. App builds `altGateLiveBySymbol` and passes it into every altcoin `SymbolCard` exactly where the majors' `gateLiveBySymbol` goes, so the altcoin 现价 slot gets the identical treatment: pushed price, up/down flash, Gate 实时/轮询/连接中/重试中 tag, and the position bar driven by the pushed price; a settled altcoin card with no open leg also gains the majors' small live 现价 line. The server-polled `getLivePrices` row remains as the pre-first-tick fallback (and still supplies the 24h % beside the price). Altcoin floating figure stays the plain unleveraged % — the feishu sizing branch in `LiveRow` is now explicitly gated on `MAJOR_SIZING[t.symbol]`, so no leverage/持仓/金额 derivation leaks onto altcoins.
- Smooth bar glide: `PositionBar`'s current-price dot and its colored segment carry a new `.bar-slide` class (theme.css): `transition: left/width 650ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1), background-color 400ms ease`, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`. With ~1 tick/s pushes the marker now glides between prices instead of teleporting; level lines and the entry dot do not move and carry no transition.
- TP1 line graying: `PositionBar` computes `tp1Passed = !watch && !pending && (long ? price ≥ take_profit : price ≤ take_profit)` — `pending` is exactly the pre-trigger gray mode, so only a leg in position （已触发·待观察） can gray, per the follow-up constraint; an untriggered leg's price crossing TP1 changes nothing. On dual-target legs the inner light-green TP1 tick turns `var(--dim)` gray; on single-target legs the end target line (which IS TP1) grays; the second target's line never grays under this rule. The under-bar labels are unchanged. The collapsible list note was updated to state all three behaviors.

## 甲乙胜率选项 (2026-11-15, user request)
- User: 「胜率里面加入一个甲乙胜率的选项」 — the 胜率口径 toggle gains a third option, 甲乙胜率 (`WinRateMode` = "pnl" | "judgment" | "paths"), next to 盈亏胜率 / 判断胜率 in both places the toggle appears (under the scoreboard and inside the win-rate detail window). Client-only; no server/schema/data change — everything is computed from the already-fetched `listTrades` rows.
- What the option shows: legs are split by the existing client `pathOf` derivation （甲路 / 乙路 from the plan column or the rationale's leading tag, so the round-7 altcoin pairs whose plan label is shared count too). New `pathFigures` helper reports each path under both calibers at once: 盈亏胜率 (finally-settled 盈/亏 via the same `pnlMark` the verdict chips use), 判断胜率 （对/错）, cumulative R, and expectancy mirroring the server's by_plan math (summed R over 对/错 trades ÷ decided count; 未触发 legs book 0, are counted in the header line, and — per the standing detail-window rule — are never listed as rows). Single calls and watch calls belong to neither path and never enter the figures.
- Surfaces: the scoreboard win-rate cell in this mode shows the two paths' 盈亏胜率 stacked （甲 X% / 乙 Y%) under the label 甲乙胜率， and the win-rate detail window renders one block per path — accent path badge, big 盈亏胜率 with its 盈/亏 bar, a second line with 判断胜率 （对/错/未触发 counts), 累计 R, 期望 and total legs, then that path's settled per-trade rows. A path with no legs reads 还没有X路的单； no dual-path legs at all reads 还没有甲乙双路的单.
- Single-number surfaces in this mode: a 甲/乙 comparison is not one rate, so coin-card headers, the altcoin ranking badge and the 总判断 window's per-symbol rows keep showing the coin's 盈亏胜率 (the standing default caliber) under its own 盈亏胜率 label — `coinFigures` / `RateText` treat "paths" as the P/L branch and the three label sites key off `mode === "judgment"` instead. No number is ever shown under a label it doesn't match. The collapsible list note gained a closing sentence stating exactly this.

## 已触发·待观察腿补回数据条 (2026-11-16, user report)
- User: 「已触发为什么没有数据条」 — after the round-7 repair restored IDOL 甲 (id 81) and AKE 乙 (id 80) to interim form, both legs read 已触发·待观察 and the card headers counted them as 进行中， yet neither card face drew the data bar (position bar). Root cause: the face split keyed on the raw status column — only `status === "open"` legs render through `OpenLegBlock` (live row + `PositionBar`); an interim-reviewed leg is `status === "reviewed"`, so it fell into `faceSettled` / `SettledFaceBlock`, which draws no live row and no bar (and even printed the interim snapshot under a 最终盈亏 label).
- Fix (client-only, `SymbolCard` in App.tsx): the face's running-leg list now includes `isStillRunning` legs alongside open ones — the still-running leg itself is spared by the voided-sibling filter (its batch is in `triggeredBatchKeys` precisely because IT triggered; open legs are never triggered, so the exemption changes nothing for them). A still-running leg therefore renders through `OpenLegBlock` exactly like an in-position open leg: full 入场/止损/止盈 triplet, live Gate price, floating figure and the vivid data bar (TP1-line graying included, since `legState` reads observing and `isPreTriggerState` is false). `OpenLegBlock`'s instant label now keys 开仓/判断 off the observing state so a triggered conditional leg no longer reads 判断 next to 已持有. Header counts, StatusChip, settlement caliber and stored rows untouched.

## 空间自带复盘下线 + 第 7 轮两腿修复 (2026-11-16, user order)
- User: 「去掉空间自带的复盘」 — the artifact's own review system is removed for good. Root cause it had to go: two settlement writers on the same rows with different calibers. The built-in review force-settled any trade held ≥3.5h by a simple rule (first take-profit touched = settle at TP1; neither level touched = settle at current price), knowing nothing of TP2-booking or the ledger's next-day 21:10 expiry — it prematurely closed round 6 rows (later rewritten by the ledger; see the notes on ids 61/63) and round 7's IDOL 甲 / AKE 乙 on 2026-11-15.
- Removed: the server `requestReview` action (the 3.5h due rule + the spawned review agent task); the client 「复盘到期单（N 笔）」 header button; the per-leg 「立即复盘这笔」 button on due open legs; the client's 3.5h `isDue` concept everywhere it leaked into display — watch chips and position chips no longer highlight 「已到期 · 待复盘」 (an open leg's chip is its lifecycle state name only), history rows show plain 已过去 for open watch legs, and the 待复盘 detail window drops its 「已到期（满 3.5 小时）」 count box and the sub-line about immediate review. The review-only notice banner went with them.
- Kept, deliberately: `saveReview` is now the ONLY review write path and belongs to the external ledger/settlement flow; all review display （判定 / 实际走势 / 思路复盘 / R / 胜率） is unchanged and renders whatever the ledger writes. No space-owned cron ever called the built-in review (space.json managedCronJobs was already empty; the ledger's checks are goal-owned agent crons outside the artifact).
- Ledger repair (same user order, 台账为准）: AKE 乙 id 80 and IDOL 甲 id 81 were restored to the ledger's interim form — verdict stays correct, first_touch back to `none` (id 81's TP1 touch stays recorded in its outcome note as booked-only, per its terms), and each outcome note is marked 「仍在进行中，未终局结算」 — so both legs read 已触发·待观察， carry only their interim floating R (+0.25R / +1.16R net per their notes), and stay out of the 盈亏胜率 until the real settlement. Done via the one-time `repairInterimLegsR7` action (declared as this exit's post-submit action), guarded by each row's exact wrongly-written snapshot (verdict + first_touch + review_price 0.01121 / 0.019389) so a row the ledger already repaired or finally settled is never touched; verified on the sandbox copy first (fixed=2, second run fixed=0, siblings untouched).
- Round-7 sweep: HYPE 71/72, INIT 73/74, UAI 75/76, ELSA 83/84 were checked — all still open with no review fields, nothing wrongly closed. MAGMA 77 (final stop-loss) / 78 and AKE 甲 79 / IDOL 乙 82 (no_trigger sibling voids) are legitimate ledger states and were not touched.

## 中途记录判定修正：备注标记为准，first_touch 不作终局依据 (2026-11-16, user report「AKE 的数据条没有了」)
- Root cause: the ledger's half-hour settlement check wrote AKE 乙 id 80's TP1 booking as a new interim record — status reviewed, verdict correct, first_touch `tp` (02:45 candle genuinely touched TP1 0.01220), outcome note stating 只记账、不终局 and carrying the 「仍在进行中，未终局结算」 marker, position running on to TP2 0.01300 / stop 0.01020 / expiry. The page's `isStillRunning` only recognised the OLD interim encoding (first_touch `none` + marker — the form the one-time `repairInterimLegsR7` had restored earlier that day), so id 80 read as finally settled: it left the card face's running legs, and its live row + data bar vanished. IDOL 甲 id 81 (same TP1 booking, encoded first_touch `none`) was unaffected — the two encodings of the same ledger fact diverged on the page.
- Corrected convention (supersedes the "first_touch tp/sl = finally settled" clauses in the 2026-11-13/14 sections above): the outcome note's 「仍在进行中」 marker is the ONLY finality signal. A leg whose note carries the marker is an interim, still-running position WHATEVER first_touch says (none or tp); a reviewed correct/wrong leg whose note no longer carries it is finally settled. The ledger flow's data is NOT rewritten — its TP1-booked interim encoding (first_touch `tp` + marker) is legitimate and will recur whenever a first target is booked mid-flight.
- Changes: client `isStillRunning` drops the `first_touch === "none"` condition (id 80 now reads 已触发·待观察 and renders through the open-leg block: live price row, floating figure, vivid data bar, TP1 vertical line graying by live price as usual); client `pnlMark` and server `pnlOutcome` drop the `first_touch tp/sl ⇒ settled` shortcut, so a TP1-booked interim leg earns no 盈/亏 mark and stays out of the 盈亏胜率 until its real settlement — matching id 81's treatment and the ledger caliber （第一止盈只是记账、不终局）. 累计 R caliber unchanged (interim snapshot R still included, as id 81's always was). `repairInterimLegsR7` is superseded by this fix; its exact-snapshot guards no longer match the re-written rows, so it remains a harmless no-op.

## 第一止盈已记账状态名 + TP1 线中深灰 (2026-11-16, user request)
- User: 「触发第一止盈的就标已触发第一止盈.待观察 +nR，全触发就直接结算」+「那个第一止盈的灰色不明显」.
- State name (client-only, `StatusChip` + new `tp1Booked` / `tp1BookedR` helpers in App.tsx): a still-running leg （仍在进行中 marker — that finality test is unchanged) whose TP1 has been touched and booked wears 「已触发第一止盈·待观察 +nR」 in the same chip position as 已触发·待观察 (card face + expanded record header, both render through `StatusChip`). Touch detection accepts either ledger encoding: `first_touch === "tp"` (AKE 乙 id 80), or the outcome note recording the arrival （第一止盈到达 / 首触第一止盈 / 第一止盈已到 / 第一止盈已触 — IDOL 甲 id 81, whose first_touch stays `none`). +nR is the gross R banked at TP1 from stored prices, |TP1 − entry| ÷ |entry − stop| at 2 decimals — both live samples read +1.50R (id 80: 0.0012/0.0008; id 81: 0.0018/0.0012). Legs still running short of TP1 keep 已触发·待观察 + interim floating R; TP2-full-trigger legs are finally settled by the ledger side and read 已结算 — no space-side change. Statistics calibers untouched.
- TP1 line gray: the passed-TP1 vertical line in `PositionBar` (inner tick on dual-target legs, end line on single-target legs) now uses a new theme token `--tp-passed` — a medium-dark neutral gray (#4e5862 light / #5d6873 dark) chosen to read at a glance against the light-green TP1 line and the deep-green TP2 line; the old `--dim` gray was too pale. Position and line form unchanged; unpassed TP1 stays light green, TP2 never grays. The collapsible explainer text was updated to match （已触发第一止盈·待观察 state name + 中深灰 wording).

## 结算卡触线标注 + 算法一重算 (2026-11-16, user request)
- User (with a screenshot circling the empty right side of a settled card's 最终盈亏/现价 rows): in that spot, separately show which lines a settled position touched — only the stop → 「已触止损」; first take-profit touched (booked) then price fell back to the stop → 「第一止盈 +n%」「已触止损 −n%」 with the final P/L recomputed; both take-profits touched → 「已盈利」. The user then chose 算法一 for the recompute (75% of the position exits at TP1, 25% rides to the stop: final % = 0.75 × TP1% + 0.25 × SL%; per-leg R = 0.75 × TP1-R − 0.25) and explicitly deferred 累计 R ("累计R那里等会我单独修改").
- Scope honored: display/stat caliber only — stored rows and the ledger's net R untouched; 累计 R everywhere (scoreboard total, card-header cumulative, detail-window sums, server `realizedR`/`total_r`) keeps the old caliber. What follows the new caliber: the card-face 最终盈亏 %, the per-leg R in the status chip, the 盈/亏 mark, and the 盈亏胜率 (client surfaces via `finalPnlPct`/`pnlMark`; the scoreboard's via the server's `pnlOutcome`, which mirrors the TP1-then-stop test and the 算法一 split for that case only). A leg that touched only TP1 then expired, and a leg that touched neither line, keep the standing display.
- Detection (from the ledger's own final record; verified against all production rows in the sandbox copy): on a final record `first_touch` names the EXIT level, so a TP1-then-stop leg reads `first_touch="sl"` (AKE 乙 id 80). TP1's earlier arrival is read from the outcome note by `noteLevelState` (the level's own clause, earliest keyword decides — HYPE 乙 id 56's TP2 clause carries both 首触 and a 未到 about an earlier candle and must read hit), corroborated by the window extreme passing TP1; the window alone cannot decide because it starts at placement and can cross TP1 pre-entry (MAGMA 甲 id 77: window high 0.30342 > TP1 0.3000 but the note says 未到 → sl_only). - Verified classifications over the live ledger: tp1_then_sl = AKE 80 (+6.36%, was −7.27%), MAGMA 61 (+2.18%), INIT 58 (+1.67%), INIT 44 (+2.78%) — all four flip to 盈 under 算法一 (INIT 58 is the discriminating case: 算法二 would have kept it a loss); sl_only = MAGMA 77, HYPE 72/42/9, UAI 46, INIT 12; both_tp = ELSA 66, HYPE 56; TP1-only-expiry legs (IDOL 64, MAGMA 48, BTC 5) and neither-line legs (ELSA 54, UAI 13, ETH 6) stay unannotated.

## 状态时间口径 + 未触发不写结算 + 共做了只数开过仓的单 (2026-11-16, user request)
- User: 「那甲路就没了就不用写结算了，还有，下面那个开仓时间怎么都是一样的？开仓时间指的是这个单子已经过了条件然后触发了的那个时候才是开仓时间，未触发但是满足条件的单子就写挂单中，条件未满足的就写观望中，对应状态的时间在状态更改的时候也改一下」 + follow-up: 「共做了几笔是历史做过单子的几笔，未触发的不记入」.
- Root cause of the identical times: every leg of a round shares `opened_at` (the recording instant), and the card time line printed it as 开仓 for every state. No trigger/state-change timestamp was stored or derived anywhere.
- Server (`getConditionStates` extended, same action name and poll): the existing Gate futures 15m candle scan (per symbol, since placement, 2000-candle cap) now also returns, per leg — `condition_met_at` for open 乙路 legs: the close time of the first candle that closed beyond the leg's breakout line (the moment the leg entered 挂单中）; and `triggered_at` for reviewed position legs the review confirmed triggered (verdict correct/wrong) that ran a trigger-pending plan (conditional or 甲/乙）: the leg's real 开仓 time by the settlement caliber — for 乙路， the close of the first post-break candle that closed inside the retest band between the breakout line and the entry price （收在回踩带内确认入场； a literal touch test fails these legs because the booked entry is the band's edge, which price may never exactly reach — ELSA 乙 id 84: entry 0.0600 vs post-placement high 0.05959, triggered on an in-band close); for any other leg, the start of the first 15m candle that touched the entry price after placement and before the confirming review, clamped to not precede placement. no_trigger legs get no entry: they never opened, there is no 开仓 time. Unparseable lines / failed candle fetches yield nulls — nothing invented; the client falls back to the placement time.
- Client time lines (card face open legs, settled face block, expanded history — one caliber): the line follows the leg's state and its timestamp is when the leg entered that state. 观望中 （乙路 pre-break): 置单 time + 已过去； 挂单中 (condition met, untriggered — 乙路 post-break since the break close, 甲路 since placement): state-start time + 已挂单； 开仓 (in position / triggered): the real trigger time + 已持有 measured from it; finally settled: 开仓 time + 持有 (trigger → review) 后结算. Watch legs keep 判断 wording. The old 「是否触发由复盘按 K 线判定 · 已过去」 face line is replaced by this state line (the history detail still states the review caliber).
- 未触发不写结算： `StatusChip` no longer prefixes 已结算 on no_trigger legs — the chip reads just 「未触发」 (card face, history and scoreboard detail rows share the chip). The settled face block's line for such a leg reads 「置单 · 未触发」 with no 开仓/后结算 wording (its 最终盈亏 row already said 未触发·没有开仓，按 0% 记）. Triggered legs keep 已结算 chips and 后结算 wording.
- 共做了口径 (follow-up): the header's M now counts only legs that actually opened a position — review-confirmed triggered legs (settled or still running) plus open legs already in position (immediate entries). Untriggered legs (settled no_trigger or still waiting) and watch calls do not count. ELSA/IDOL/AKE round-7 cards therefore read 共做了 1 笔 (one leg of each pair fired). The collapsible rules note was updated with both calibers. No stored data, settlement flow, or stats change.

- User: 「把开仓时间写进空间存住」 — the real 开仓 time (triggered_at) was only derived on read by `getConditionStates`' Gate 15m candle scan and never stored; now it persists on the trade row.
- Schema: new nullable `triggered_at` column on `paper_trades` (migration 0007, integer epoch ms like opened_at). Null = never triggered, immediate entry (opened_at IS the open), or not yet computed.
- Server (`getConditionStates`, same action/poll): a review-confirmed triggered leg with a stored `triggered_at` reads it back verbatim and skips the candle scan; when the scan computes a value for a leg whose stored value is still null, the handler writes it back in the same call (conditional update guarded by `isNull(triggered_at)` — a stored value is never overwritten; a failed write is swallowed so the read still returns, and the next poll retries). The computed caliber is unchanged （乙路 in-band close / other legs first touch). `listTrades` now also returns `triggered_at` per trade.
- Client: the three 开仓 fallback chains read `lifecycle?.triggeredAt ?? t.triggered_at ?? t.opened_at`, so the stored value carries the card even when a poll has no candle evidence. Display, state names, settlement flow and stats otherwise unchanged. Note the earlier 「已结算卡现价行」 sections' layout history was further superseded the same day by the grid layout in `SettledFaceBlock` (price line spans both columns in its own row) — see the in-code layout note.

## 单个币累计 R 新口径 + 已结算徽章去单笔 R (2026-11-16, user request)
- User: 「单个币的累计R那里的意思是它之前做过所有单的止盈加起来，有止损就去减掉，全盈利就直接全加，有第一止盈又到止损的就直接只算加止盈的就行了」 + confirmation 「整笔按第一止盈算，AKE累计变+1.50R」 + follow-up 「盈旁边那个+0.88r那个显示没意义了删掉」.
- Single-coin caliber (new): a coin's cumulative R sums its settled trades as — fully profitable leg: full R added; stop-only leg: its stop R subtracted (−1R); TP1-then-stop leg: the WHOLE trade counts at its first-take-profit R (tp1BookedR), the stop is not subtracted. AKE has one settled leg (乙 id 80, TP1 R +1.50), so its cumulative goes −1.00R → +1.50R. All other legs (expiry/floating exits, no_trigger = 0, watch excluded) book exactly as before.
- Surfaces changed: client `coinFigures` now sums the new `coinSettledR` (was `settledR`) — this feeds the card-header 累计 and the altcoin R ranking / overall figures; server `getStats` gains `coinRealizedR` (mirror) feeding ONLY the `by_symbol` buckets' total_r, so the 总判断 window's 分币种 rows agree with the card headers.
- Surfaces deliberately unchanged: the scoreboard's TOP 累计 R (server overall total_r + the client's `settledR` detail-window sums), the per-plan buckets and expectancy, the 甲乙 path figures, the card-face 最终盈亏 % （算法一 75/25 split), the 盈/亏 mark and every win rate — TP1-then-stop still books −1R in the top total and +6.36% / 盈 on the card face.
- Settled chip R removed: `StatusChip`'s settled branch no longer prints any per-leg R after the 盈/亏 mark (the AKE +0.88R was the 算法一 split — 0.75×1.50−0.25 — which matches neither cumulative caliber, hence 「没意义」). The chip now reads only 「已结算 · 判断对/错 · 盈/亏」 for every settled leg. Running legs keep their booked/floating R （已触发第一止盈·待观察 +1.50R etc.); DetailTradeRow's separate R figure in the 总判断/胜率 windows is untouched. The now-unused client `algo1R` helper is deleted （算法一 survives only as `algo1PnlPct` for the % and the server's pnlOutcome split for the win rate). The collapsible rules note's caliber sentences were updated to match. No stored data, schema or settlement-flow change.

## 胜率新口径 + 甲乙胜率拆两个选项 (2026-11-16, user request)
- User: 「跟之前那个最终盈亏不一样，我们这个只影响胜率里面的内容，只要摸到第一止盈就算盈了，直接算第一止盈的r，其他的就一样，亏就减掉」 + 「对，里面的单的数据都按照这样子修改，还有甲乙胜率分开各一个选项」.
- Win-rate caliber (new, win-rate content ONLY): a finally settled position leg that TOUCHED its first take-profit at any point counts as a WIN and books its WHOLE first-take-profit R inside win-rate figures; a leg that never touched TP1 scores exactly as before (win if its final result is positive, loss subtracts). Three touch shapes: exit at a target (first_touch "tp"), TP1-then-stop (tp1ThenSl), and TP1-then-expiry/floating exit (note records TP1's arrival + window extreme passed TP1 — IDOL round-6 乙 id 64 flips from loss to win this way). New helpers: client `tp1Touched` / `winRateMark` / `winRateR`, server mirrors `tp1Touched` / `pnlOutcome` (rewritten) / `winRateR`.
- Surfaces changed: scoreboard 盈亏胜率 (server pnl_win_rate), card-header 盈亏胜率 + altcoin win-rate ranking (client `coinFigures` via winRateMark), the win-rate detail window's win/loss lists, per-plan rows (server by_plan pnl counts AND total_r/decidedR/expectancy now winRateR-based), and the 甲/乙 path figures (client `pathFigures`: winRateMark + winRateR).
- 甲/乙 split: `WinRateMode` "paths" is replaced by "jia" （甲胜率） and "yi" （乙胜率） — the toggle now has four options （盈亏胜率 / 判断胜率 / 甲胜率 / 乙胜率）; the scoreboard cell in jia/yi mode shows that one path's rate as a single big number labelled 甲胜率/乙胜率； the win-rate window renders only the selected path's section. Card headers and the 总判断 per-symbol rows keep the 盈亏胜率 caliber under its own label in jia/yi modes.
- Deliberately unchanged in this round: the card-face 最终盈亏 % and 盈/亏 mark （算法一 via finalPnlPct/pnlMark), 判断胜率 everywhere, the top 累计 R and per-coin cumulative (both still realizedR/coinRealizedR at this point — synced by the follow-up below), stored data, schema and settlement flow. Window subtitles and the collapsible rules note state the new caliber.

## 第 5 轮盘中复扫 ELSA 一对补回甲/乙路标签 (2026-11-16, user question — one-time data repair)
- User, screenshotting the 累计 R detail: 「这里有一个怎么没显示那条路？」 — the ELSA row （第5轮山寨纸面·盘中复扫， 入场 0.0658, +1.30R) carried no 甲路/乙路 badge while every neighbouring dual-path row did.
- Root cause (recording omission, not a display bug): the 2026-11-14 09:40 intraday-rescan batch wrote its two ELSA legs (ids 53/54) with the shared batch label 第5轮山寨纸面·盘中复扫 in BOTH the plan column and the rationale, with no per-leg path tag. Every other dual-path batch writes the path into the plan column （甲路·反弹空 / 乙路·破位回踩空） or the rationale's leading tag （第N轮山寨纸面·甲空/·乙多：…), which is all `pathOf` reads — so this pair alone resolved to no path, showed no badge anywhere, and was also excluded from the 甲/乙 per-path win-rate figures. A full-ledger scan confirms ids 53/54 are the ONLY pathless non-watch rows.
- Ground truth: the paper ledger (~/workspace/paper-trading/records.md, 第5轮山寨纸面·盘中复扫 entry) records the pair explicitly as 「ELSA 空（空间 id 53/54）：甲 入场 0.0677，止损 0.0690…；乙 触发 0.0654 → 入场 0.0658，止损 0.0670…」, and each leg's own stored reasoning review opens by naming its own path (id 53: 「甲路反弹空全程未到区…」; id 54: 「乙路破位回踩确认有效…」). A client-side text heuristic was rejected: three 乙路 no_trigger legs elsewhere (ids 36/62/82) have reviews that OPEN with the sibling 甲路's trigger, so review text cannot be parsed generically — the repair writes the ledger's fact into the plan column instead.
- Fix: one-time `repairRescanPathLabelsR5` action (declared as the exit's post-submit action), guarded by each row's exact recorded snapshot (symbol ELSA / plan 第5轮山寨纸面·盘中复扫 / direction short / entry 0.0677 + stop 0.069 for id 53, entry 0.0658 + stop 0.067 for id 54): id 53 → plan 甲路·反弹空， id 54 → plan 乙路·破位回踩空 — the same labels the round-6 ELSA pair carries. Only the plan column changes; prices, verdicts, reviews and the rationale (which keeps the 盘中复扫 batch label) are untouched. After the repair the pair renders its badges everywhere and id 54 joins the 乙路 win-rate figures as the ledger intends. Safe to re-run: repaired rows no longer match the guard.

## 第 8 轮甲/乙标签新写法导致徽章消失 (2026-11-16, user report — parser fix)
- User: 「空间的甲乙去哪里了？怎么路的不显示了」 — every round-8 card (ids 85–98, recorded 2026-11-16 21:16 CST) showed no 甲路/乙路 badge.
- Root cause (recording drift, parser too narrow): the round-8 batch writes its rationale leading tag WITH the 路 — 「第8轮山寨纸面·甲路空：… / ·乙路多：…」 — while rounds ≤7 write 「·甲空 / ·乙多」. Both `pathOf` derivations (client App.tsx, server `pathOfRow` in actions.ts) matched only `/·(甲|乙)[空多]/`, so all 14 round-8 legs resolved to no path: no PathBadge anywhere, excluded from the 甲/乙 per-path win-rate figures, and — server side — `getConditionStates` skipped the round-8 乙路 legs entirely, so their breakout precondition （条件未满足 → 条件满足·未触发） could never be computed. The breakout-line parse itself was unaffected (round 8 still writes 收盘破位/收盘站上 + number).
- Fix: both regexes widened to `/·(甲|乙)(?:路)?[空多]/`, accepting both recorded spellings. No data change, no caliber change; older rounds parse exactly as before.
```
