```markdown
## 第二批修复：前端同源 + 真实出场价字段 (2026-11-17, fix batch 2 — items 6–14 of the audit list)
- 【6】P1-01 前端同源： client `breakoutLineOf` rewritten byte-for-byte as the server's `breakoutLineOfRow` — cut the rationale at the first 作废， strip thousands commas repeatedly, pattern priority 触发 → 收盘破位 → 收盘跌破 → 收盘站上， null on no match. Verified against the production rationales: id 5→71850, 7→1985, 56→67, 66→0.0635, 44→0.1060, 46→0.0378, 64→0.0178 (all pass).
- 【7】P1-02 真实出场价： migration 0008 adds `exit_price` REAL, `exit_reason` TEXT, `triggered_at_unverified` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 to paper_trades; schema + tradeShape/toTrade carry all three. `saveReview` accepts optional `exit_price` (positive) / `exit_reason` (tp1|tp2|sl|expiry|manual) and writes each ONLY when provided (omitted = stored value kept — verified in the sandbox: write with fields, re-save without, values survive). Client: `exitPriceOf` prefers a stored exit_price; `finalPnlPct` order unchanged (tp1_then_sl still 算法一 first); HistoryLegDetail's settleNote reads 「按出场价 X 结算（原因标签）」 when exit_price exists （算法一 caption still leads for tp1_then_sl, matching the figure). `getConditionStates` excludes triggered_at_unverified rows from derivation entirely.
- 【8】P2-04: client gains the server's interim test (`isInterim`: note contains 仍在进行中 OR 未终局）; `isStillRunning` and `pnlMark`'s settled test read through it; `settledR` returns null for interim rows (server winRateR/coinRealizedR parity; running legs' floating display still uses tradeR).
- 【9】P2-07: `winRateMark` rewritten to the server's pnlOutcome gate order — not reviewed / watch / verdict not 对·错 / interim → null; then TP1 touched → 盈 (even at final P/L exactly 0); then finalPnlPct sign, 0/null → null.
- 【10】P2-08: `tp1Booked`'s note test is now the shared `noteLevelState(note, "第一止盈") === "hit"` clause scan (first_touch "tp" and the isStillRunning gate kept). 「第一止盈 0.0580 已于 12:40 到达（已记账）」→ hit; 「第一止盈 0.0580 未到」→ miss.
- 【11】P2-09 报价过期 (60s): both Gate hooks track per-contract `updatedAt` (WS tick or successful REST poll refreshes it; disconnect/failure keeps the old price and lets it age). The polled price carries the getLivePrices query's dataUpdatedAt into LiveRow as `LivePriceWithTs.updatedAt`. LiveRow picks fresh-direct → fresh-polled → any price (direct first) marked stale; a stale price still displays with a 「行情过期」 marker but the floating zone shows 「行情过期」 instead of any computed figure. GatePriceLine marks stale the same way. Fresh behavior is byte-identical to before.
- 【12】P2-12: `majorLeverageSafe` — undefined for non-majors / watch / missing entry-stop; true when any candidate tier's estimated liquidation sits beyond the stop; false when none does. LiveRow's majors branch appends 「无安全杠杆档」 in the warn color when false; majorLeverage's fallback numbers and all derived figures are unchanged (display-only).
- 【13】P2-05: `listTrades` limit cap 200 → 10000 server-side (default 100 unchanged); the client's trades query now asks for limit 10000 so totals / per-coin / 甲乙 figures and the detail lists share one full data set.
- 【14】开仓时间待核实： when `triggered_at_unverified` is true, the card-face time lines (OpenLegBlock / SettledFaceBlock) and HistoryLegDetail's header ignore stored triggered_at and lifecycle values and read 「置单 {opened_at} · 开仓时间待核实」; false renders exactly as before. No homepage layout, message position, modal size, or settled caliber （算法一 / TP1-touch win rate / 甲乙 split) was touched.

## 第三批修复：批次原子性/幂等 + 历史数据修复通道 (2026-11-17, fix batch 3 — items 15–17 of the audit list)
- P2-06 recordBatch 原子性＋幂等： migration 0009 creates `record_batches` (batch_key TEXT PK, inserted_ids TEXT = JSON array of inserted paper_trades ids, created_at INTEGER epoch ms); schema gains `recordBatches`. `recordBatch` accepts an optional `batch_key`: the existing whole-batch pre-validation runs first and unchanged (any failure writes nothing and does NOT burn the key — verified in the sandbox: a rejected batch's key stayed usable for the corrected retry); then a stored key replays its original ids with `replayed: true` and inserts nothing (verified: two keyed calls returned the same id, the next unkeyed insert took the following id). Atomicity: ctx.db exposes no transaction API （平台事务不支持/未验证）, so the inserts go out as ONE `db.batch` — every trade insert plus a placeholder key row in a single atomic statement batch — followed by an UPDATE filling the key row's inserted_ids (the ids only exist once the inserts land) and a hard check that the inserted count equals the submitted count, throwing otherwise. The response keeps `ids` and adds `replayed: boolean` (false for a fresh insert, and for every call without a key — unkeyed behavior is otherwise exactly as before).
- repairTriggeredAtV104 (one-time, guarded, re-runnable; lands on durable via the combined runner in the hardening note below): id 64 triggered_at 2026-11-14T13:45:00Z → 2026-11-14T18:00:00Z (ledger: 01:15 candle closed below the trigger line, 01:45 candle closed 0.018001 back inside the retest band — confirmation is that candle's close, 02:00 CST); id 5 triggered_at 2026-11-12T14:25:00Z → 2026-11-12T16:15:00Z (ledger: the 00:00 15m candle closed 71,966.8 inside the 71,850–72,000 retest band, confirmed at its close 00:15 CST); id 95 triggered_at 2026-11-16T13:16:00Z (= its placement instant, false precision — the real touch is only known to fall in the 21:25–21:30 window) → triggered_at null + triggered_at_unverified true, so the row renders 置单 time + 开仓时间待核实. Each row is guarded by its exact current stored value (id 95 also requires unverified=false) in both the read check and the UPDATE's WHERE; id 85 is deliberately not handled and no other row is touched. Sandbox verification: first run updated all three with the exact before/after values; second run skipped all three.
- backfillExitPricesV104 (one-time, guarded, re-runnable; lands on durable via the combined runner in the hardening note below): writes ONLY exit_price/exit_reason for twelve pre-0008 settled legs, each value the ledger/settlement-note actual exit and exactly the row's stored review_price — 5 → 70921.7 expiry, 32 → 70503.77 tp1, 34 → 1946.48 tp1, 35 → 68848.34 tp1, 37 → 1932.539375 tp1, 39 → 79.81 tp1, 48 → 0.31437 expiry, 56 → 65.2 tp2, 64 → 0.01936 expiry, 66 → 0.0605 tp2, 81 → 0.018495 expiry, 84 → 0.05721 expiry. Guard per row: exit_price still null AND review_price equals the target AND first_touch equals the expected snapshot — first_touch is "tp" for all twelve (verified against the live rows before shipping: every one of these legs touched its first take-profit first; the expiry rows then ran past it to their expiry market-price settlement, e.g. id 84's note records 第一止盈已到达（已记账）before the 21:10 settlement). Sandbox verification: across two runs all twelve rows landed with the specified values, already-written rows skip cleanly on re-run. No homepage layout, message position, modal size, or settled caliber was touched in this batch.
- Post-audit hardening (same batch, after the first audit): the submit pipeline runs exactly ONE post-submit action, so the two V104 repairs are also exposed through a combined `applyV104DataRepairs` action that runs both implementations back to back — that combined action is the one declared as this exit's post-submit action, guaranteeing both repairs (16 and 17) land on the durable ledger in a single invocation (both underlying actions remain independently callable and guarded). Separately, recordBatch's replay path now refuses an incomplete key record: a stored key whose parsed id count differs from the submitted batch size (e.g. the "[]" placeholder of a batch whose ids backfill never landed) throws instead of returning a false empty success.

## GPT 补充包第二批：服务端统计与取价 (2026-11-17, fix batch — P2-21 / P2-19 / P2-18 / P2-05)
- P2-21 getLivePrices 取价集合扩围： symbol 集合由「仅 status=open」改为并集——open 行 ∪ reviewed 且 isInterim（outcome_note 含 仍在进行中/未终局）且 verdict 为 correct/wrong 的在场持仓行。已终局 reviewed 与 no_trigger 行不取。修复前线上基线只有 AKE/ELSA/INIT/MAGMA 四币；在场 interim 的 #85 HYPE、#95 IDOL 缺席，扩围后应一并返回。
- P2-19 by_plan 拆甲乙： getStats 分桶键——plan 本身含「甲路/乙路」字样按原样；不含路径标记但 pathOfRow 判出明确路径的行，桶名 = plan + "·甲路"/"·乙路"（如「第7轮山寨纸面·甲路」）；无路径行（观望等）按原样。桶内全部统计字段口径不变，只是分组变细；存储的 plan 列不改写。基线对照：第7轮合桶 total 14、pnl_win 3、pnl_loss 2、total_r +2.11，拆后两桶之和须与之自洽（甲 pnl_win_rate 0.5、乙 0.6667）。
- P2-18 观望 expectation_r 改 null： getStats 每桶新增 R 贡献行计数（winRateR 非 null 的行数，与 total_r 现行排除逻辑一致——interim 与观望行均不贡献）；贡献数为 0 时 expectation_r 返回 null，>0 时公式与数值不变。客户端两处 by_plan 期望显示：null 时显示「期望 —」，不再出现假的 0.00R。基线：观望桶 total 16、win_rate 0.6 保持，expectation_r 由 0 改 null；其余桶逐项不变（乙路·破位回踩空 1.05、甲路·反弹空 0.84、甲路·反弹多 null）。
- P2-05 消除 10000 硬上限：选型＝服务端游标分页＋前端完整加载（前端分组统计依赖全量行，服务端全量分组改造面更大；游标分页后前端仍拿全量、同源一致，且单次响应有界）。服务端 listTrades：新增可选 cursor（上一页最后一行 id）；稳定序保持现行 opened_at desc + id desc，页边界按 (opened_at, id) 复合序表达，分页间不重不漏；单页 limit 上限与缺省均为 500；响应新增 next_cursor（有下一页为本页最后一行 id，否则 null）；不带参数的旧式调用返回第一页。未知 cursor id 返回空的末页（不从头重来，避免走页调用方重复计行）。客户端：trades 查询循环按 next_cursor 拉完所有页合并为 all；后续分组/明细计算不变；后续页拉取失败时保留已加载页并置 incomplete，页面在卡片列表上方显示「数据加载不完整」提示＋重新加载按钮，第一页失败仍走原 读取失败 状态。代码内不再有 10000 硬上限假设。

## 卡面排版按状态各归其位：已结算第二行恢复水平 + 作废卡套 UAI 模板 (2026-11-17 03:09, user correction — client only)
- 硬规则（RGB 原话）：「每种状态都有它该有的排版」——已结算/作废/未触发/进行中/观望各用各的模板，互不套用；本次只动下面两处，其余状态逐字不动。
- 已结算卡（SettledFaceBlock 中 verdict=correct/wrong 分支）：第二行恢复 UAI #89 模板的水平并列——一行 flex、两端对齐，左「最终盈亏 ±x.xx%」、右 TouchNote 触线标注框。v114 把该行拆进两列 grid（盈亏左列、标注框右列跨行）导致标注框读起来掉到盈亏下方，本次改回整行 flex（gridColumn 1 / -1）。第一行不变：时间＋方向徽章在左、「已结算 · 判断对/错 · 盈/亏」徽章在右，无甲/乙徽章（计划徽章仍随 hidePlan，与 v114 一致）。
- 作废卡（isVoided 腿，如 ELSA #97/#98）：改套同一个 UAI 已结算模板只换词——第一行置单时间＋「置单」（去掉原「· 已作废」后缀）＋方向徽章，右侧黄色「已作废」徽章（StatusChip 现行黄色）；去掉甲/乙徽章与计划徽章（原走徽章行排版）；第二行在最终盈亏的同一行位写「作废 · 没有开仓」，不挂盈亏数字、无触线标注框。现价行仍由 SymbolCard 独立成行，不变。
- 明确未动：普通未触发卡（no_trigger 且非作废，仍走徽章行＋时间行＋「未触发 · 没有开仓」的 grid 排版）、OpenLegBlock 进行中卡、观望卡、HistoryLegDetail、DetailTradeRow、isVoided 派生定义、全部统计函数与服务端。getStats 基线对照：total=90、total_r +19.39、pnl_win 19、pnl_loss 7（改前审计捕获头部 90 / +19.39 已一致；本次纯客户端渲染改动不经统计路径）。

## GPT 合并包（v110 起点 11 项）核查后修复批 (2026-11-17, RGB 拍板「直接修」— 基线 total=90 / total_r +18.39 / pnl_win 19 / pnl_loss 8，#85 已终局结算后的现行值)
- P2-24 opened_at 强制时区：addTrade / recordBatch 新增 hasExplicitTimezone 校验——opened_at 存在时必须以 Z/z 或 ±HH:MM / ±HHMM 结尾，否则按现行中文风格拒绝（单条「未记录」/ 批量「整批未记录」并提示改用带时区 ISO 时间）；不传 opened_at 语义不变。沙箱实测：无时区串被拒、零写入。

- P2-26 repairOpenedAtBatch 身份守卫：两条 UPDATE 的 WHERE 在时间戳等值之外分别加 inArray(id, [4..8]) 与 inArray(id, [9..29])，action 描述同步注明身份限定；线上现无行带两个错误时间戳，沙箱重跑 fixed=0。
- P1-31 真值级 CAS：repairInterimLegsR7（id 80/81）、repairRescanPathLabelsR5（id 53/54）、backfillTakeProfit2、runBackfillExitPricesV104 的 UPDATE WHERE 全部补上 SELECT 读到的守卫字段精确值（可空字段按 isNull/eq 分支，与 saveReview 现行写法一致）；无竞态时行为不变，沙箱重跑四接口全部 fixed/skipped=0。
- P2-29 noteLevelState 多段时序：server 与 client 两处同构改写——遍历级别词每一次出现、逐子句按「子句内最早关键词」判 hit/miss、取最后一个有定论子句；首触{级别} 直判 hit 与千分位清洗不变。离线回归：包内复现串「第一止盈 110 未到；后来第一止盈 110 已到达」由 miss 变 hit；「若…到达则…；实际未到」由误判 hit 变 miss；其余样本新旧一致（主会话已离线验过线上 90 行零差异）。
- P2-28 bothTargetsHit 去窗口捷径（client）：both_tp 只认 noteLevelState(第二止盈)==='hit' 或 exit_reason==='tp2'，删除 window 极值分支；#56/#66 两行均 exit_reason=tp2 且备注 hit，结论不变。
- P2-30 getConditionStates K 线分页：去掉 now−2000×900 的 20.83 天下限，改为自该币最早置单起按 2000 根一窗顺序分页至 now、最多 10 窗（约 208 天），每窗保留一次重试，累积后走现行归一化；data_status 与其余判定逐字不动。沙箱实测全腿 data_status=ok、存量 triggered_at 读回不变。
- P2-27 卡头进行中口径（client）：SymbolCard runningCount 改为 openLegs.filter(!isTriggerPending) + settledLegs.filter(isStillRunning)，与 madeCount 的「未触发不算开仓」对齐；卡头文案格式逐字不变。MAGMA/AKE 的挂单腿不再计进行中，腿卡自身仍显示挂单中。
- P2-32 planOf 观望优先：server 与 client 两处同构——explicit plan 之后、rationale 标签解析之前先判 direction==='watch' 返回「观望」；沙箱 getStats by_plan 观望仍为 16，与基线一致。
- 不改两项确认：P2-06（db.batch 原子 + batch_key 先占位幂等 + 重放自愈代码在位；故障注入/并发交错在平台沙箱无隔离 DB 手段，维持「部分修复、故障场景未验证」，不写已闭环）；P2-22（0006 迁移保持原样未改未重跑，MIGRATION-NOTES.md §1 与 schema.ts 的 P2-22 注释均在位，本次未新增改动）。
- 验收：构建+类型检查通过；改后沙箱实读 getStats 与基线逐项一致（total=90、total_r=+18.39、pnl_win=19、pnl_loss=8），listTrades 90 行，#80/#81/#56/#66 字段与改前一致，全部测试零数据变动。回滚方式：restore 回 v122。
```

### MIGRATION-NOTES.md

```markdown
# 迁移安全说明（Migration Safety Notes）

本文件记录已执行迁移中一处已知的数据回填风险、生产核对结论，以及今后所有
数据回填类迁移 / 一次性修复必须遵守的强制规范。本文件放在源码根目录而非
`drizzle/` 目录内，以免干扰迁移运行器；它不属于迁移序列，修改它不改变任何
已执行的迁移历史。

## 1. 0006 的裸 ID 回填风险（P2-22）

`drizzle/0006_add_take_profit_2.sql` 在新增 `take_profit2` 列之后，用 14 条
`UPDATE paper_trades SET take_profit2 = <值> WHERE id = <71..84>` 按裸 ID
回填第 7 轮双路腿的第二止盈。

风险事实：

- 这 14 条 UPDATE 的唯一匹配条件是主键 `id`，没有 `symbol`、方向或任何
  关键价格（入场 / 止损 / 第一止盈）等交易身份守卫，也没有留下逐行审计
  记录。
- 裸 ID 只在「该环境的 id 分配与编写迁移时的环境完全一致」时才指向同一
  笔交易。在任何 id 映射不同的环境（例如从空库重放、导入顺序不同、或
  历史行有增删导致自增序列偏移的环境）重跑这条迁移，14 个值会逐一写到
  别的交易行上，且写入过程静默成功——误写不会报错，只能靠事后逐笔核对
  发现。

## 2. 生产现状：已核对正确（2026-11-17）

生产库已于 2026-11-17 逐笔核对：ids 71–84 恰为第 7 轮七对
（HYPE/INIT/UAI/MAGMA/AKE/IDOL/ELSA 各两腿），其 `take_profit2` 与迁移
SQL 的 14 个值逐一全对，无一笔误写。

因此生产存量数据是正确的，本批不改动任何一行数据；0006 的风险只存在于
「在 id 映射不同的环境重跑」这一潜伏面，靠本说明与下文规范防患，不靠
改存量消除。

## 3. 为何不修改已执行的 0006

- 已执行迁移是校验历史的一部分：迁移运行器按文件内容校验已应用的迁移，
  改动已执行迁移的 SQL 会让校验与历史对不上，轻则迁移状态报错，重则在
  其他环境触发重复执行。
- 生产数据已经核对正确（见上节），0006 在生产环境达成的结果没有错误
  需要纠正；为「修写法」去改一条结果正确的历史迁移，收益为零。
- 改存量只会引入新风险：任何对已执行迁移或已核对数据的事后改动，都
  可能在某个环境造成重复回填、校验失败或新的误写，风险高于保留原样
  加文档防护。

结论：0006 的 SQL 与迁移 journal 保持原样，一字不动。

## 4. 今后强制规范：数据回填类迁移 / 一次性修复

今后任何数据回填类迁移、一次性修复脚本或修复 action，必须同时满足：

1. **必须带交易身份校验**：匹配条件不得只用裸 ID。每行更新必须带
   `symbol` ＋关键价格条件守卫（入场价 / 止损价 / 第一止盈等能唯一确认
   这笔交易身份的字段组合），且守卫值必须写进 UPDATE 的 WHERE / 更新
   条件里（真值级 CAS），不能只在读取后于内存里比对。参照做法：
   2026-11-16 的 `backfillTakeProfit2` action——每行按
   symbol / direction / entry / stop / TP1 全快照守卫、且仅当
   `take_profit2` 仍为 null 时才写入；以及 `repairTriggeredAtV104` /
   `repairTriggeredAtV106` 的做法——守卫值（当前存储的精确值）同时写进
   读取判断与 UPDATE 的 WHERE 子句。
2. **必须可重复调用**：同一修复重复执行不得产生第二次写入；已处理
   （已达目标状态）的行必须自动跳过，重跑结果应是全部 skipped 而非
   报错或重复改动。
3. **必须返回逐行报告**：每次执行返回逐行的改前值 / 改后值与
   updated / skipped 状态，使执行结果可审计、可与台账逐笔对账；
   不允许只返回一个总数了事。

不满足以上三条的数据修复不得上线执行。
```

### client/src/main.tsx

```tsx
import { spaceQueryClient } from "@hatch/space-sdk/client";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import { App } from "./App";
import "./theme.css";

const rootEl = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-generated-space-root]");
if (!rootEl) {
  throw new Error("missing generated space root element");
}

// If you edit this file: keep `<QueryClientProvider client={spaceQueryClient}>`
// wrapping the app (every space shares the SDK's QueryClient), and keep
// BOTH the `hatch-space-root` class AND the `data-hatch-space-root`
// attribute on the outer div. The SDK styles select on
// `.hatch-space-root[data-hatch-space-root]` to apply mobile safe-area
// insets (notches, gesture bars, etc.), correct viewport sizing, and the
// hosting header/chrome offsets — dropping either breaks layout silently.
createRoot(rootEl).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <QueryClientProvider client={spaceQueryClient}>
      <div className="hatch-space-root" data-hatch-space-root>
        <App />
      </div>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

### client/src/App.tsx

```tsx
import {
useEffect,
useMemo,
useRef,
useState,
type FormEvent,
type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { SafeAreaTopScrim } from "@hatch/space-sdk/client";
import { api, type ApiResponse } from "./api";

type Trade = ApiResponse<typeof api, "listTrades">["trades"][number];
type LivePrice = ApiResponse<typeof api, "getLivePrices">["prices"][number];
// A polled price plus when the poll that produced it landed
// (P2-09): App stamps each row with the getLivePrices query's
// dataUpdatedAt so LiveRow can apply the same 60s freshness test
// to the polled price as to the browser-direct one.
type LivePriceWithTs = LivePrice & { updatedAt?: number | null };
type LiveFailure = ApiResponse<typeof api, "getLivePrices">["failures"][number];
type CoinAccount = ApiResponse<
  typeof api,
  "getCoinAccounts"
>["accounts"][number];

// Coin-account balance for the card headers: whole numbers stay whole,
// anything else keeps one decimal (100 → "100", 87.34 → "87.3").
function fmtBalance(n: number): string {
  const r = Math.round(n * 10) / 10;
  return Number.isInteger(r) ? String(r) : r.toFixed(1);
}

// The balance item in a major coin's card header band: a small tinted
// pill whose color follows the account's health — green while the
// balance is at or above the 100U start, a warm neutral tone in the
// 50–100 band, amber under 50, and a red 「已爆仓」 once the account is
// gone. Busted is a statistical marker only (a push notification goes
// out separately); the background testing keeps running and R scoring
// is unaffected. Renders nothing without an account (altcoins have
// none).
function AccountBalanceBit({ account }: { account?: CoinAccount }) {
  if (!account) return null;
  const pill =
    "inline-flex items-center whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 font-semibold leading-none";
  if (account.busted) {
    return (
      <span
        className={pill}
        style={{
          color: "var(--short)",
          backgroundColor:
            "color-mix(in srgb, var(--short) 12%, transparent)",
          borderColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--short) 40%, transparent)",
        }}
      >
        已爆仓
      </span>
    );
  }
  const b = account.balance;
  const tone =
    b >= 100 ? "var(--long)" : b >= 50 ? "var(--accent)" : "var(--warn)";
  return (
    <span
      className={pill}
      style={{
        color: tone,
        backgroundColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${tone} 12%, transparent)`,
        borderColor: `color-mix(in srgb, ${tone} 38%, transparent)`,
      }}
    >{`余额 ${fmtBalance(b)} U`}</span>
  );
}

// The plan (playbook) a call belongs to — mirrors the server's derivation:
// explicit plan column first, then the tags written into the rationale.
function planOf(t: Trade): string {
  if (t.plan?.trim()) return t.plan.trim();
  // Watch calls are 观望 before any rationale tag is read (P2-32,
  // 2026-11-17): a watch note mentioning 第三轮 must not be
  // misclassified as a 甲路 position plan.
  if (t.direction === "watch") return "观望";
  const r = t.rationale;
  const tag = r.match(/#\d+\s*(甲|乙)·([^\s（(]+)/);
  if (tag) return `${tag[1]}路·${tag[2]}`;
  if (r.includes("与甲路同一单")) return "乙路·破位回踩空";
  if (r.includes("第三轮")) return "甲路·反弹空";
  return "未标注";
}

function roundOf(t: Trade): string | null {
  // Rounds are tagged in the rationale (and sometimes the plan column),
  // in either Chinese or Arabic numerals: 第二轮 / 第5轮 both occur in
  // the real ledger (round 5's batches write 第5轮）. Whatever form the
  // tag was recorded in, the label shown is normalized to ordinary
  // Chinese numerals: 第二轮 / 第五轮.
  const hay = `${t.plan ?? ""} ${t.rationale}`;
  const m = hay.match(
    /第([0-9一二三四五六七八九十百壹贰叁肆伍陆柒捌玖拾佰零]+)轮/,
  );
  if (!m || !m[1]) return null;
  const n = parseRoundNumeral(m[1]);
  return n != null ? `第${toCnNumeral(n)}轮` : `第${m[1]}轮`;
}

// Grouping key for the round filter: untagged rows (e.g. the altcoin
// batch's watch calls) group under one label instead of disappearing.
const UNTAGGED_ROUND = "未标注轮次";

function roundKeyOf(t: Trade): string {
  return roundOf(t) ?? UNTAGGED_ROUND;
}

const CN_DIGITS: Record<string, number> = {
  一: 1,
  壹: 1,
  二: 2,
  贰: 2,
  三: 3,
  叁: 3,
  四: 4,
  肆: 4,
  五: 5,
  伍: 5,
  六: 6,
  陆: 6,
  七: 7,
  柒: 7,
  八: 8,
  捌: 8,
  九: 9,
  玖: 9,
};

const CN_DIGIT_CHARS = [
  "零",
  "一",
  "二",
  "三",
  "四",
  "五",
  "六",
  "七",
  "八",
  "九",
];

// Ordinary (lowercase) Chinese numeral: 5 → 五， 10 → 十，
// 11 → 十一， 20 → 二十， 100 → 一百. Round labels are displayed in
// this form no matter how the round was tagged when recorded.
function toCnNumeral(n: number): string {
  const d = (x: number): string => CN_DIGIT_CHARS[x] ?? "";
  if (n < 10) return d(n);
  if (n < 100) {
    const tens = Math.floor(n / 10);
    const ones = n % 10;
    return `${tens > 1 ? d(tens) : ""}十${ones > 0 ? d(ones) : ""}`;
  }
  const hundreds = Math.floor(n / 100);
  const rest = n % 100;
  if (rest === 0) return `${d(hundreds)}百`;
  if (rest < 10) return `${d(hundreds)}百零${d(rest)}`;
  const tens = Math.floor(rest / 10);
  const ones = rest % 10;
  return `${d(hundreds)}百${d(tens)}十${ones > 0 ? d(ones) : ""}`;
}

// Parses a round numeral in any recorded form — Arabic digits (5),
// ordinary Chinese （五 / 十一 / 二十） or formal uppercase Chinese
// （伍 / 拾壹 / 贰拾） — into its number. Null when unparseable.
function parseRoundNumeral(raw: string): number | null {
  if (/^\d+$/.test(raw)) return Number.parseInt(raw, 10);
  const s = raw.replace(/零/g, "");
  const baiIdx = s.indexOf("佰") >= 0 ? s.indexOf("佰") : s.indexOf("百");
  if (baiIdx >= 0) {
    const hPart = s.slice(0, baiIdx);
    const rest = s.slice(baiIdx + 1);
    const hundreds = hPart === "" ? 1 : CN_DIGITS[hPart];
    if (hundreds == null) return null;
    if (rest === "") return hundreds * 100;
    const r = parseRoundNumeral(rest);
    return r == null ? null : hundreds * 100 + r;
  }
  const tenIdx = s.search(/[十拾]/);
  if (tenIdx >= 0) {
    const tensPart = s.slice(0, tenIdx);
    const onesPart = s.slice(tenIdx + 1);
    const tens = tensPart === "" ? 1 : CN_DIGITS[tensPart];
    const ones = onesPart === "" ? 0 : CN_DIGITS[onesPart];
    if (tens == null || ones == null) return null;
    return tens * 10 + ones;
  }
  return CN_DIGITS[s] ?? null;
}

// The number inside a round label （第五轮 → 5). Returns null for
// untagged labels.
function roundNumberOf(label: string): number | null {
  const m = label.match(/第(.+)轮/);
  if (!m || !m[1]) return null;
  return parseRoundNumeral(m[1]);
}

// Calendar day (YYYY-MM-DD) of an instant in the user's timezone —
// rounds are a daily rhythm (a new round each evening), so "当天那轮"
// is the round whose calls were recorded on today's Shanghai date.
function shanghaiDayKey(iso: string): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
    timeZone: "Asia/Shanghai",
    year: "numeric",
    month: "2-digit",
    day: "2-digit",
  }).format(new Date(iso));
}

// Short plan name for compact rows: the full playbook names
// （甲路·反弹空 / 乙路·破位回踩空） plus a round tag cannot fit a phone's
// header row, and truncating them with an ellipsis just hides the text.
// Compact rows therefore carry the genuinely shortened name; the full
// plan + round stay in the chip's title and in the expanded detail rows.
function shortPlanLabel(t: Trade): string {
  const p = planOf(t);
  if (p.startsWith("甲路")) return "甲路·反弹";
  if (p.startsWith("乙路")) return "乙路·破位";
  return p;
}

// The mainstream coins, per the user's classification: BTC/ETH/SOL.
// (ZEC briefly sat in this set on 2026-11-14 and was removed the same
// day at the user's request.) Everything else recorded here is an
// altcoin （山寨）. The card list renders the majors section first,
// altcoins below.
const MAJOR_SYMBOLS = new Set(["BTC", "ETH", "SOL"]);

// Canonical display order for the mainstream coins — used to append the
// majors that have no call in the selected round, so the 主流币 section
// always lists all three, traded or not.
const MAJOR_ORDER = ["BTC", "ETH", "SOL"];

// Whether an open call is still waiting on its trigger — i.e. there is no
// position yet and any floating figure would be invented. True when the
// row is stored as a conditional order, and also for the dual-path plans
// （甲路·反弹 / 乙路·破位回踩）: both playbooks are trigger-based by
// definition (rebound into the zone and stall / close-break then retest),
// and the third-round rows under those plans were recorded with
// entry_type "immediate" even though their entry only exists once the
// trigger fires — the INIT 甲路 row behind the fake +16.73% is one of
// them. Trigger state is settled only by the K-line review either way.
function isTriggerPending(t: Trade): boolean {
  if (t.direction === "watch" || t.status !== "open") return false;
  if (t.entry_type === "conditional") return true;
  const p = planOf(t);
  return p.startsWith("甲路") || p.startsWith("乙路");
}

// A dual-path leg （甲路/乙路）: the two alternative plans recorded for
// one coin in one round, of which at most one ever becomes a position.
function isDualLeg(t: Trade): boolean {
  const p = planOf(t);
  return p.startsWith("甲路") || p.startsWith("乙路");
}

// A leg counts as triggered once its review rules it a real position:
// verdict 对/错 means price reached the entry and the position existed;
// 未触发 (no_trigger) is the leg that never fired. While a leg is still
// open its trigger is unsettled by definition (K-line review decides).
function isTriggeredLeg(t: Trade): boolean {
  return (
    t.status === "reviewed" &&
    (t.verdict === "correct" || t.verdict === "wrong")
  );
}

// Whether a reviewed row is only an INTERIM snapshot rather than a
// final settlement (P2-04, 2026-11-17 — mirrors the server's
// isInterim): the outcome note carries either marker — 仍在进行中
// or 未终局 (the ledger writes them together, "进行中（未终局结
// 算）", but either one alone means the leg has not finally
// settled). Every finality test on the page reads through this one
// helper so the two spellings can never drift apart.
function isInterim(t: Trade): boolean {
  const note = t.outcome_note ?? "";
  return note.includes("仍在进行中") || note.includes("未终局");
}

// Whether a reviewed leg was VOIDED （作废） rather than merely
// untriggered (user, 2026-11-17: 标成已作废，用黄色）. At the storage
// layer a voided leg shares the no_trigger verdict with a leg that
// simply never fired — the schema's verdict enum has no voided
// value — and the distinction lives only in the outcome note,
// exactly like isInterim distinguishes an interim snapshot by its
// note marker. Every voided spelling in the ledger is the leg's own
// voiding statement (full-corpus check of all 47 no_trigger notes,
// 2026-11-17, no false positives): 两路全废 / 随之作废 （姊妹腿先触
// 发、本腿随之作废） / 到期作废·按条款作废 / the old two-choice 二
// 选一作废. Watch calls are judgments, not orders, so the direction
// guard excludes them even when their note mentions a voided leg.
// Pure derivation: never stored, and rendering branches ONLY — it
// must never feed settledR / winRateR / pnlMark / finalPnlPct /
// madeCount or any other statistics caliber.
function isVoided(t: Trade): boolean {
  return (
    t.status === "reviewed" &&
    t.direction !== "watch" &&
    t.verdict === "no_trigger" &&
    (t.outcome_note ?? "").includes("作废")
  );
}

// A reviewed trade can be an INTERIM snapshot: the review found the
// leg triggered and in position, and its outcome note still reports
// it as running （仍在进行中 / 未终局）. Such a leg has triggered but has NOT
// settled — the same test the server's pnlOutcome and this page's
// pnlMark use. The note marker is the ONLY finality signal: an
// interim record may legitimately carry first_touch "tp" when the
// first take-profit was touched and booked only （只记账、不终局 —
// the position runs on to the second target / stop / expiry, e.g.
// AKE 乙 id 80), so first_touch must never veto the marker.
function isStillRunning(t: Trade): boolean {
  return (
    t.status === "reviewed" &&
    (t.verdict === "correct" || t.verdict === "wrong") &&
    isInterim(t)
  );
}

// A still-running leg whose FIRST take-profit has already been
// touched and booked （只记账、不终局）: the position runs on toward
// the second target / stop, but TP1 is in the bag, so the leg wears
// its own state name — 已触发第一止盈·待观察. Two encodings of the
// touch exist in the ledger and either one counts (user spec):
// first_touch "tp" (AKE 乙 id 80), or first_touch "none" with the
// outcome note recording the arrival (IDOL 甲 id 81: "已触发并第一
// 止盈到达…首触第一止盈 0.0190…只记账、不终局"). The note test is
// the shared noteLevelState clause scan (P2-08, 2026-11-17): only
// an actual "hit" reading counts — "第一止盈未到" style misses
// never match it.
function tp1Booked(t: Trade): boolean {
  if (t.direction === "watch" || !isStillRunning(t)) return false;
  if (t.first_touch === "tp") return true;
  return noteLevelState(t.outcome_note ?? "", "第一止盈") === "hit";
}

// Gross R banked at the first take-profit, from stored prices:
// |TP1 − entry| ÷ |entry − stop|, always positive (TP1 sits on the
// profit side by construction). AKE 乙： |0.01220−0.01100| ÷
// |0.01100−0.01020| = +1.50R; IDOL 甲： |0.0190−0.0208| ÷
// |0.0208−0.0220| = +1.50R.
function tp1BookedR(t: Trade): number | null {
  const risk = Math.abs(t.entry_price - t.stop_loss);
  if (!(risk > 0)) return null;
  return Math.abs(t.take_profit - t.entry_price) / risk;
}

// The single test behind every TP1 graying on the page — the TP1
// vertical line on the data bar and the TP1 price NUMBER wherever
// the targets print merged (bar label row, card-face terms,
// expanded history terms) all gray off this one test, in the one
// --tp-passed gray, so line and number always agree. The condition
// is tp1Booked (user caliber change, 2026-11-16, superseding the
// same-day live-price test): the leg has TOUCHED its first
// take-profit and the touch has been booked by an interim review
// while the leg is still running. The gray then STICKS — it does
// not depend on where the live price sits now, and price falling
// back inside TP1 does not restore the green — until the leg's
// final settlement. A leg that has not booked TP1 keeps line and
// number in their original colors however far price wanders, and
// the second target never grays.
function tp1Grayed(t: Trade): boolean {
  return tp1Booked(t);
}

// The breakout line an 乙路 leg must CLOSE beyond on a 15-minute
// candle before its retest entry can trigger — written into the
// rationale by the recording convention ("15m收盘破位0.0585后回踩
// 0.0600确认入场" / "15m收盘站上0.01150后回踩0.01100确认入场";
// older rows name it as （触发0.0635）). This parse is byte-for-byte
// the server's breakoutLineOfRow (P1-01, 2026-11-17): only the
// clause before the first 作废 is read (the invalidation tail must
// never supply the line), thousands commas are stripped repeatedly
// (71,850 → 71850), and the pattern priority is 触发 → 收盘破位 →
// 收盘跌破 → 收盘站上. Null when nothing matches — never fall back
// to a touch price or the entry price. The server checks the actual
// candles; this one only labels the distance shown on the card.
function breakoutLineOf(t: Trade): number | null {
  let clause = t.rationale.split("作废")[0] ?? "";
  let prev = "";
  while (prev !== clause) {
    prev = clause;
    clause = clause.replace(/(\d),(\d)/g, "$1$2");
  }
  const m =
    clause.match(/触发\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/) ??
    clause.match(/收盘破位\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/) ??
    clause.match(/收盘跌破\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/) ??
    clause.match(/收盘站上\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
  const v = m?.[1] ? Number(m[1]) : NaN;
  return Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0 ? v : null;
}

// One lifecycle name per order (user: the state names were a mess —
// 条件单·待结算 / 待复盘 / 未触发 described overlapping stages, and
// the stop/targets were drawn before the entry condition existed).
//  · 甲路 has no precondition stage — price reaching its entry IS
//    the trigger — so before the review confirms the trigger it is
//    simply 未触发.
//  · 乙路 alone runs a two-step entry (a 15m close breaks the line,
//    then price retests the entry), so it alone splits the wait:
//    条件未满足 → 条件满足·未触发. Whether the break happened is
//    checked against Gate 15m candles by getConditionStates.
//  · A leg in position — an immediate entry, or an interim review
//    that found it triggered and still running — is 已触发·待观察.
//  · A reviewed leg whose result is final is 已结算.
// Watch calls are not orders and keep their own labels; legState is
// only applied to position legs.
type LegState =
  | "cond_unmet"
  | "cond_met"
  | "untriggered"
  | "observing"
  | "settled";

const LEG_STATE_LABEL: Record<LegState, string> = {
  cond_unmet: "条件未满足",
  cond_met: "条件满足·未触发",
  untriggered: "未触发",
  observing: "已触发·待观察",
  settled: "已结算",
};

// Lifecycle timestamps for one leg, from getConditionStates (Gate
// futures 15m candles, the settlement caliber): conditionMetAt is
// when an 乙路 leg's breakout precondition was met — the moment
// it entered the 挂单中 state; triggeredAt is when a
// review-confirmed dual-path/conditional leg actually touched its
// entry price — its real 开仓 time, as opposed to opened_at,
// which is only when the call was recorded (every leg of a round
// shares that instant, which is why the old time line showed the
// same "开仓" time for all of them). The server persists that
// 开仓 time on the trade row the first time it is computed
// (triggered_at, user: 把开仓时间写进空间存住）, so the card's
// fallback chain reads lifecycle → stored triggered_at →
// placement time. Null when the candles offer no evidence; the
// card then falls back to the placement time.
type LegLifecycle = {
  conditionMetAt: string | null;
  triggeredAt: string | null;
  // Whether the server's candle scan could actually verify this
  // leg (getConditionStates' data_status, P2-11): "unavailable" /
  // "insufficient_history" mean a cond_unmet reading is NOT a
  // checked-and-failed condition — the leg reads 行情暂不可核实
  // instead of 条件未满足. Null when absent (older payloads).
  dataStatus: "ok" | "unavailable" | "insufficient_history" | null;
};

function legState(t: Trade, conditionMet: boolean): LegState {
  if (t.status === "reviewed") {
    return isStillRunning(t) ? "observing" : "settled";
  }
  if (isTriggerPending(t)) {
    if (pathOf(t) === "乙路") {
      return conditionMet ? "cond_met" : "cond_unmet";
    }
    return "untriggered";
  }
  return "observing";
}

// Whether the leg is still waiting for its trigger to be confirmed.
// Drives the live row's state + distance text (never a floating
// figure) and the gray form of its data bar.
function isPreTriggerState(t: Trade, conditionMet: boolean): boolean {
  if (t.direction === "watch" || t.status !== "open") return false;
  const s = legState(t, conditionMet);
  return s === "cond_unmet" || s === "cond_met" || s === "untriggered";
}

// Whether the leg's stop and targets stay off the card entirely.
// Only an 乙路 leg whose breakout precondition has NOT happened yet
// hides them (user correction: 未触发的止盈止损也放回原位，就乙的
// 那个条件没满足就不要写). Every other waiting leg — 甲路 未触发,
// 乙路 条件满足·未触发 — shows its full 入场/止损/止盈 terms again,
// exactly where a triggered leg shows them; only its bar stays gray
// and its live row keeps distance instead of a floating figure until
// it actually triggers.
function levelsHidden(t: Trade, conditionMet: boolean): boolean {
  return (
    t.direction !== "watch" &&
    t.status === "open" &&
    legState(t, conditionMet) === "cond_unmet"
  );
}

const mono = "font-[ui-monospace,SFMono-Regular,Menlo,monospace] tabular-nums";

function fmtPrice(n: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return "—";
  if (n >= 1000) {
    return n.toLocaleString("en-US", {
      minimumFractionDigits: 1,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    });
  }
  if (n >= 1) return n.toFixed(4).replace(/\.?0+$/, "");
  // Sub-1 prices (small caps): scale the decimals to the price's magnitude
  // so ~7 significant figures survive — 0.0003924 must not collapse to
  // 0.00039, and 0.10988 must not round to 0.1099.
  const decimals = Math.min(12, Math.floor(-Math.log10(n)) + 6);
  return n.toFixed(decimals).replace(/\.?0+$/, "");
}

function fmtTime(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  return d.toLocaleString("zh-CN", {
    month: "numeric",
    day: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
    hour12: false,
    timeZone: "Asia/Shanghai",
  });
}

function heldText(openedAt: string, reviewedAt: string | null): string {
  const end = reviewedAt ? new Date(reviewedAt).getTime() : Date.now();
  const mins = Math.max(0, Math.round((end - new Date(openedAt).getTime()) / 60000));
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins} 分钟`;
  const h = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  const m = mins % 60;
  return m > 0 ? `${h} 小时 ${m} 分` : `${h} 小时`;
}

// The price a reviewed trade actually exited at: the REAL exit
// price the settlement flow recorded (exit_price, P1-02) wins when
// present; otherwise the review record's derivation — the
// take-profit if it was touched first, the stop if it was, otherwise
// the price when the review ran. Untriggered calls have no exit.
function exitPriceOf(t: Trade): number | null {
  if (t.status !== "reviewed") return null;
  if (t.verdict === "no_trigger") return null;
  if (t.exit_price != null) return t.exit_price;
  if (t.first_touch === "tp") return t.take_profit;
  if (t.first_touch === "sl") return t.stop_loss;
  return t.review_price;
}

// ---- Touch-case caliber for FINALLY settled legs (user, 2026-11-16) ----
// A settled card carries one extra annotation at the right of its
// 最终盈亏 row, naming which lines the position actually touched:
//  · sl_only — only the stop was touched → 「已触止损」, figures as-is.
//  · tp1_then_sl — the first take-profit was touched first and booked
//    （只记账）, then price fell back to the stop and the ledger closed
//    the whole position there → the card shows 「第一止盈 +n%」 and
//    「已触止损 −n%」, and the final P/L is recomputed by 算法一： 75%
//    of the position is treated as having exited at TP1 and the
//    remaining 25% as stopped out (0.75 × TP1% + 0.25 × SL%).
//  · both_tp — both take-profits were touched → 「已盈利」, figures as-is.
// A leg that touched only TP1 and then expired, and a leg that touched
// neither line, keep the standing display untouched. The stored rows
// and the ledger's net R are NOT rewritten. 累计 R caliber
// (user, 2026-11-16, synced by the same-day follow-up): EVERY
// cumulative surface — a single coin's cumulative (card-header
// 累计， the altcoin R ranking, the server's by_symbol rows, fed
// by coinSettledR / coinRealizedR) AND the scoreboard's TOP total
// with its detail-window sums (settledR / the server's winRateR)
// — counts a finally settled leg that touched its first
// take-profit WHOLE at its first take-profit R (tp1BookedR — AKE
// 乙 books +1.50R, the stop leg is NOT subtracted; a leg that
// touched TP1 then expired, like IDOL round-6 乙， likewise books
// its TP1 R instead of its floating loss), a fully profitable leg
// at its full R and a stop-only leg at −1R. What follows 算法一 here: the card-face 最终盈亏 %
// and the 盈/亏 mark (via finalPnlPct). The WIN-RATE surfaces no
// longer follow 算法一 (user caliber change, 2026-11-16: 只影响胜
// 率里面的内容）: inside win-rate content a leg that touched its
// first take-profit at any point counts as a win at its whole TP1
// R (winRateMark / winRateR; the server's pnlOutcome / winRateR
// mirror them), even when its final money result was a loss. The settled status chip no longer
// prints a per-leg R next to its 盈/亏 mark at all (user,
// 2026-11-16: the +0.88R 算法一 figure there meant nothing once
// the calibers split — delete it); running legs keep theirs.
//
// Detection rests on the ledger's own final record. first_touch names
// the EXIT level on a final record (sl = closed at the stop, tp =
// closed at a target), so a TP1-then-stop leg reads first_touch "sl"
// even though TP1 came first chronologically (AKE 乙 id 80). That TP1
// was really touched is read from the outcome note — the ledger always
// writes it out （第一止盈 0.01220 已到达（已记账） vs 第一止盈 0.3000
// 未到） — corroborated by the window extreme passing TP1. The window
// alone canNOT decide: it starts at placement, so a pre-entry excursion
// can cross the TP1 level without the position ever seeing it (MAGMA
// 甲 id 77: window high 0.30342 > TP1 0.3000, note says 未到 — the note
// wins). Both-targets legs are recognised the same way: the stored TP2
// column's window extreme when the column exists, else the note's own
// 第二止盈 settlement wording (older rows keep TP2 only in the note,
// e.g. ELSA 乙 id 66 / HYPE 乙 id 56, whose review_price is the TP2).

// A position leg whose review is final (not an interim 仍在进行中
// snapshot, not an untriggered leg, not a watch judgment).
function isFinallySettled(t: Trade): boolean {
  return (
    t.status === "reviewed" &&
    t.direction !== "watch" &&
    (t.verdict === "correct" || t.verdict === "wrong") &&
    !isStillRunning(t)
  );
}

// Whether the settlement note records a level （第一止盈 / 第二止盈） as
// touched ("hit") or explicitly not touched ("miss"). EVERY occurrence
// of the level word is read (P2-29, 2026-11-17): each occurrence's own
// clause (up to the next ，/；/。) is judged by the EARLIEST keyword
// inside it — a clause can carry both, e.g. HYPE 乙 id 56's TP2:
// "第二止盈 65.20 … 首触（00:45 根低 65.374 未到；…）" is a hit (the
// 未到 describes an earlier candle, inside the same clause) — and the
// LAST clause with a verdict wins, so a note that first reports a
// level 未到 and later reports it 已到达 reads as hit. Thousands
// commas are stripped first so a price like 71,200 does not break the
// clause scan. "首触第一止盈" puts the arrival word before the level
// name and is checked separately.
function noteLevelState(
  note: string,
  word: "第一止盈" | "第二止盈",
): "hit" | "miss" | null {
  const clean = note.replace(/(\d),(\d)/g, "$1$2");
  if (clean.includes(`首触${word}`)) return "hit";
  let result: "hit" | "miss" | null = null;
  let searchFrom = 0;
  for (;;) {
    const idx = clean.indexOf(word, searchFrom);
    if (idx < 0) break;
    const rest = clean.slice(idx + word.length);
    const end = rest.search(/[。；，,]/);
    const seg = end >= 0 ? rest.slice(0, end) : rest;
    const earliest = (keys: string[]): number => {
      let best = -1;
      for (const k of keys) {
        const i = seg.indexOf(k);
        if (i >= 0 && (best < 0 || i < best)) best = i;
      }
      return best;
    };
    const hitAt = earliest(["到达", "已到", "已触", "首触", "触及", "结算", "记账"]);
    const missAt = earliest(["未到", "未触", "未达"]);
    if (hitAt >= 0 && (missAt < 0 || hitAt < missAt)) result = "hit";
    else if (missAt >= 0) result = "miss";
    searchFrom = idx + word.length;
  }
  return result;
}

// TP1 touched (and booked) before the position was finally stopped
// out: exit at the stop, the note records TP1's arrival, and the
// window extreme passed TP1 at some point in the holding window.
function tp1ThenSl(t: Trade): boolean {
  if (!isFinallySettled(t) || t.first_touch !== "sl") return false;
  if (noteLevelState(t.outcome_note ?? "", "第一止盈") !== "hit") {
    return false;
  }
  if (t.direction === "long") {
    return t.window_high == null || t.window_high >= t.take_profit;
  }
  return t.window_low == null || t.window_low <= t.take_profit;
}

// Both take-profits touched on a leg that exited at a target.
// Structured evidence only (P2-28, 2026-11-17): the settlement note
// recording 第二止盈 as hit, or the recorded exit reason being the
// second target. The window-extreme shortcut is gone — the window
// starts at placement and can cross TP2 before entry, which
// contradicts the note-wins-over-window caliber TP1 already follows.
function bothTargetsHit(t: Trade): boolean {
  if (!isFinallySettled(t) || t.first_touch !== "tp") return false;
  if (t.exit_reason === "tp2") return true;
  return noteLevelState(t.outcome_note ?? "", "第二止盈") === "hit";
}

type TouchCase = "sl_only" | "tp1_then_sl" | "both_tp";

function touchCase(t: Trade): TouchCase | null {
  if (!isFinallySettled(t)) return null;
  if (t.first_touch === "sl") return tp1ThenSl(t) ? "tp1_then_sl" : "sl_only";
  if (t.first_touch === "tp") return bothTargetsHit(t) ? "both_tp" : null;
  return null;
}

// Whether a FINALLY settled position leg touched its first
// take-profit at any point before its final exit — the trigger of
// the win-rate caliber (user, 2026-11-16: 只要摸到第一止盈就算盈）.
// Three shapes: the leg exited at a target (first_touch "tp" — TP1
// is necessarily passed on the way); it touched TP1 and was later
// stopped out (tp1ThenSl); or it touched TP1 and ran to an expiry /
// floating exit — the note records TP1's arrival and the window
// extreme passed TP1 (the same note-first, window-corroborated test
// tp1ThenSl uses; the note wins over the window, which starts at
// placement). Mirrors the server's tp1Touched.
function tp1Touched(t: Trade): boolean {
  if (!isFinallySettled(t)) return false;
  if (t.first_touch === "tp") return true;
  if (tp1ThenSl(t)) return true;
  if (noteLevelState(t.outcome_note ?? "", "第一止盈") !== "hit") {
    return false;
  }
  if (t.direction === "long") {
    return t.window_high == null || t.window_high >= t.take_profit;
  }
  return t.window_low == null || t.window_low <= t.take_profit;
}

// Signed % from entry to the first take-profit (positive by
// construction) and to the stop (negative by construction).
function tp1PctOf(t: Trade): number {
  const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  return (s * (t.take_profit - t.entry_price) / t.entry_price) * 100;
}

function slPctOf(t: Trade): number {
  const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  return (s * (t.stop_loss - t.entry_price) / t.entry_price) * 100;
}

// Signed % from entry to the FULL take-profit （全止盈）: the second
// target when the call carries one, otherwise the first target IS
// the full target. Used by the touch badge's 已盈利 case.
function tpFullPctOf(t: Trade): number {
  const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  const target = t.take_profit2 ?? t.take_profit;
  return (s * (target - t.entry_price) / t.entry_price) * 100;
}

// 算法一 (user, 2026-11-16): 75% of the position exits at the first
// take-profit, the remaining 25% rides to the stop. AKE 乙：
// 0.75 × +10.91% + 0.25 × −7.27% = +6.36% (was −7.27%).
function algo1PnlPct(t: Trade): number {
  return 0.75 * tp1PctOf(t) + 0.25 * slPctOf(t);
}

// Final P/L as a percentage of the entry price, from stored review fields
// only. An untriggered call settles at exactly 0% — the position never
// existed. Watch calls have no position, so they get no P/L percentage.
// A TP1-then-stop leg books 算法一 instead of the bare stop exit (see
// the touch-case caliber above).
function finalPnlPct(t: Trade): number | null {
  if (t.status !== "reviewed") return null;
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  if (t.verdict === "no_trigger") return 0;
  if (touchCase(t) === "tp1_then_sl") return algo1PnlPct(t);
  const exit = exitPriceOf(t);
  if (exit == null || !(t.entry_price > 0)) return null;
  const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  return (s * (exit - t.entry_price) / t.entry_price) * 100;
}

function fmtPct(n: number): string {
  return `${n > 0 ? "+" : ""}${n.toFixed(2)}%`;
}

function pctCls(n: number): string {
  return n > 0
    ? "text-[var(--long)]"
    : n < 0
      ? "text-[var(--short)]"
      : "text-[var(--dim)]";
}

// Realized R, mirroring the server's definition.
function tradeR(t: Trade): number | null {
  if (t.status !== "reviewed") return null;
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  if (t.verdict === "no_trigger") return null;
  const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  const risk = s * (t.entry_price - t.stop_loss);
  if (!(risk > 0)) return null;
  if (t.first_touch === "tp") return (s * (t.take_profit - t.entry_price)) / risk;
  if (t.first_touch === "sl") return -1;
  if (t.review_price == null) return null;
  return (s * (t.review_price - t.entry_price)) / risk;
}

// R that counts toward the scoreboard's TOP 累计 R. Watch calls
// never count, an untriggered call books exactly 0, and every other
// leg follows tradeR — EXCEPT a finally settled leg that touched
// its first take-profit, which books its WHOLE first take-profit R
// (user follow-up, 2026-11-16: 累计 R 那边也同步地去算 — the
// TP1-touch caliber now governs the cumulative R too, so a
// TP1-then-stop leg and a TP1-then-expiry leg both book +TP1 R
// here instead of their final-exit R). Mirrors the server's
// winRateR, which now feeds the headline total_r. Used by the
// detail windows so the per-trade list sums to the headline 累计 R.
function settledR(t: Trade): number | null {
  if (t.status !== "reviewed") return null;
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  // Interim snapshots book nothing yet (P2-04 — the server's
  // winRateR / coinRealizedR exclude them the same way); a running
  // leg's floating figure is tradeR's business, not this one's.
  if (isInterim(t)) return null;
  if (t.verdict === "no_trigger") return 0;
  if (tp1Touched(t)) return tp1BookedR(t);
  return tradeR(t);
}

// R that counts toward a SINGLE coin's cumulative (user caliber,
// 2026-11-16: 单个币的累计 R 是它历史成交单的合计——全盈利整笔 R
// 全加、纯止损减其 R、先触第一止盈又回踩止损的整笔只按第一止盈
// 算、不减止损）: a TP1-then-stop leg books its WHOLE first
// take-profit R (tp1BookedR) instead of the −1R stop (AKE 乙：
// +1.50R, so AKE's cumulative over its one settled leg reads
// +1.50R). Mirrors the server's coinRealizedR, which feeds the
// by_symbol rows. Since the same-day follow-up synced the top
// total to the same TP1-touch caliber, this is now identical to
// settledR for every leg — the two names stay because the
// surfaces were specced separately.
function coinSettledR(t: Trade): number | null {
  if (tp1Touched(t)) return tp1BookedR(t);
  return settledR(t);
}

type DetailKind = "total" | "open" | "winrate" | "totalR";

// Which win rate the page shows. "judgment" is the original caliber
// （判断对 ÷（对+错）). "pnl" （盈亏胜率） counts only position trades
// that finally settled, under the WIN-RATE caliber (user, 2026-11-16:
// 只影响胜率里面的内容）: a leg that touched its first take-profit
// at any point counts as a win (winRateMark), whatever its final
// money result — the card face's 最终盈亏 and 盈/亏 mark keep the
// 算法一 caliber and do NOT follow. "jia" / "yi" （甲胜率 / 乙胜率，
// user, 2026-11-16: 甲乙胜率分开各一个选项） each show ONE dual
// path's own win rate under the same win-rate caliber — the two
// paths are separate options, no longer one combined comparison.
// The surfaces that show one number per coin (card headers, the
// altcoin ranking, the 总判断 window's per-symbol rows) keep the
// win-rate caliber under its own 盈亏胜率 label in the jia/yi
// modes too — only the scoreboard cell and the win-rate detail
// window render the selected path itself. The user asked for the
// P/L caliber to be the default; one switch drives the scoreboard
// cell, the win-rate detail window, the per-plan / per-symbol rows
// and the coin cards.
type WinRateMode = "pnl" | "judgment" | "jia" | "yi";

// One coin's headline figures over an arbitrary set of its trades: the
// win rate under the page's current caliber plus the coin's cumulative
// R under the SINGLE-coin caliber (coinSettledR — a TP1-then-stop leg
// counts whole at its TP1 R). SymbolCard runs it over the trades the
// card shows (the selected round's); the altcoin ranking runs it over
// ALL of the coin's trades （整体/总笔）, so both surfaces share one
// caliber. The scoreboard's top 累计 R is NOT this sum — it keeps
// settledR (see StatsDetailModal / getStats).
function coinFigures(
  trades: Trade[],
  mode: WinRateMode,
): { rate: number | null; totalR: number } {
  const totalR = trades.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (coinSettledR(t) ?? 0), 0);
  // Win/loss counts follow the WIN-RATE caliber (winRateMark: a leg
  // that touched TP1 counts as 盈）, not the card face's money mark.
  const pnlWins = trades.filter((t) => winRateMark(t) === "盈").length;
  const pnlLosses = trades.filter((t) => winRateMark(t) === "亏").length;
  const judgedCorrect = trades.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "correct",
  ).length;
  const judgedWrong = trades.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "wrong",
  ).length;
  const rate =
    mode !== "judgment"
      ? pnlWins + pnlLosses > 0
        ? pnlWins / (pnlWins + pnlLosses)
        : null
      : judgedCorrect + judgedWrong > 0
        ? judgedCorrect / (judgedCorrect + judgedWrong)
        : null;
  return { rate, totalR };
}

// One dual path's figures （甲路 or 乙路） over an arbitrary trade set,
// under BOTH calibers at once: the 甲胜率 / 乙胜率 views each report
// one path's figures, so each path reports its win rate under the
// WIN-RATE caliber (winRateMark — a leg that touched TP1 counts as
// 盈， and its R inside these figures is the whole TP1 R, winRateR),
// its 判断胜率 (verdicts), its cumulative R and its
// per-decided-trade expectancy (expectancy = summed win-rate R over
// the 对/错 trades ÷ their count; untriggered legs book 0 and never
// enter it).
// Legs are classified by pathOf, so legs whose path lives only in the
// rationale tag (the round-7 altcoin pairs) count too; single calls and
// watch calls belong to neither path and never enter these figures.
function pathFigures(trades: Trade[], path: "甲路" | "乙路") {
  const legs = trades.filter((t) => pathOf(t) === path);
  const wins = legs.filter((t) => winRateMark(t) === "盈").length;
  const losses = legs.filter((t) => winRateMark(t) === "亏").length;
  const correct = legs.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "correct",
  ).length;
  const wrong = legs.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "wrong",
  ).length;
  const noTrigger = legs.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "no_trigger",
  ).length;
  const decidedLegs = legs.filter(
    (t) =>
      t.status === "reviewed" &&
      (t.verdict === "correct" || t.verdict === "wrong"),
  );
  const decidedR = decidedLegs.reduce(
    (acc, t) => acc + (winRateR(t) ?? 0),
    0,
  );
  const totalR = legs.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (winRateR(t) ?? 0), 0);
  return {
    total: legs.length,
    wins,
    losses,
    pnlRate: wins + losses > 0 ? wins / (wins + losses) : null,
    correct,
    wrong,
    noTrigger,
    judgmentRate:
      correct + wrong > 0 ? correct / (correct + wrong) : null,
    totalR,
    expectation:
      decidedLegs.length > 0 ? decidedR / decidedLegs.length : null,
  };
}

// How the 山寨币 section is ordered: "default" keeps the standing
// most-recent-call order; "winrate" / "r" rank the visible altcoin
// cards by the coin's OVERALL figures (every round it has ever been
// called in), highest first.
type AltRankMode = "default" | "winrate" | "r";

function RateText({
  mode,
  judgment,
  pnl,
}: {
  mode: WinRateMode;
  judgment: number | null;
  pnl: number | null;
}) {
  const rate = mode === "judgment" ? judgment : pnl;
  return <>{rate != null ? `${Math.round(rate * 100)}%` : "—"}</>;
}

// Phone-width fit for the scoreboard tiles: the four headline values
// render in ui-monospace (≈0.6em advance per glyph) inside a tile
// whose text width is only ≈60px at a 390px viewport, so a long value
// — 累计 R "+20.39" is the standing example — overflows its tile and
// gets clipped (audit flagged it truncated by 12px). All four values
// share one size, stepped down by the longest value's glyph count, so
// the row stays uniform and nothing clips; at ≥sm the tiles are wide
// enough for the full text-2xl at any realistic length.
function statValueSize(texts: string[]): string {
  const n = Math.max(1, ...texts.map((t) => t.length));
  if (n <= 4) return "text-xl";
  if (n <= 6) return "text-base";
  if (n === 7) return "text-sm";
  return "text-[11px]";
}

function WinRateModeToggle({
  mode,
  onChange,
}: {
  mode: WinRateMode;
  onChange: (m: WinRateMode) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">胜率口径</span>
      <div className="flex gap-1" role="group" aria-label="胜率口径切换">
        {(
          [
            ["pnl", "盈亏胜率"],
            ["judgment", "判断胜率"],
            ["jia", "甲胜率"],
            ["yi", "乙胜率"],
          ] as const
        ).map(([key, label]) => (
          <button
            key={key}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={mode === key}
            onClick={() => onChange(key)}
            className={`min-h-8 rounded-md px-2.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
              mode === key
                ? "bg-[var(--text)] text-[var(--bg)]"
                : "border border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--dim)]"
            }`}
          >
            {label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// The 盈/亏 mark appended after a settled trade's verdict: the judgment
// （对/错） and the money result are separate facts — a call can be judged
// 对 and still have lost money. Only a position trade (long/short) that
// really triggered AND has finally settled earns the mark:
//  · watch calls never had a position;
//  · no_trigger calls never opened, so there is nothing to win or lose;
//  · a reviewed trade whose outcome note still reports it as running
//    （仍在进行中） carries only an interim snapshot, not a final
//    result — it is marked only once a later review closes it out.
//    The note marker alone decides: first_touch "tp" does NOT make
//    it final, because a first-take-profit touch can be booked only
//    （只记账、不终局） on an interim record whose position runs on.
// The sign comes from finalPnlPct — the same 最终盈亏 figure the
// expanded history prints for the row (same sign as its R by
// construction); positive → 盈， negative → 亏， exactly 0 → no mark.
function pnlMark(t: Trade): { label: "盈" | "亏"; cls: string } | null {
  if (t.status !== "reviewed") return null;
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  if (t.verdict !== "correct" && t.verdict !== "wrong") return null;
  const settled = !isInterim(t);
  if (!settled) return null;
  const pnl = finalPnlPct(t);
  if (pnl == null || pnl === 0) return null;
  return pnl > 0
    ? { label: "盈", cls: "text-[var(--long)]" }
    : { label: "亏", cls: "text-[var(--short)]" };
}

// The win/loss classification used INSIDE the win-rate surfaces
// only (user caliber, 2026-11-16: 跟最终盈亏不一样，只影响胜率里
// 面的内容）: a finally settled position leg that TOUCHED its
// first take-profit at any point counts as 盈， full stop — a
// TP1-then-stop leg, and a leg that touched TP1 then expired at a
// loss (IDOL round-6 乙）, both flip to 盈 here. A leg that never
// touched TP1 scores by its final P/L exactly as pnlMark does.
// The card face's 盈/亏 mark and 最终盈亏 % still follow pnlMark /
// finalPnlPct — this mark never renders on a card, it only feeds
// win-rate counts (coinFigures, pathFigures, the win-rate detail
// window). The gate order mirrors the server's pnlOutcome (P2-07,
// 2026-11-17): not reviewed / watch / verdict not 对·错 / interim
// → no mark at all; then TP1 touched → 盈 (even when the final P/L
// is exactly 0 — touched TP1 and broke even still counts); then
// the final P/L's sign, with exactly 0 counting as neither.
function winRateMark(t: Trade): "盈" | "亏" | null {
  if (t.status !== "reviewed") return null;
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  if (t.verdict !== "correct" && t.verdict !== "wrong") return null;
  if (isStillRunning(t)) return null;
  if (tp1Touched(t)) return "盈";
  const pnl = finalPnlPct(t);
  if (pnl == null || pnl === 0) return null;
  return pnl > 0 ? "盈" : "亏";
}

// The R a leg contributes under the TP1-touch caliber (the 甲/乙
// path figures' cumulative R and expectancy, the win-rate window's
// per-plan rows via the server): a leg that touched its first
// take-profit books its WHOLE first take-profit R (tp1BookedR);
// every other leg books its plain settledR (a loss subtracts as
// before). Since the 2026-11-16 follow-up synced the 累计 R
// surfaces to this same caliber, winRateR and settledR now agree
// on every leg — mirrors the server's winRateR.
function winRateR(t: Trade): number | null {
  if (winRateMark(t) == null && settledR(t) == null) return null;
  if (tp1Touched(t)) return tp1BookedR(t);
  return settledR(t);
}

function verdictMeta(v: Trade["verdict"]): { label: string; cls: string } {
  switch (v) {
    case "correct":
      return { label: "判断对", cls: "text-[var(--long)] border-[var(--long)]" };
    case "wrong":
      return { label: "判断错", cls: "text-[var(--short)] border-[var(--short)]" };
    case "no_trigger":
      return { label: "未触发", cls: "text-[var(--accent)] border-[var(--accent)]" };
    default:
      return { label: "待复盘", cls: "text-[var(--dim)] border-[var(--border)]" };
  }
}

function DirectionChip({
  direction,
  leverage,
}: {
  direction: Trade["direction"];
  // Majors live only: the leverage printed exchange-style right after
  // 做多/做空 (e.g. "做多 x10") — no 杠杆 wording, same chip style.
  leverage?: number;
}) {
  if (direction === "watch") {
    return (
      <span className="inline-block shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded bg-[var(--accent)]/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
        观望
      </span>
    );
  }
  const long = direction === "long";
  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-block shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
        long
          ? "bg-[var(--long)]/15 text-[var(--long)]"
          : "bg-[var(--short)]/15 text-[var(--short)]"
      }`}
    >
      {long ? "做多" : "做空"}
      {leverage != null ? ` x${leverage}` : ""}
    </span>
  );
}

// Which of the two dual paths a leg runs on — 甲路 or 乙路 — derived
// from the plan column / rationale tags the recording convention already
// writes. Two recorded shapes exist: the older rounds put the path in
// the plan column itself （甲路·反弹空 / 乙路·破位回踩空， which planOf
// returns verbatim), while the round-7 altcoin batch shares one plan
// label （第7轮山寨纸面） across both legs and writes the path only in
// the rationale's leading tag （第7轮山寨纸面·甲空：… / ·乙多：…) —
// which is why those legs were indistinguishable on the card. The
// round-8 batch writes the same tag WITH the 路 （·甲路空： /
// ·乙路多：), so the tag match accepts both spellings. Returns
// null for legs that are not part of a dual-path pair (single calls,
// watch calls), which then carry no path badge.
function pathOf(t: Trade): "甲路" | "乙路" | null {
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  const p = planOf(t);
  if (p.startsWith("甲路")) return "甲路";
  if (p.startsWith("乙路")) return "乙路";
  const tag = t.rationale.match(/·(甲|乙)(?:路)?[空多]/);
  if (tag && tag[1]) return tag[1] === "甲" ? "甲路" : "乙路";
  if (t.rationale.includes("与甲路同一单")) return "乙路";
  return null;
}

// The 甲路 / 乙路 badge: a solid, bold marker at the head of every
// dual-path leg, so the two legs of one coin read as two different
// plans at a glance instead of two identical-looking cards told apart
// only by their prices. Renders nothing for non-dual-path legs.
function PathBadge({ t }: { t: Trade }) {
  const path = pathOf(t);
  if (!path) return null;
  return (
    <span className="inline-block shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded bg-[var(--accent)] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-black">
      {path}
    </span>
  );
}

// The plan chip that says which playbook a call runs under — the label the
// grouped card carries on every leg, so two legs of the same coin (e.g. a
// 甲路 rebound leg and an 乙路 breakout leg) read as two plans of one
// coin, not as two separate coins.
function PlanChip({ t, full = false }: { t: Trade; full?: boolean }) {
  if (t.direction === "watch") return null;
  const label = `${planOf(t)}${roundOf(t) ? ` · ${roundOf(t)}` : ""}`;
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-block min-w-0 truncate rounded bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]"
      title={label}
    >
      {full ? label : shortPlanLabel(t)}
    </span>
  );
}

function StatusChip({
  t,
  conditionMet = false,
  dataStatus = null,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  // 乙路 only: whether the leg's breakout precondition has happened
  // (from getConditionStates); drives the 条件未满足 / 条件满足·未触发
  // split for open legs.
  conditionMet?: boolean;
  // Whether the server could actually verify the leg's candles
  // (getConditionStates' data_status, P2-11): when the leg reads
  // cond_unmet only because the scan had no usable data, the chip
  // says 行情暂不可核实 instead of 条件未满足.
  dataStatus?: "ok" | "unavailable" | "insufficient_history" | null;
}) {
  const r = tradeR(t);
  if (t.status === "reviewed") {
    // An interim review that found the leg triggered and still
    // running: the position exists and is under observation — it is
    // not settled yet, whatever the status column says.
    if (t.direction !== "watch" && isStillRunning(t)) {
      // First target already touched and booked: the leg gets its
      // own name and carries the R that target banked (gross, from
      // stored prices) instead of the interim floating R. A leg
      // still running short of TP1 keeps 已触发·待观察 + floating R
      // exactly as before.
      const booked = tp1Booked(t);
      const shownR = booked ? tp1BookedR(t) : r;
      return (
        <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border border-[var(--accent)] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
          {booked ? "已触发第一止盈·待观察" : "已触发·待观察"}
          {shownR != null && (
            <span className={mono}>
              {" "}
              {shownR >= 0 ? "+" : ""}
              {shownR.toFixed(2)}R
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
      );
    }
    // Watch calls are judgments, not orders — they keep the verdict
    // chip they have always had.
    if (t.direction === "watch") {
      const vm = verdictMeta(t.verdict);
      return (
        <span
          className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${vm.cls}`}
        >
          {vm.label}
        </span>
      );
    }
    // A leg that was voided before it ever opened reads 已作废
    // (user, 2026-11-17): same yellow accent family as the plain
    // 未触发 chip below — the voiding is a note-level distinction
    // over the shared no_trigger verdict (see isVoided).
    if (isVoided(t)) {
      return (
        <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)] border-[var(--accent)]">
          已作废
        </span>
      );
    }
    // A leg that never triggered never opened a position, so
    // there is nothing to settle (user: 那甲路就没了就不用写结算
    // 了) — it reads 未触发 only, never 已结算.
    if (t.verdict === "no_trigger") {
      const vm = verdictMeta(t.verdict);
      return (
        <span
          className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${vm.cls}`}
        >
          {vm.label}
        </span>
      );
    }
    // A completed order reads 已结算 first (user's naming); the
    // verdict and the 盈/亏 mark follow in the same chip — and
    // nothing else: the per-leg R that used to trail the mark is
    // deleted (user, 2026-11-16: 盈旁边那个 +0.88R 显示没意义了
    // 删掉 — for a TP1-then-stop leg that figure was the 算法一
    // split, which matches neither the coin cumulative's whole-TP1
    // R nor the top total's −1R, so the chip carries no R at all
    // now, for every settled leg alike). R still lives where it is
    // a sum with one stated caliber: the card header's per-coin
    // 累计 and the scoreboard's top 累计 R.
    const vm = verdictMeta(t.verdict);
    const mark = pnlMark(t);
    const verdictCls =
      t.verdict === "correct"
        ? "text-[var(--long)]"
        : t.verdict === "wrong"
          ? "text-[var(--short)]"
          : "text-[var(--accent)]";
    return (
      <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border border-[var(--border)] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
        已结算
        <span className={verdictCls}> · {vm.label}</span>
        {mark && <span className={mark.cls}> · {mark.label}</span>}
      </span>
    );
  }
  // Open watch calls are not orders either: 待复盘 as before.
  if (t.direction === "watch") {
    return (
      <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border border-[var(--border)] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--dim)]">
        待复盘
      </span>
    );
  }
  const state = legState(t, conditionMet);
  const cls =
    state === "cond_unmet"
      ? "border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--dim)]"
      : state === "observing"
        ? "border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--text)]"
        : "border-[var(--accent)] text-[var(--accent)]";
  // A cond_unmet that rests on missing / short candle data is not a
  // verified "condition not met" (P2-11): say so instead.
  const label =
    state === "cond_unmet" &&
    (dataStatus === "unavailable" || dataStatus === "insufficient_history")
      ? "行情暂不可核实"
      : LEG_STATE_LABEL[state];
  return (
    <span
      className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${cls}`}
    >
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}

function PriceTriplet({
  t,
  preTrigger = false,
  tp1Grayed = false,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  // An 乙路 leg whose breakout precondition has not happened yet
  // (see levelsHidden): only its trigger price is drawn — its stop
  // and targets belong to a plan whose first condition has not even
  // been met (user: 就乙的那个条件没满足就不要写). Every other leg,
  // untriggered ones included, draws the full triplet.
  preTrigger?: boolean;
  // The leg has touched its first take-profit and the touch is
  // booked, still running (tp1Grayed — the same test that grays
  // the TP1 line on the data bar): the first target's NUMBER
  // prints in the --tp-passed gray, and stays gray whatever the
  // live price does until final settlement. On a merged
  // dual-target figure only that first number grays; the slash
  // and the second target keep their colors.
  tp1Grayed?: boolean;
}) {
  const watch = t.direction === "watch";
  if (preTrigger && !watch) {
    return (
      <div className={`text-sm ${mono}`}>
        <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">触发价</div>
        <div className="text-[var(--text)]">{fmtPrice(t.entry_price)}</div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  // Two targets print as ONE 止盈 value joined by a slash (user: no
  // separate 止盈1 / 止盈2 labels) — the row stays three columns
  // either way, and a single-target leg shows its lone target.
  const tp2 = !watch && t.take_profit2 != null ? t.take_profit2 : null;
  return (
    <div className={`grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-sm ${mono}`}>
      <div>
        <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {watch ? "判断时价" : "入场"}
        </div>
        <div className="text-[var(--text)]">{fmtPrice(t.entry_price)}</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {watch ? "区间下沿" : "止损"}
        </div>
        <div className="text-[var(--short)]">{fmtPrice(t.stop_loss)}</div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {watch ? "区间上沿" : "止盈"}
        </div>
        <div className="whitespace-nowrap text-[var(--long)]">
          <span
            style={
              tp1Grayed ? { color: "var(--tp-passed)" } : undefined
            }
          >
            {fmtPrice(t.take_profit)}
          </span>
          {tp2 != null && <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">/</span>}
          {tp2 != null && fmtPrice(tp2)}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Where the live price sits between the stop and the target (for a watch
// call: between the range's lower and upper bounds). The bar is always a
// price axis ascending left → right, whichever direction the call is: the
// low end carries whichever level sits lower (for a short that is the
// take-profit, since a short's target is below its stop), the high end the
// other one, and the marker moves right as price rises — the same reading
// direction for longs and shorts alike.
// Position legs get a SINGLE-hue treatment (user correction: the old
// green→gold→red multi-color track read as dark and muddy). One
// direction color only, decided by which side of the entry price the
// live price sits on: profit side → bright green family, loss side →
// bright red family (the vivid --bar-profit / --bar-loss tokens, kept
// bright in both color schemes). How deep the color runs is set by the
// floating R — the signed price move measured in stop distances: at
// 0R the color is at its lightest, it deepens toward +1R (green) or
// −1R (red), and anything beyond ±1R is capped at full depth. The base
// track stays a light neutral; the colored segment starts at the price
// marker — where the color is deepest — and fades out in a FIXED
// direction set by the call direction alone, never by profit or loss:
// a long colors only the marker's left side, fading leftward; a short
// mirrors that, coloring only the marker's right side, fading
// rightward. Profit and loss only swap the hue (green / red) and its
// depth, never the fade direction. The marker dot follows exactly the same hue
// and depth, so line and segment always read as one color. Under the
// bar, each end's level prints in its own color (止盈 green / 止损 red)
// with the signed distance from the current price beside it; a leg
// with two targets slashes both target prices and distances together
// at the target end instead of printing a third middle group. The
// entry price carries a small yellow (accent) dot on the track; the
// stop and take-profit levels are vertical lines instead — the stop
// a red line, each take-profit its own green line —
// the first target in a lighter green, the far (second) target in
// the full deep green, so the two targets read apart at a glance.
// These level lines mark plan positions only, so they stay colored
// even in the pre-trigger gray mode, exactly like the entry dot.
// One exception, and only for a leg that has actually entered: once
// the FIRST take-profit has been touched and booked (tp1Booked),
// that target's line turns gray and the first
// target's price number grays with it — in this bar's label row and
// in the terms rows (PriceTriplet), off the same tp1Grayed test —
// and the gray sticks until final settlement, even if price later
// falls back inside the first target; a leg that has not booked its
// first target never grays, however far its price wanders. The current-price dot
// and the colored segment glide between ticks (the .bar-slide
// transition in theme.css) instead of jumping.
// Which legs get a bar at all is decided in LiveRow: legs in position
// (and watch ranges) draw it in the vivid single-hue treatment; a leg
// that has reached the 未触发 stage (甲路 未触发 / 乙路
// 条件满足·未触发) draws it GRAY (the `pending` prop: gray marker
// dot, gray fading tail — the plan's shape is visible but nothing
// about it is in force yet); an 乙路 leg still at 条件未满足 draws
// no bar at all. A watch range has no entry/profit semantics, so it
// keeps the neutral track and plain labels.
function PositionBar({
  t,
  price,
  pending = false,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  price: number;
  // Pre-trigger gray mode: the marker dot and the fading tail render
  // in neutral gray (--dim) instead of the vivid profit/loss hue —
  // there is no position, so there is no profit or loss to color.
  pending?: boolean;
}) {
  // The band runs from the stop to the FARTHEST target: a leg with a
  // second take-profit spans stop ↔ second target, with a small tick
  // marking where the first target sits inside the band. The far end
  // is labelled plainly 止盈 and slashes both targets together (see
  // the label row below). Single-target legs span stop ↔ 止盈 exactly
  // as before.
  const tpFar = t.take_profit2 ?? t.take_profit;
  const lo = Math.min(t.stop_loss, tpFar);
  const hi = Math.max(t.stop_loss, tpFar);
  const frac =
    hi > lo ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (price - lo) / (hi - lo))) : 0.5;
  const watch = t.direction === "watch";
  const tpEndLabel = "止盈";
  const loLabel = watch ? "下沿" : t.stop_loss < tpFar ? "止损" : tpEndLabel;
  const hiLabel = watch ? "上沿" : t.stop_loss < tpFar ? tpEndLabel : "止损";
  const loIsTp = !watch && tpFar < t.stop_loss;
  const tp1Frac =
    !watch && t.take_profit2 != null && hi > lo
      ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (t.take_profit - lo) / (hi - lo)))
      : null;
  // Where the entry price sits inside the band — it gets its own
  // small yellow dot on the track (user request), so the entry reads
  // at a glance next to the moving price marker. Watch ranges have
  // no entry to mark.
  const entryFrac =
    !watch && hi > lo
      ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (t.entry_price - lo) / (hi - lo)))
      : null;
  // Floating R: signed move from the entry, in stop distances.
  // Positive = profit side of the entry, negative = loss side.
  const dirSign = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  const risk = Math.abs(t.entry_price - t.stop_loss);
  const floatR =
    !watch && risk > 0 ? (dirSign * (price - t.entry_price)) / risk : 0;
  const vivid =
    floatR >= 0 ? "var(--bar-profit)" : "var(--bar-loss)";
  // Depth interpolation over ±1R, capped: 0R → lightest (35% vivid
  // mixed into the surface), ±1R and beyond → full vivid.
  const depth = Math.min(1, Math.abs(floatR));
  const vividPct = Math.round(35 + depth * 65);
  // First-target graying (user, 2026-11-15; numbers added and the
  // condition changed to the booked test, 2026-11-16): once a leg's
  // FIRST take-profit has been touched and booked while it is still
  // running (tp1Grayed = tp1Booked), that target's line turns gray
  // AND so does the first target's price number in the label row
  // below — the card-face and history terms gray their TP1 number
  // off the same test. The gray does NOT follow the live price:
  // price falling back inside the first target restores nothing;
  // the gray holds until the leg's final settlement. The gray is
  // the dedicated --tp-passed token — a medium-dark neutral gray
  // (user, 2026-11-16: the old --dim gray was too pale to tell
  // apart from the light-green TP1 line at a glance). A leg that
  // has not booked TP1 (pre-trigger legs included) NEVER grays —
  // its price wandering past the target means nothing until the
  // booking lands. On a dual-target leg the line at the first
  // target is the inner light tick; on a single-target leg the end
  // line IS the first target, so it grays instead. The far
  // (second) target line never grays here.
  const tp1Passed = tp1Grayed(t);
  const deepColor = pending
    ? "var(--dim)"
    : watch
      ? "var(--text)"
      : `color-mix(in srgb, ${vivid} ${vividPct}%, var(--surface))`;
  const barColor = deepColor;
  // Fixed fade direction by call direction only (user correction):
  // long → segment covers the marker's LEFT side only, deepest at the
  // marker (its right edge) and fading to transparent at the bar's
  // left end; short → mirrored, segment covers the marker's RIGHT
  // side only, deepest at the marker (its left edge) and fading to
  // transparent at the bar's right end. Profit/loss never flips this.
  const isLong = t.direction === "long";
  const segmentStyle = watch
    ? undefined
    : {
        left: isLong ? "0%" : `${frac * 100}%`,
        width: isLong ? `${frac * 100}%` : `${(1 - frac) * 100}%`,
        background: isLong
          ? `linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, ${barColor})`
          : `linear-gradient(90deg, ${barColor}, transparent)`,
      };
  // Label row under the bar: exactly TWO end groups, nothing in the
  // middle (user: the old three-group row — stop / first target /
  // far target, each with its own price + distance — wrapped and
  // read as clutter on a phone). The target end slashes both targets
  // together under the one 止盈 label, prices and distances alike
  // (止盈 0.30 / 0.312 · +0.00% / +4.00%); the stop end and watch
  // ends keep their single level + distance. Every group is nowrap
  // so a label or number never breaks mid-word.
  const pctText = (level: number) => {
    const d = ((level - price) / price) * 100;
    return `${d > 0 ? "+" : ""}${d.toFixed(2)}%`;
  };
  const endSpan = (isLo: boolean) => {
    const level = isLo ? lo : hi;
    const label = isLo ? loLabel : hiLabel;
    const isTp = !watch && (isLo ? loIsTp : !loIsTp);
    const tp2v = t.take_profit2;
    const dual = isTp && tp2v != null;
    return (
      <span className="inline-flex items-baseline gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap">
        <span className="font-sans text-[var(--dim)]">{label}</span>
        <span
          className="font-semibold"
          style={{
            color: watch
              ? "var(--text)"
              : isTp
                ? "var(--long)"
                : "var(--short)",
```
