          }}
        >
          {dual ? (
            <>
              <span
                style={
                  tp1Passed ? { color: "var(--tp-passed)" } : undefined
                }
              >
                {fmtPrice(t.take_profit)}
              </span>
              /{fmtPrice(tp2v)}
            </>
          ) : isTp && tp1Passed ? (
            <span style={{ color: "var(--tp-passed)" }}>
              {fmtPrice(level)}
            </span>
          ) : (
            fmtPrice(level)
          )}
        </span>
        <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">
          {dual
            ? `${pctText(t.take_profit)}/${pctText(tp2v)}`
            : pctText(level)}
        </span>
      </span>
    );
  };
  return (
    <div className="mt-2" aria-hidden="true">
      <div className="relative h-2 rounded-full bg-[var(--border)]">
        {segmentStyle && (
          <div
            className="bar-slide absolute top-0 h-full rounded-full"
            style={segmentStyle}
          />
        )}
        {!watch && (
          <>
            {/* Stop end: red line. Far-target end: deep green line. */}
            <div
              className="absolute top-1/2 h-3.5 w-[2.5px] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full shadow"
              style={{
                left: `${loIsTp ? 100 : 0}%`,
                backgroundColor: "var(--bar-loss)",
              }}
            />
            <div
              className="absolute top-1/2 h-3.5 w-[2.5px] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full shadow"
              style={{
                left: `${loIsTp ? 0 : 100}%`,
                backgroundColor:
                  t.take_profit2 == null && tp1Passed
                    ? "var(--tp-passed)"
                    : "var(--bar-profit)",
              }}
            />
          </>
        )}
        {tp1Frac != null && (
          <div
            className="absolute top-1/2 h-3.5 w-[2.5px] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full shadow"
            style={{
              left: `${tp1Frac * 100}%`,
              backgroundColor: tp1Passed
                ? "var(--tp-passed)"
                : "color-mix(in srgb, var(--bar-profit) 45%, var(--surface))",
            }}
          />
        )}
        {entryFrac != null && (
          <div
            className="absolute top-1/2 h-2 w-2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-[var(--accent)] ring-1 ring-[var(--surface)]"
            style={{ left: `${entryFrac * 100}%` }}
          />
        )}
        {/* Current price: the round marker dot in the bar's hue
            (gray in pending mode), as before. Only the take-profit
            and stop levels are vertical lines. The dot carries the
            bar-slide class so each pushed price glides it to its new
            spot instead of teleporting (theme.css owns the timing). */}
        <div
          className="bar-slide absolute top-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--surface)] shadow"
          style={{ left: `${frac * 100}%`, backgroundColor: barColor }}
        />
      </div>
      <div
        className={`mt-1.5 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] ${mono}`}
      >
        {endSpan(true)}
        {endSpan(false)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// --- Majors live feed (BTC/ETH/SOL) ---
// The browser connects straight to Gate's public futures feed.
// (Switched from OKX on 2026-11-15: the user's phone routes the whole
// okx.com suffix DIRECT for the OKX app, which makes OKX's web feed
// unreachable from the page, and carving the shared web domains back
// to the proxy made the app lag again — the two cannot be split.
// gateio.ws is not in that direct list, so this feed naturally goes
// through the user's proxy node.) Nothing passes through the server
// or any model: the page subscribes to the WebSocket futures.tickers
// channel for <SYMBOL>_USDT (USDT perpetual), reconnects on drop, and
// falls back to REST polling (~5s) per contract whenever that
// contract's push stream is missing or stale (P2-14). Gate's
// futures WebSocket lives on the fx-ws.gateio.ws host — the
// api.gateio.ws/ws/v4/ endpoint is spot-only and rejects the
// futures.tickers channel ("Unknown channel", verified live), so the
// socket uses wss://fx-ws.gateio.ws/v4/ws/usdt while the REST
// fallback uses api.gateio.ws exactly as specified. Display only —
// nothing is written back, and settlement still happens through the
// normal review flow. Altcoin cards run on the same feed through the
// multi-contract variant below (one shared socket for every altcoin);
// their server-polled row remains only as the fallback until the
// socket's first tick lands.
type GateLiveStatus = "connecting" | "live" | "polling" | "offline";
// updatedAt (P2-09, 2026-11-17): when this contract last actually
// RECEIVED a price (a WS tick or a successful REST poll), epoch ms.
// Disconnects and failures keep the old price but never refresh
// updatedAt, so a dead feed's price ages naturally and LiveRow can
// tell fresh from stale (60s threshold).
type GateLiveState = {
  price: number | null;
  status: GateLiveStatus;
  updatedAt: number | null;
};

function useGateLivePrice(contract: string): GateLiveState {
  const [state, setState] = useState<GateLiveState>({
    price: null,
    status: "connecting",
    updatedAt: null,
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    let disposed = false;
    let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
    let reconnectTimer: number | undefined;
    let pollTimer: number | undefined;
    let pingTimer: number | undefined;
    let polling = false;
    let hasPrice = false;
    // Per-contract liveness (P2-14): when this contract last got a
    // WS tick, and the newest data instant applied from either
    // source. The REST loop NEVER stops for good any more — it runs
    // for the hook's whole life, but each round skips the fetch
    // while the socket's ticks are fresh (inside PRICE_STALE_MS),
    // so a socket that stays open yet goes silent on this contract
    // hands the price back to REST once its last tick ages out.
    let lastWsAt: number | null = null;
    let latestAt: number | null = null;

    const stopPolling = () => {
      polling = false;
      if (pollTimer != null) {
        window.clearInterval(pollTimer);
        pollTimer = undefined;
      }
    };
    const startPolling = () => {
      if (polling || disposed) return;
      polling = true;
      setState((s) =>
        s.status === "live" || s.status === "connecting"
          ? s
          : { ...s, status: "polling" },
      );
      const tick = async () => {
        // The socket owns the price while its ticks are fresh —
        // REST does not even fetch, let alone overwrite.
        if (lastWsAt != null && Date.now() - lastWsAt <= PRICE_STALE_MS) {
          return;
        }
        // The snapshot's as-of instant is the moment the request is
        // SENT (the ticker rows carry no usable timestamp of their
        // own). Monotonic merge (P2-09 race): if a WS tick landed
        // while this request was in flight, latestAt is newer than
        // t0 and the late snapshot must neither overwrite the price
        // nor refresh updatedAt over it. This guard is also what
        // preserves the old "while live, REST never overwrites the
        // socket's price" behaviour — it is now enforced by data
        // instant instead of by arrival order.
        const t0 = Date.now();
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            "https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/tickers?contract=" +
              contract,
          );
          const j = (await res.json()) as { last?: string }[];
          const last = Number(Array.isArray(j) ? j[0]?.last : undefined);
          if (disposed) return;
          if (Number.isFinite(last) && last > 0) {
            if (latestAt != null && latestAt > t0) return;
            hasPrice = true;
            latestAt = t0;
            setState({ price: last, status: "polling", updatedAt: t0 });
          } else if (!hasPrice) {
            setState((s) => ({ price: null, status: "offline", updatedAt: s.updatedAt }));
          }
        } catch {
          if (!disposed && !hasPrice) {
            setState((s) => ({ price: null, status: "offline", updatedAt: s.updatedAt }));
          }
        }
      };
      void tick();
      pollTimer = window.setInterval(() => void tick(), 5000);
    };

    const connect = () => {
      if (disposed) return;
      try {
        ws = new WebSocket("wss://fx-ws.gateio.ws/v4/ws/usdt");
      } catch {
        startPolling();
        reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(connect, 3000);
        return;
      }
      ws.onopen = () => {
        try {
          ws?.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              time: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
              channel: "futures.tickers",
              event: "subscribe",
              payload: [contract],
            }),
          );
        } catch {
          // A failed subscribe surfaces via onclose and reconnects.
        }
        // Gate expects a futures.ping heartbeat to keep the socket
        // open; send one every 20s.
        pingTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
          try {
            ws?.send(
              JSON.stringify({
                time: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
                channel: "futures.ping",
              }),
            );
          } catch {
            // Ignore — onclose handles the reconnect.
          }
        }, 20000);
      };
      ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
        try {
          const msg = JSON.parse(String(ev.data)) as {
            channel?: string;
            event?: string;
            result?: { last?: string }[] | { status?: string };
          };
          // Only futures.tickers updates carry a price: the subscribe
          // ack (result {status}) and pong frames carry none.
          if (msg.channel !== "futures.tickers" || msg.event !== "update") {
            return;
          }
          const rows = Array.isArray(msg.result) ? msg.result : [];
          const last = Number(rows[0]?.last);
          if (Number.isFinite(last) && last > 0) {
            // A WS tick is by definition the newest data: it
            // overwrites unconditionally and stamps both liveness
            // clocks. Polling is NOT stopped (P2-14) — the loop
            // keeps running and simply skips its fetch while ticks
            // stay fresh, so it is already there if they stop.
            const now = Date.now();
            lastWsAt = now;
            latestAt = now;
            hasPrice = true;
            setState({ price: last, status: "live", updatedAt: now });
          }
        } catch {
          // Non-JSON frames carry no price — ignore.
        }
      };
      ws.onerror = () => {
        try {
          ws?.close();
        } catch {
          // Already closing.
        }
      };
      ws.onclose = () => {
        if (pingTimer != null) {
          window.clearInterval(pingTimer);
          pingTimer = undefined;
        }
        if (disposed) return;
        // The price survives the drop; its updatedAt does NOT move,
        // so it ages toward stale while the socket is down (P2-09).
        // The dead socket's ticks no longer count as freshness
        // (P2-14): clearing lastWsAt makes the already-running REST
        // loop eligible again on its very next round.
        lastWsAt = null;
        setState((s) => ({
          price: s.price,
          status: s.price != null ? "polling" : "connecting",
          updatedAt: s.updatedAt,
        }));
        startPolling();
        reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(connect, 3000);
      };
    };
    connect();
    // Polling runs from the start (P2-14): its rounds fetch only
    // while the socket has delivered nothing fresh, so a socket
    // that never delivers — or later goes silent — never leaves
    // the price blank or frozen.
    startPolling();
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      stopPolling();
      if (reconnectTimer != null) window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      if (pingTimer != null) window.clearInterval(pingTimer);
      try {
        ws?.close();
      } catch {
        // Already closed.
      }
    };
  }, [contract]);
  return state;
}

// Multi-contract variant of the feed above, for the altcoins: ONE
// Gate futures socket subscribed to every altcoin contract at once
// (the majors keep their own per-coin sockets). Same rules — push
// first, heartbeat, auto-reconnect, and a REST fallback (~5s); the
// fallback pulls the all-contracts ticker list in a single request
// and picks out the subscribed symbols. Returns one GateLiveState
// per symbol (keyed by base symbol).
// Liveness is PER CONTRACT (P2-14, 2026-11-17): the old shape let
// any one contract's WS tick mark the whole socket live and stop
// the REST loop for every contract, so a contract Gate never
// pushes (illiquid, delisted-from-push) froze silently at its last
// price. Now each contract carries its own lastWsAt; the REST loop
// never stops, and each round fetches only the contracts that have
// never received a WS tick or whose last tick is older than the
// stale threshold (PRICE_STALE_MS, the standing 60s caliber).
// Each contract's status — and therefore its 实时/轮询 label —
// follows its own stream, never a busier neighbour's.
function useGateLivePrices(
  contracts: string[],
): Record<string, GateLiveState> {
  const key = contracts.join(",");
  const [prices, setPrices] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  // Per-contract last-received timestamps (P2-09): refreshed only
  // when a tick/poll actually delivers that contract's price.
  const [updatedAts, setUpdatedAts] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
  // Per-contract feed status (P2-14): which source currently
  // drives THIS contract's price.
  const [statuses, setStatuses] = useState<Record<string, GateLiveStatus>>(
    {},
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    const list = key ? key.split(",") : [];
    if (list.length === 0) return;
    const wanted = new Set(list);
    let disposed = false;
    let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
    let reconnectTimer: number | undefined;
    let pollTimer: number | undefined;
    let pingTimer: number | undefined;
    // When each contract last received a WS tick (P2-14), and the
    // newest data instant applied per contract from either source
    // (the monotonic-merge reference, P2-09 race). priced tracks
    // which contracts have ever carried a price, for the offline
    // label.
    const lastWsAt = new Map<string, number>();
    const latestAt = new Map<string, number>();
    const priced = new Set<string>();

    // WS pushes are by definition the newest data: overwrite
    // unconditionally, stamp both clocks, mark the contract live.
    const applyWsRows = (rows: { contract?: string; last?: string }[]) => {
      const next: Record<string, number> = {};
      for (const r of rows) {
        if (!r.contract || !wanted.has(r.contract)) continue;
        const last = Number(r.last);
        if (Number.isFinite(last) && last > 0) next[r.contract] = last;
      }
      if (Object.keys(next).length === 0) return;
      const now = Date.now();
      for (const c of Object.keys(next)) {
        lastWsAt.set(c, now);
        latestAt.set(c, now);
        priced.add(c);
      }
      setPrices((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...next }));
      setUpdatedAts((prev) => {
        const stamps = { ...prev };
        for (const c of Object.keys(next)) stamps[c] = now;
        return stamps;
      });
      setStatuses((prev) => {
        const s = { ...prev };
        for (const c of Object.keys(next)) s[c] = "live";
        return s;
      });
    };
    // REST snapshot merge with the monotonic guard (P2-09 race):
    // asOf is the instant the request was SENT (the ticker rows
    // carry no usable timestamp of their own, so the send instant
    // is the snapshot's as-of). A contract whose applied data is
    // newer than asOf — a WS tick landed while the request was in
    // flight — is skipped entirely: the late snapshot neither
    // overwrites its price nor refreshes its updatedAt. Applied
    // rows stamp updatedAt = asOf, the snapshot's own instant.
    // Only the contracts the round selected (WS-missing or
    // WS-stale) are eligible at all; WS-fresh contracts are never
    // touched by REST.
    const applyRestRows = (
      rows: { contract?: string; last?: string }[],
      asOf: number,
      only: Set<string>,
    ) => {
      const next: Record<string, number> = {};
      for (const r of rows) {
        if (!r.contract || !only.has(r.contract)) continue;
        const last = Number(r.last);
        if (!(Number.isFinite(last) && last > 0)) continue;
        const cur = latestAt.get(r.contract);
        if (cur != null && cur > asOf) continue;
        next[r.contract] = last;
      }
      if (Object.keys(next).length === 0) return;
      for (const c of Object.keys(next)) {
        latestAt.set(c, asOf);
        priced.add(c);
      }
      setPrices((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...next }));
      setUpdatedAts((prev) => {
        const stamps = { ...prev };
        for (const c of Object.keys(next)) stamps[c] = asOf;
        return stamps;
      });
      setStatuses((prev) => {
        const s = { ...prev };
        for (const c of Object.keys(next)) s[c] = "polling";
        return s;
      });
    };
    // A poll round that could not price a never-priced contract
    // marks that contract offline （重试中）; a contract that
    // already has a price keeps it (and its status) and simply
    // ages toward the stale marker.
    const markUnpricedOffline = (cs: string[]) => {
      setStatuses((prev) => {
        let changed = false;
        const s = { ...prev };
        for (const c of cs) {
          if (!priced.has(c) && s[c] !== "offline") {
            s[c] = "offline";
            changed = true;
          }
        }
        return changed ? s : prev;
      });
    };
    const stopPolling = () => {
      if (pollTimer != null) {
        window.clearInterval(pollTimer);
        pollTimer = undefined;
      }
    };
    const startPolling = () => {
      if (pollTimer != null || disposed) return;
      const tick = async () => {
        // This round's fetch set (P2-14): contracts that have
        // never received a WS tick, or whose last tick is older
        // than the stale threshold. WS-fresh contracts are
        // skipped — the socket owns their price.
        const now0 = Date.now();
        const stale = list.filter((c) => {
          const w = lastWsAt.get(c);
          return w == null || now0 - w > PRICE_STALE_MS;
        });
        if (stale.length === 0) return;
        const t0 = Date.now();
        try {
          const res = await fetch(
            "https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/tickers",
          );
          const j = (await res.json()) as {
            contract?: string;
            last?: string;
          }[];
          if (disposed) return;
          if (Array.isArray(j)) {
            applyRestRows(j, t0, new Set(stale));
          }
          markUnpricedOffline(stale);
        } catch {
          if (!disposed) markUnpricedOffline(stale);
        }
      };
      void tick();
      pollTimer = window.setInterval(() => void tick(), 5000);
    };

    const connect = () => {
      if (disposed) return;
      try {
        ws = new WebSocket("wss://fx-ws.gateio.ws/v4/ws/usdt");
      } catch {
        startPolling();
        reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(connect, 3000);
        return;
      }
      ws.onopen = () => {
        try {
          ws?.send(
            JSON.stringify({
              time: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
              channel: "futures.tickers",
              event: "subscribe",
              payload: list,
            }),
          );
        } catch {
          // A failed subscribe surfaces via onclose and reconnects.
        }
        pingTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
          try {
            ws?.send(
              JSON.stringify({
                time: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
                channel: "futures.ping",
              }),
            );
          } catch {
            // Ignore — onclose handles the reconnect.
          }
        }, 20000);
      };
      ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
        try {
          const msg = JSON.parse(String(ev.data)) as {
            channel?: string;
            event?: string;
            result?:
              | { contract?: string; last?: string }[]
              | { status?: string };
          };
          if (msg.channel !== "futures.tickers" || msg.event !== "update") {
            return;
          }
          const rows = Array.isArray(msg.result) ? msg.result : [];
          // Polling is NOT stopped (P2-14): the loop keeps
          // running and simply finds nothing to fetch while every
          // contract's ticks stay fresh.
          applyWsRows(rows);
        } catch {
          // Non-JSON frames carry no price — ignore.
        }
      };
      ws.onerror = () => {
        try {
          ws?.close();
        } catch {
          // Already closing.
        }
      };
      ws.onclose = () => {
        if (pingTimer != null) {
          window.clearInterval(pingTimer);
          pingTimer = undefined;
        }
        if (disposed) return;
        // The dead socket's ticks no longer count as freshness
        // (P2-14): clearing lastWsAt makes every contract
        // REST-eligible on the next round, and contracts that
        // were live fall back to the polling label until either
        // source delivers again. Prices and their updatedAt
        // survive untouched, so they age honestly (P2-09).
        lastWsAt.clear();
        setStatuses((prev) => {
          const s = { ...prev };
          for (const c of Object.keys(s)) {
            if (s[c] === "live") s[c] = "polling";
          }
          return s;
        });
        startPolling();
        reconnectTimer = window.setTimeout(connect, 3000);
      };
    };
    connect();
    // Polling runs from the start (P2-14): its rounds fetch only
    // the contracts the socket has not kept fresh, so a contract
    // the socket never pushes is priced by REST from the first
    // round on instead of freezing.
    startPolling();
    return () => {
      disposed = true;
      stopPolling();
      if (reconnectTimer != null) window.clearTimeout(reconnectTimer);
      if (pingTimer != null) window.clearInterval(pingTimer);
      try {
        ws?.close();
      } catch {
        // Already closed.
      }
    };
  }, [key]);
  return useMemo(() => {
    const out: Record<string, GateLiveState> = {};
    for (const c of contracts) {
      out[c.replace(/_USDT$/, "")] = {
        price: prices[c] ?? null,
        status: statuses[c] ?? "connecting",
        updatedAt: updatedAts[c] ?? null,
      };
    }
    return out;
  }, [contracts, prices, statuses, updatedAts]);
}

const GATE_LIVE_STATUS_META: Record<
  GateLiveStatus,
  { label: string; dotCls: string }
> = {
  live: { label: "实时", dotCls: "bg-[var(--long)] animate-pulse" },
  polling: { label: "轮询", dotCls: "bg-[var(--accent)]" },
  connecting: { label: "连接中", dotCls: "bg-[var(--dim)] animate-pulse" },
  offline: { label: "重试中", dotCls: "bg-[var(--short)]" },
};

// Leverage and position sizing for the majors' live rows follow the
// user's feishu-trader automated-trading capital plan (display-only
// derivation, applied to OPEN legs only — settled legs are historical
// records and keep their original labels):
//  · Margin: 100U capital — BTC/ETH take the majors tier ×40% = 40U,
//    SOL takes the altcoin tier ×30% = 30U.
//  · Leverage: candidates from high to low (BTC/ETH [100, 75, 50, 40,
//    30], SOL [30, 20, 10]); pick the first whose estimated
//    liquidation price sits beyond the stop (MMR = 0.005): long
//    liq = entry × (1 − 1/lev + 0.005) must be below the stop; short
//    liq = entry × (1 + 1/lev − 0.005) must be above it. If no tier
//    passes, fall back to the lowest tier.
//  · Contracts: OKX swap face values — BTC 0.01, ETH 0.1, SOL 1 per
//    contract — so contracts = floor(margin × lev ÷ (face × entry))
//    and the notional position = contracts × face × entry. A budget
//    that floors to 0 buys NO contract (P2-23): it is reported as
//    0 / unaffordable, never rounded up to a phantom 1.
//  · Floating P/L is NOT fixed: % = direction × price move % × lev,
//    amount = contracts × face × (price − entry) × direction.
// Nothing here is stored or settled — it is recomputed from the live
// price on every tick, exactly like the floating % beside it.
type MajorSizing = {
  candidates: number[];
  marginU: number;
  contractFace: number;
};
const MAJOR_SIZING: Record<string, MajorSizing> = {
  BTC: { candidates: [100, 75, 50, 40, 30], marginU: 40, contractFace: 0.01 },
  ETH: { candidates: [100, 75, 50, 40, 30], marginU: 40, contractFace: 0.1 },
  SOL: { candidates: [30, 20, 10], marginU: 30, contractFace: 1 },
};
const MAJOR_MMR = 0.005;

// The fixed 10x the settled (10x/50U-era) BTC legs actually ran at —
// their direction labels keep showing it; only open legs use the
// selected tier above. Settled ETH/SOL legs never carried a leverage
// label and still don't.
const BTC_LEVERAGE = 10;

function majorLeverage(
  symbol: string,
  t: {
    direction: Trade["direction"];
    entry_price: number;
    stop_loss: number;
  },
): number | undefined {
  const cfg = MAJOR_SIZING[symbol];
  if (!cfg) return undefined;
  const tiers = cfg.candidates;
  for (const lev of tiers) {
    if (t.direction === "long") {
      const liq = t.entry_price * (1 - 1 / lev + MAJOR_MMR);
      if (liq < t.stop_loss) return lev;
    } else if (t.direction === "short") {
      const liq = t.entry_price * (1 + 1 / lev - MAJOR_MMR);
      if (liq > t.stop_loss) return lev;
    } else {
      return tiers[tiers.length - 1] ?? 30;
    }
  }
  return tiers[tiers.length - 1] ?? 30;
}

// Whether ANY leverage tier passes the liquidation check for this
// leg (P2-12, 2026-11-17) — a display-only warning signal: when no
// tier's estimated liquidation price sits beyond the stop,
// majorLeverage still falls back to the lowest tier and every
// derived figure stays exactly as computed; LiveRow merely adds a
// 「无安全杠杆档」 warning next to them. Undefined when the question
// does not apply (not a major, watch call, missing entry/stop).
function majorLeverageSafe(
  symbol: string,
  t: {
    direction: Trade["direction"];
    entry_price: number;
    stop_loss: number;
  },
): boolean | undefined {
  const cfg = MAJOR_SIZING[symbol];
  if (!cfg) return undefined;
  if (t.direction === "watch") return undefined;
  if (!(t.entry_price > 0) || !(t.stop_loss > 0)) return undefined;
  for (const lev of cfg.candidates) {
    if (t.direction === "long") {
      const liq = t.entry_price * (1 - 1 / lev + MAJOR_MMR);
      if (liq < t.stop_loss) return true;
    } else {
      const liq = t.entry_price * (1 + 1 / lev - MAJOR_MMR);
      if (liq > t.stop_loss) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

// How many contracts the leg's margin tier buys at the selected
// leverage. Returns { contracts, affordable } (P2-23, 2026-11-17):
// when the floored count is below 1 the budget cannot open even a
// single contract (e.g. 40U at 50x against a 2500U per-contract
// notional needs 50U of margin for one) — the old Math.max(1, …)
// floor invented a phantom contract that LiveRow then computed
// floating P/L and R from. Unafordable legs report contracts: 0
// with affordable: false, and LiveRow shows 不足 1 张·不可开仓
// with no position figures at all. Non-major symbols and degenerate
// prices keep the standing 1-contract default.
function majorContracts(
  symbol: string,
  entry: number,
  leverage: number,
): { contracts: number; affordable: boolean } {
  const cfg = MAJOR_SIZING[symbol];
  if (!cfg) return { contracts: 1, affordable: true };
  const perContract = cfg.contractFace * entry;
  if (!(perContract > 0)) return { contracts: 1, affordable: true };
  const floored = Math.floor((cfg.marginU * leverage) / perContract);
  if (floored < 1) return { contracts: 0, affordable: false };
  return { contracts: floored, affordable: true };
}

// Brief up/down flash state for a streaming price: green when the new
// tick is above the previous one, red when below, cleared shortly
// after. Used inline wherever the BTC live price is printed — the
// figure stays in its existing slot and style, it just ticks.
function usePriceFlash(price: number | null): "up" | "down" | null {
  const [flash, setFlash] = useState<"up" | "down" | null>(null);
  const prevRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (price == null) return;
    const prev = prevRef.current;
    prevRef.current = price;
    if (prev != null && price !== prev) {
      setFlash(price > prev ? "up" : "down");
      const id = window.setTimeout(() => setFlash(null), 400);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(id);
    }
  }, [price]);
  return flash;
}

function flashCls(flash: "up" | "down" | null): string {
  return flash === "up"
    ? "text-[var(--long)]"
    : flash === "down"
      ? "text-[var(--short)]"
      : "text-[var(--text)]";
}

// The small inline tag printed right after the BTC live price, in the
// same line and type size as its neighbours: a status dot plus which
// feed is currently driving the number (Gate push, or the REST polling
// fallback while the socket is down).
function GateStatusTag({ status }: { status: GateLiveStatus }) {
  const meta = GATE_LIVE_STATUS_META[status];
  return (
    <span className="ml-1.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
      <span
        aria-hidden="true"
        className={`inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full ${meta.dotCls}`}
      />
      Gate {meta.label}
    </span>
  );
}

// One small line in the card's existing text flow, used where a card
// currently has no open leg (settled face / 观望 placeholder): the
// live Gate price in the same 现价 slot style the open legs use. No
// floating figure — with no position entered there is nothing to mark.
// The line is a nowrap flex row (user, 2026-11-16: 这个问题你还没有
// 解决，还是在下面）: as a plain paragraph the Gate tag still dropped
// below the price whenever a tick widened the figure (a sub-1 price
// gains a digit as it crosses a power-of-ten boundary, and the status
// label itself varies — 实时/轮询/连接中/重试中）, so a screenshot
// could catch it on one line while the live card kept dropping it.
// Now the price cluster and the tag are both shrink-0 on one flex
// line: wrapping is structurally impossible, whatever the tick.
function GatePriceLine({
  live,
  className = "mt-3",
}: {
  live: GateLiveState;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const flash = usePriceFlash(live.price);
  // Same freshness rule as LiveRow (P2-09): an aged price still
  // prints, but carries the stale marker.
  const stale =
    live.price != null && !isPriceFresh(live.price, live.updatedAt);
  return (
    <p className={`${className} flex items-baseline whitespace-nowrap text-sm ${mono}`}>
      <span className="shrink-0 text-[var(--dim)]">
        现价{" "}
        <span
          className={flashCls(flash)}
          style={flash ? { animation: "tick-flash .4s ease-out" } : undefined}
        >
          {live.price != null ? fmtPrice(live.price) : "—"}
        </span>
      </span>
      <span className="shrink-0">
        <GateStatusTag status={live.status} />
      </span>
      {stale && (
        <span className="shrink-0 font-sans text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--warn)]">
          行情过期
        </span>
      )}
    </p>
  );
}

// Freshness threshold for a displayed price (P2-09): a price whose
// last update is older than 60s is stale — still shown, but marked
// 行情过期， and no floating figure is computed from it.
const PRICE_STALE_MS = 60_000;

function isPriceFresh(
  price: number | null | undefined,
  updatedAt: number | null | undefined,
): boolean {
  return (
    price != null &&
    updatedAt != null &&
    Date.now() - updatedAt <= PRICE_STALE_MS
  );
}

// The live row under an open leg: current price + 24h change, and on the
// right the figure that is honest for this kind of call —
//  · immediate call: 浮动, the floating P/L of a position that exists;
//  · pre-trigger call: NO floating figure (the position does not exist
//    until the review rules it triggered) — only its lifecycle state
//    and how far the live price still is from the level that matters
//    （甲路： the trigger price; 乙路： the breakout line while the
//    break has not happened, the entry price once it has);
//  · watch call: 较判断时, how far price has moved since the snapshot.
// When no source can price the symbol, the row says so and names why.
function LiveRow({
  t,
  live,
  failure,
  loading,
  state,
  gateLive,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  live?: LivePriceWithTs;
  failure?: LiveFailure;
  loading?: boolean;
  // The leg's lifecycle state while it has not triggered yet
  // (legState's cond_unmet / cond_met / untriggered); null for legs
  // in position and for watch calls.
  state?: LegState | null;
  // When present, the Gate browser-direct feed drives this row's
  // price in place of the polled one — same slot, same styles; the
  // price ticks with each push. For the majors, a position leg's
  // floating figures follow the feishu-trader capital plan (see
  // majorLeverage/majorContracts): floating % at the selected
  // leverage, floating R against the stop distance, position size as
  // notional U (derived contract count × contract face × entry
  // price), and the floating P/L amount in U (contracts × face ×
  // signed price move). Altcoin legs show their plain floating %
  // off the same pushed price.
  gateLive?: GateLiveState;
}) {
  const gatePrice = gateLive?.price ?? null;
  const flash = usePriceFlash(gatePrice);
  // Price choice by freshness (P2-09): a FRESH browser-direct price
  // wins; else a fresh polled price; else any price on hand (direct
  // first) shown as stale — displayed, but no floating figure is
  // computed from it.
  const gateFresh = isPriceFresh(gatePrice, gateLive?.updatedAt);
  const liveFresh = isPriceFresh(live?.price, live?.updatedAt);
  let price: number | null;
  let priceStale = false;
  if (gateFresh) {
    price = gatePrice;
  } else if (liveFresh) {
    price = live?.price ?? null;
  } else if (gatePrice != null) {
    price = gatePrice;
    priceStale = true;
  } else if (live?.price != null) {
    price = live.price;
    priceStale = true;
  } else {
    price = null;
  }
  if (price == null) {
    if (loading) {
      return (
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">现价加载中…</p>
      );
    }
    return (
      <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[var(--short)]">
        现价取不到
        {failure
          ? `：${failure.errors.join("；")}`
          : "：行情源暂未返回这个币的价格"}
      </p>
    );
  }
  const watch = t.direction === "watch";
  const isCond = state != null;
  let right: ReactNode;
  if (watch) {
    const vs = ((price - t.entry_price) / t.entry_price) * 100;
    right = (
      <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">
        较判断时{" "}
        <span className={`font-semibold ${pctCls(vs)}`}>{fmtPct(vs)}</span>
      </span>
    );
  } else if (isCond) {
    const gap = (Math.abs(t.entry_price - price) / price) * 100;
    if (state === "cond_met") {
      // 乙路 with its break behind it: what remains is the retest of
      // the entry price.
      right = (
        <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
          条件满足 · 未触发 · 离入场价 {gap.toFixed(2)}%
        </span>
      );
    } else if (state === "cond_unmet") {
      // 乙路 still waiting on its break: the distance that matters
      // is to the breakout line, not to the entry.
      const line = breakoutLineOf(t);
      right = (
        <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
          {line != null
            ? `条件未满足 · 离破位线 ${((Math.abs(line - price) / price) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`
            : `条件未满足 · 离触发价 ${gap.toFixed(2)}%`}
        </span>
      );
    } else {
      right = (
        <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
          未触发 · 离触发价 {gap.toFixed(2)}%
        </span>
      );
    }
  } else if (gateLive && !MAJOR_SIZING[t.symbol]) {
    // Altcoin leg on the live feed: the same plain floating figure
    // the polled row always showed (no leverage, no position size —
    // the feishu sizing below is a majors-only caliber), now driven
    // by the pushed price with its flash and Gate tag.
    const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
    const pct = ((s * (price - t.entry_price)) / t.entry_price) * 100;
    right = (
      <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">
        浮动{" "}
        <span className={`font-semibold ${pctCls(pct)}`}>{fmtPct(pct)}</span>
      </span>
    );
  } else if (gateLive) {
    const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
    const lev = majorLeverage(t.symbol, t) ?? 10;
    const face = MAJOR_SIZING[t.symbol]?.contractFace ?? 0.01;
    const pct = ((s * (price - t.entry_price)) / t.entry_price) * lev * 100;
    const risk = Math.abs(t.entry_price - t.stop_loss);
    const floatR = risk > 0 ? (s * (price - t.entry_price)) / risk : null;
    // Same line, next slots: the position's notional value in U and the
    // floating P/L amount in U — both exist only because this leg has
    // actually entered; trigger-pending and watch legs never reach this
    // branch, so they never show a position that does not exist. Sizing
    // follows the feishu-trader capital plan: the symbol's margin tier
    // at the selected leverage, contracts floored (see majorContracts);
    // notional U = contracts × contract face × entry price, and the
    // P/L amount is the notional times the signed price move — not a
    // fixed figure.
    const sizing = majorContracts(t.symbol, t.entry_price, lev);
    if (!sizing.affordable) {
      // P2-23: the margin tier cannot buy even one contract at
      // this leverage — there is no position to float, so the
      // floating zone carries only the notice (the same warn
      // styling as the 无安全杠杆档 warning in the branch below),
      // never figures computed from a phantom contract.
      right = (
        <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--warn)]">
          不足 1 张 · 不可开仓
        </span>
      );
    } else {
      const contracts = sizing.contracts;
      const notionalU = contracts * face * t.entry_price;
      const floatU = contracts * face * (price - t.entry_price) * s;
      right = (
        <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">
          浮动{" "}
          <span className={`font-semibold ${pctCls(pct)}`}>{fmtPct(pct)}</span>
          {floatR != null && (
            <span className={`font-semibold ${pctCls(floatR)}`}>
              {" · "}
              {floatR > 0 ? "+" : ""}
              {floatR.toFixed(2)}R
            </span>
          )}
          <span>
            {" · 持仓 "}
            <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
              {fmtPrice(notionalU)} U
            </span>
          </span>
          {floatU != null && (
            <span className={`font-semibold ${pctCls(floatU)}`}>
              {" · "}
              {floatU > 0 ? "+" : ""}
              {floatU.toFixed(2)} U
            </span>
          )}
          {majorLeverageSafe(t.symbol, t) === false && (
            <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--warn)]">
              {" · 无安全杠杆档"}
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
      );
    }
  } else {
    const s = t.direction === "long" ? 1 : -1;
    const pct = ((s * (price - t.entry_price)) / t.entry_price) * 100;
    right = (
      <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">
        浮动{" "}
        <span className={`font-semibold ${pctCls(pct)}`}>{fmtPct(pct)}</span>
      </span>
    );
  }
  // A stale price (P2-09) is displayed but never computed from:
  // the whole floating zone collapses to the stale marker, whatever
  // figure the branch above derived.
  if (priceStale) {
    right = (
      <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--warn)]">行情过期</span>
    );
  }
  // A pre-trigger leg's bar follows its lifecycle stage (user: 到了
  // 未触发这个时候就可以出那个灰色数据条了): while an 乙路 leg is
  // still 条件未满足 it draws NO bar — its break has not happened and
  // the plan is not on the table yet. Once a leg reaches a 未触发
  // stage （甲路 未触发 / 乙路 条件满足·未触发） the bar appears in
  // its gray form (PositionBar's pending mode: gray dot, gray tail),
  // so the plan's shape is visible without pretending a position
  // exists; the vivid colors return only once the leg has actually
  // triggered. A pre-trigger leg also gets a note when the price has
  // already reached the entry price — and for an 乙路 leg whose
  // break has not happened, that arrival does NOT count as a
  // trigger, which the note says outright (a plain limit order would
  // have filled there; this leg's two-step condition has not been
  // met).
  // For legs in position, the bar only makes sense while the live
  // price is still inside the stop ↔ target band (the band runs to
  // the second target when the leg has one). Once price has run past
  // either end, a marker pinned at the edge would pretend it is still
  // in range — say in words which side it left by instead, and leave
  // the verdict to the review.
  const tpFar = t.take_profit2 ?? t.take_profit;
  const lo = Math.min(t.stop_loss, tpFar);
  const hi = Math.max(t.stop_loss, tpFar);
  let outText: string | null = null;
  if (isCond) {
    const reached =
      t.direction === "long"
        ? price <= t.entry_price
        : price >= t.entry_price;
    if (reached) {
      outText =
        state === "cond_unmet"
          ? "现价已到入场价，但破位条件未满足，不算触发"
          : "现价已到触发价，是否触发等复盘判定";
    }
  } else if (price > hi || price < lo) {
    if (watch) {
      outText =
        price > hi ? "现价已跑出观望区间上沿" : "现价已跑出观望区间下沿";
    } else {
      const tpSide = t.direction === "long" ? price > hi : price < lo;
      outText = tpSide
        ? "现价已越过止盈位，等复盘判定"
        : "现价已越过止损位，等复盘判定";
    }
  }
  return (
    <div>
      <div
        className={`mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-sm ${mono}`}
      >
        <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">
          {/* The 现价 + price + Gate tag cluster never wraps internally
              (same lock as GatePriceLine): only the 24h suffix may
              drop to a second line if a row ever gets that tight. */}
          <span className="whitespace-nowrap">
            现价{" "}
            <span
              className={gateLive ? flashCls(flash) : "text-[var(--text)]"}
              style={
                gateLive && flash
                  ? { animation: "tick-flash .4s ease-out" }
                  : undefined
              }
            >
              {fmtPrice(price)}
            </span>
            {gateLive && <GateStatusTag status={gateLive.status} />}
          </span>
          {priceStale && (
            <span className="ml-1.5 font-sans font-semibold text-[var(--warn)]">
              行情过期
            </span>
          )}
          {live?.change_pct_24h != null && (
            <span> · 24h {fmtPct(live.change_pct_24h)}</span>
          )}
        </span>
        <span className="font-sans">{right}</span>
      </div>
      {outText ? (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--accent)]">
          {outText}
        </p>
      ) : isCond ? (
        state === "cond_unmet" ? null : (
          <PositionBar t={t} price={price} pending />
        )
      ) : (
        <PositionBar t={t} price={price} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// One leg of a coin that is still running, shown on the grouped card's
// face: which plan it runs under, its direction and status, its terms,
// and its live state — everything readable without tapping.
function OpenLegBlock({
  t,
  live,
  failure,
  liveLoading,
  hidePlan = false,
  gateLive,
  conditionMet = false,
  lifecycle,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  live?: LivePriceWithTs;
  failure?: LiveFailure;
  liveLoading?: boolean;
  hidePlan?: boolean;
  gateLive?: GateLiveState;
  // 乙路 only: whether the breakout precondition has happened yet
  // (getConditionStates), driving the leg's lifecycle state.
  conditionMet?: boolean;
  // The leg's lifecycle timestamps (getConditionStates): when its
  // condition was met / when it actually triggered.
  lifecycle?: LegLifecycle;
}) {
  // A trigger-pending call's trigger is only ever settled by the K-line
  // review (see DATA-PLAN), never by the page — while it is untriggered
  // it carries its lifecycle state name （未触发 / 条件未满足 /
  // 条件满足·未触发） and its live row shows distance, never a
  // floating figure for a position that may not exist. Its terms are
  // drawn in full except for an 乙路 leg still at 条件未满足, which
  // shows only its trigger price (levelsHidden).
  const state = legState(t, conditionMet);
  const preTrigger = isPreTriggerState(t, conditionMet);
  const hideLevels = levelsHidden(t, conditionMet);
  // The time line follows the leg's STATE, and its timestamp is
  // when the leg entered that state (user: 开仓时间是这个单子过
  // 了条件、真正触发的那个时候；未触发但满足条件的写挂单中；条
  // 件未满足的写观望中；状态变了，对应的时间也跟着改):
  //  · 观望中 （乙路， precondition not met): placed at opened_at
  //    and watching since — the placement time is the state's time.
  //  · 挂单中 (condition met, not yet triggered): since the state
  //    began — for 乙路 the break candle's close (conditionMetAt),
  //    for 甲路 the placement itself (its order has been live and
  //    waiting since it was placed).
  //  · 开仓 (in position): the real trigger time — a dual-path
  //    leg's first 15m touch of its entry (triggeredAt); an
  //    immediate leg opened at placement, so opened_at stands.
  // 已持有 is likewise measured from the real entry, not from
  // placement.
  const entryAt = lifecycle?.triggeredAt ?? t.triggered_at ?? t.opened_at;
  const pendingSince =
    state === "cond_met"
      ? (lifecycle?.conditionMetAt ?? t.opened_at)
      : t.opened_at;
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {!hidePlan && <PathBadge t={t} />}
        {!hidePlan && <PlanChip t={t} />}
        <DirectionChip
          direction={t.direction}
          leverage={majorLeverage(t.symbol, t)}
        />
        <span className="ml-auto">
          <StatusChip
            t={t}
            conditionMet={conditionMet}
            dataStatus={lifecycle?.dataStatus}
          />
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
        {t.direction === "watch"
          ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 判断 · 判断后已过去 ${heldText(t.opened_at, null)}`
          : t.triggered_at_unverified
            ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单 · 开仓时间待核实`
            : preTrigger
              ? state === "cond_unmet"
                ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单 · 观望中 · 已过去 ${heldText(t.opened_at, null)}`
                : `${fmtTime(pendingSince)} 挂单中 · 已挂单 ${heldText(pendingSince, null)}`
              : `${fmtTime(entryAt)} 开仓 · 已持有 ${heldText(entryAt, null)}`}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3">
        <PriceTriplet
          t={t}
          preTrigger={hideLevels}
          tp1Grayed={tp1Grayed(t)}
        />
      </div>
      <LiveRow
        t={t}
        live={live}
        failure={failure}
        loading={liveLoading}
        state={preTrigger ? state : null}
        gateLive={gateLive}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

// Display labels for the recorded exit reasons (exit_reason, P1-02):
// why the settlement flow closed the leg at its real exit price.
const EXIT_REASON_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  tp1: "第一止盈",
  tp2: "第二止盈",
  sl: "止损",
  expiry: "到期市价",
  manual: "手动平仓",
};

// One leg inside the expanded area: its full plan label, terms, settled
// result when reviewed, the written rationale, and the review itself
// （实际走势 / 思路复盘）. Used for every leg of the coin, running or
// settled, oldest → newest.
function HistoryLegDetail({
  t,
  conditionMet = false,
  live,
  gateLive,
  lifecycle,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  conditionMet?: boolean;
  // The coin's live prices (same pair the card face runs on). The
  // TP1-number graying no longer reads them — it follows the
  // booked test (tp1Grayed) alone — but the props stay part of
  // this row's interface alongside the face's.
  live?: LivePriceWithTs;
  gateLive?: GateLiveState;
  // The leg's lifecycle timestamps (getConditionStates): its real
  // trigger time, so the header time and the 持有 span below run
  // from the actual entry, not the placement.
  lifecycle?: LegLifecycle;
}) {
  const pnlPct = finalPnlPct(t);
  const exitPrice = exitPriceOf(t);
  // An interim review (P2-04 wording): the leg triggered and is
  // still running — its badge already says 已触发·待观察， so this
  // block must not speak finality. finalPnlPct falls back to the
  // review price for such a row, which makes the figure a floating
  // snapshot, not a settlement: the block labels it 暂记浮动 with
  // a 未终局结算 marker and the caption drops every 按…结算
  // wording. Finally settled rows render exactly as before.
  const interim = isStillRunning(t);
  // The settlement caption that rides with 最终盈亏 in the left
  // text column of the settled block below （左文字）. A recorded
  // REAL exit price (exit_price, P1-02) names itself and its
  // reason; the tp1_then_sl case still leads with 算法一 (the
  // figure beside it follows 算法一, ahead of exit_price — see
  // finalPnlPct); without an exit_price the standing first_touch
  // derivation captions as before.
  const settleNote = interim
    ? "未终局结算 · 暂记浮动，终局盈亏以最终复盘为准"
    : t.verdict === "no_trigger"
      ? // No caption: a leg that never opened carries the plain
        // state line （作废 · 没有开仓 / 未触发 · 没有开仓） in
        // place of 最终盈亏 below (user, 2026-11-17) — the old
        // "未触发 · 没有开仓，按 0% 记" caption is gone with it.
        ""
      : touchCase(t) === "tp1_then_sl"
        ? `按算法一结算：75% 按第一止盈价 ${fmtPrice(t.take_profit)} 走 · 余仓按止损价 ${fmtPrice(t.stop_loss)} 结算`
        : t.exit_price != null
          ? `按出场价 ${fmtPrice(t.exit_price)} 结算${t.exit_reason ? `（${EXIT_REASON_LABEL[t.exit_reason] ?? t.exit_reason}）` : ""}`
          : exitPrice != null
            ? `按${t.first_touch === "tp" ? "止盈" : t.first_touch === "sl" ? "止损" : "复盘"}价 ${fmtPrice(exitPrice)} 结算`
            : "";
  const preTrigger = isPreTriggerState(t, conditionMet);
  const hideLevels = levelsHidden(t, conditionMet);
  // Header time follows the same state-time caliber as the card
  // face: a leg that opened reads its real 开仓 time; a leg that
  // never opened (untriggered, settled or still waiting) reads
  // its 置单 time — there is no 开仓 to date it by.
  const entryAt = lifecycle?.triggeredAt ?? t.triggered_at ?? t.opened_at;
  const headerTime =
    t.direction === "watch"
      ? fmtTime(t.opened_at)
      : t.triggered_at_unverified
        ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单 · 开仓时间待核实`
        : t.verdict === "no_trigger" || (t.status === "open" && preTrigger)
          ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单`
          : `${fmtTime(entryAt)} 开仓`;
  return (
    <div className="px-3 py-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{headerTime}</span>
        <PathBadge t={t} />
        <PlanChip t={t} full />
        <DirectionChip
          direction={t.direction}
          leverage={
            t.status === "open"
              ? majorLeverage(t.symbol, t)
              : t.symbol === "BTC"
                ? BTC_LEVERAGE
                : undefined
          }
        />
        <span className="ml-auto">
          <StatusChip
            t={t}
            conditionMet={conditionMet}
            dataStatus={lifecycle?.dataStatus}
          />
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2">
        <PriceTriplet
          t={t}
          preTrigger={hideLevels}
          tp1Grayed={tp1Grayed(t)}
        />
      </div>
      {t.status === "open" && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {preTrigger
            ? `${
                legState(t, conditionMet) === "cond_unmet" &&
                (lifecycle?.dataStatus === "unavailable" ||
                  lifecycle?.dataStatus === "insufficient_history")
                  ? "行情暂不可核实"
                  : LEG_STATE_LABEL[legState(t, conditionMet)]
              }，是否触发由复盘按 K 线判定。`
            : "进行中，当前状态见卡面。"}
        </p>
      )}
      {t.status === "reviewed" && t.direction !== "watch" && (
        // 左文字右止盈止损 (user, 2026-11-16): the settled block is
        // two columns — the text （最终盈亏 + its settlement
        // caption) stacks in the LEFT column and the touch badge
        // sits in the RIGHT column beside it, parallel, exactly
        // like the card face. The badge does NOT drop onto its own
        // line under the text (an intermediate same-day change did
        // that and the user rejected it: 还改错了）.
        <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] items-start gap-x-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            {t.verdict === "no_trigger" ? (
              // Same caliber as the card face (user, 2026-11-17): a
              // leg that never opened shows the plain state line —
              // no 最终盈亏 label and no 0.00%. The voiding reason
              // itself stays readable in 实际走势 below.
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                {isVoided(t) ? "作废 · 没有开仓" : "未触发 · 没有开仓"}
              </p>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div
                  className={`flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-sm ${mono}`}
                >
                  <span className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    {interim ? "暂记浮动" : "最终盈亏"}
                  </span>
                  {pnlPct != null ? (
                    <span className={`font-bold ${pctCls(pnlPct)}`}>
                      {fmtPct(pnlPct)}
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="text-[var(--dim)]">—</span>
                  )}
                  {interim && (
                    <span className="font-sans text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--warn)]">
                      未终局结算
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                {settleNote && (
                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[var(--dim)]">
                    {settleNote}
                  </p>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
          {touchCase(t) != null && <TouchNote t={t} />}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="mt-3">
        <div className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--dim)]">判断依据</div>
        <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-[var(--text)]">
          {t.rationale}
        </p>
      </div>
      {t.status === "reviewed" && (
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
          <div className={`grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-sm ${mono}`}>
            <div>
              <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">复盘价</div>
              <div>{t.review_price != null ? fmtPrice(t.review_price) : "—"}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">窗口最高</div>
              <div>{t.window_high != null ? fmtPrice(t.window_high) : "—"}</div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">窗口最低</div>
              <div>{t.window_low != null ? fmtPrice(t.window_low) : "—"}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          {t.outcome_note && (
            <div>
              <div className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--dim)]">实际走势</div>
              <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-[var(--text)]">
                {t.outcome_note}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
          {t.reasoning_review && (
            <div className="rounded-md bg-[var(--surface)] p-3">
              <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
                思路复盘
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed text-[var(--text)]">
                {t.reasoning_review}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
          {t.reviewed_at && (
            <div className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
              复盘于 {fmtTime(t.reviewed_at)}
              {t.verdict !== "no_trigger" &&
                (interim
                  ? ` · 已持有 ${heldText(entryAt, t.reviewed_at)} · 未终局`
                  : ` · 持有 ${heldText(entryAt, t.reviewed_at)} 后结算`)}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// The touch annotation for a settled leg: which lines the position
// actually touched, in the user's own wording. Placement (user,
// 2026-11-16, final word: 左文字右止盈止损）: EVERYWHERE the badge
// appears it sits in a RIGHT-hand column with the text stacked on
// its LEFT, the two sides parallel — on the card face (right column
// of the face grid, beside 最终盈亏/现价）, in the expanded leg
// detail (right of the 最终盈亏 + settlement-caption text column),
// and in the scoreboard detail windows (the row's right-hand
// verdict/R column). An intermediate same-day change instead
// dropped the detail-view badge onto its own left-aligned line
// under the text; the user rejected that （这个都还没有改，还改错
// 了） and this right-column placement is the correction — do not
// move the badge back to a left/under-text spot.
// Only a finally settled position leg gets one (see touchCase);
// TP1-then-stop legs carry both legs of the 算法一 split with their
// own signed percentages. Rendered as a small tinted pill with a
// colored dot per line, so it reads as a deliberate marker rather
// than loose text (user follow-up: 位置对了，还可以做精致一点) —
// wording, cases and numbers are exactly the plain-text version's.
// The TP1-then-stop case's backing plate is a green→red gradient
// (user follow-up: 碰第一止盈又碰止损的背景板变成渐变的红绿色);
// the single-line cases keep their flat tints. Inside a
// single-line badge the dot + label sit vertically centered and
// left-aligned (user follow-up: 这个框里面居中靠左), and the
// gradient badge's two lines are left-aligned too (user follow-up:
// 然后把框内的文字左对齐) — the badge boxes stay where they were;
// only their contents align left.
// All three cases share ONE box size (user follow-up, 2026-11-16:
// 那个止盈止损框设置跟那个渐变的框一样大，数据也写进去字体和颜色
// 不变): the shell carries the gradient box's footprint — its
// two-line height as min-h and its longest line's width as min-w —
// and the single-line cases write their level's percentage inside
// too (sl_only: the stop's %; both_tp: the full target's %), in
// the same mono span the gradient lines use. Fonts, colors, tints
// and wording are unchanged; a single line centers vertically in
// the full-height box. And NO frame on any of them (user follow-up,
// 2026-11-16: 也框去掉 — 红色的边框）: the single-line cases drop
// their tinted border entirely, keeping only the tinted fill, so
// all three badges read as frameless plates like the gradient box
// already does in light mode.
// The scoreboard detail windows （累计 R / 总判断 / 胜率） reuse this
// same component, so their rows carry the identical annotation
// （标注跟主页的设置一样）.
function TouchNote({ t }: { t: Trade }) {
  const tc = touchCase(t);
  if (tc == null) return null;
  const dot = (color: string) => (
    <span
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full"
      style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
    />
  );
  // One shared footprint for all three cases, matched to the
  // gradient box: min-h-12 is its two-line height (2 × 16px lines
  // + 4px gap + 12px padding), min-w is its longest line
  // （第一止盈 + a two-decimal %) at text-xs. Single-line cases
  // stretch to it via flex centering.
  const shell =
    "min-h-12 min-w-34 shrink-0 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5";
  const shellSingle = `${shell} flex items-center`;
  if (tc === "sl_only") {
    return (
      <div
        className={shellSingle}
        style={{
          backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--short) 7%, transparent)",
        }}
      >
        <span className="flex items-center justify-start gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--short)]">
          {dot("var(--short)")}
          <span>已触止损</span>
          <span className={mono}>{fmtPct(slPctOf(t))}</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (tc === "both_tp") {
    return (
      <div
        className={shellSingle}
        style={{
          backgroundColor: "color-mix(in srgb, var(--long) 7%, transparent)",
        }}
      >
        <span className="flex items-center justify-start gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--long)]">
          {dot("var(--long)")}
          <span>已盈利</span>
          <span className={mono}>{fmtPct(tpFullPctOf(t))}</span>
        </span>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div
      // Palette supplied by the user verbatim (2026-11-16) — the
      // .ake-gradient-box class in theme.css carries the exact CSS:
      // light mode is the plain pale 110deg wash (green 0.07 → white
      // 0.45 → red 0.07), 12px radius, and border: none (frameless,
      // like the single-line cases above); dark mode keeps its
      // translucent rgba gradient frame (border-box: rgba(52,181,116,
      // 0.45) → rgba(180,180,180,0.25) → rgba(239,68,68,0.45)) over a
      // white-0.02 wash. Do not re-tune the numbers.
      className={`${shell} ake-gradient-box space-y-1`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-start gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--long)]">
        {dot("var(--long)")}
        <span>第一止盈</span>
        <span className={mono}>{fmtPct(tp1PctOf(t))}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-start gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--short)]">
        {dot("var(--short)")}
        <span>已触止损</span>
        <span className={mono}>{fmtPct(slPctOf(t))}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// The settled summary shown on a card's face for one leg: when it was
// recorded, its plan label (unless hidden), direction, verdict chip and
// final P/L. The dual-path face rule decides which legs get a block and
// whether the 甲/乙 label shows (see SymbolCard). SymbolCard hands its
// live Gate price line into the LAST settled block as priceLine (the
// v110 way, restored 2026-11-17), so the price shows exactly once, in
// that block's left column.
// Each state keeps its OWN layout (user, 2026-11-17: 每种状态都有
// 它该有的排版 — never borrow one state's rows for another):
//  · Final settled legs render the v102/v110 original grid,
//    restored verbatim (see the settledPosition branch inside):
//    two columns — left [time][最终盈亏][现价], right [verdict
//    chip][touch badge], the touch badge spanning rows 2–3 beside
//    最终盈亏 and 现价. Voided legs reuse that same skeleton with
//    the words swapped （已作废 chip, 作废 · 没有开仓， no badges,
//    no touch note).
//  · Never-triggered legs and watch calls render the two-column
//    grid (user, 2026-11-16): badge row first, the time line,
//    最终盈亏 and the price line stack in the left column; the
//    verdict chip sits at the top right and the touch badge spans
//    the rows beside 最终盈亏/现价， riding up instead of waiting
//    below them （右边的止盈信息可以上去一点）. A compact 持nhnmin
//    holding line briefly sat under the time line; the user
//    removed it the same day （把那个持nhnmin去掉吧不显示） so the
//    rows below move up into its place. The price line belongs in
//    the LEFT column, directly under 最终盈亏 and parallel with
//    the touch badge beside it — the layout of the user's earliest
//    screenshot (user, 2026-11-16: 给你看之前的，还是平行的；其他的
//    不要参考，这是之前的图片）. A full-width price row under the
//    whole block was tried in between and rejected: the line read
//    as dropped below the card instead of sitting in the left
//    stack. The old squeeze failure (a long price pushing the
//    Gate tag onto a second line inside the narrow column) is now
//    closed structurally instead: GatePriceLine is a nowrap flex
//    row whose price cluster and Gate tag are both shrink-0, so
//    the tag cannot drop whatever the tick's width.
function SettledFaceBlock({
  t,
  hidePlan = false,
  priceLine,
  lifecycle,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  hidePlan?: boolean;
  priceLine?: ReactNode;
  // The leg's lifecycle timestamps (getConditionStates): its real
  // trigger time, for the 开仓 line below.
  lifecycle?: LegLifecycle;
}) {
  const pnl = finalPnlPct(t);
  // Same state-time caliber as the open legs' line: a triggered
  // leg reads its real 开仓 time (first 15m touch of entry) and
  // its holding span runs from there to the settlement; a leg
  // that never triggered has no 开仓 and no 结算 to write — only
  // its placement time and 未触发 (user: 那甲路就没了就不用写结
  // 算了；开仓时间是真正触发的那个时候).
  const entryAt = lifecycle?.triggeredAt ?? t.triggered_at ?? t.opened_at;
  const showPnl = t.direction !== "watch" && pnl != null;
  // Card-face header, per state (user, 2026-11-17; voided legs
  // moved onto the settled template the same day, 03:09 — 把那个
  // 已结算的换成已作废）:
  //  · A leg that actually opened and settled （已结算 — the UAI
  //    kind) keeps its ORIGINAL header: the time line stands
  //    first, with the plan/direction chips inline beside the
  //    time text and the 已结算 chip at the top right — no badge
  //    row, no 甲/乙 badge. (A same-day unification briefly gave
  //    this state the badge-row header too; the user reverted
  //    it — this state only.) Its second row is ONE horizontal
  //    flex row: 最终盈亏 on the left, the touch badge on the
  //    right, ends apart （原来那种下面是水平的） — the grid-cell
  //    split that stacked them was the v114 miss.
  //  · A VOIDED leg uses that same settled template with the
  //    words swapped: time line first （置单 time + 置单， no
  //    · 已作废 suffix), direction chip inline, the yellow
  //    已作废 chip at the top right — no 甲/乙 badge, no plan
  //    chip — and the second row carries 最终盈亏 0.00% in the
  //    settled card's P/L row shape, accent-yellow with a medium
  //    (~2px) accent-yellow slash running top-left → bottom-right
  //    over the 0.00 only, the % outside the slash (user,
  //    2026-11-17: 添加斜杠，从左上到右下，不用太粗也不要太细）,
  //    with a yellow ● 待下一轮 box in the touch badge's cell
  //    (user, 2026-11-17).
  //  · Legs that merely never triggered （未触发） and watch
  //    calls keep the unified two-row header: row 1 is the badge
  //    row ([PathBadge][PlanChip][DirectionChip] on the left,
  //    the status chip at the right), row 2 is the time line
  //    standing on its own below the badges.
  // In the badge-row shape the status chip and the touch badge
  // stack in the right-hand column, so the 止盈 badge rides up
  // beside the 最终盈亏 stack instead of waiting below it
  // （右边的止盈信息可以上去一点，整体就没那么多空白了）; the
  // touch badge spans from the pnl row down through the price
  // row, so it stays parallel with the left stack （最终盈亏 and
  // 现价 included) instead of ending above it.
  const settledPosition =
    t.direction !== "watch" && t.verdict !== "no_trigger";
  const voided = isVoided(t);
  // Final settled legs and voided legs return early above on
  // the restored v102/v110 grid; every other reviewed face
  // (never-triggered legs, watch calls, interim snapshots)
  // keeps the badge-row grid below.
  const uaiTemplate = settledPosition || voided;
  const pnlRow = uaiTemplate ? 2 : 3;
  const lastLeftRow = showPnl ? pnlRow : uaiTemplate ? 1 : 2;
  const priceRow = lastLeftRow + 1;
  const timeText =
    t.direction === "watch"
      ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 判断`
      : t.triggered_at_unverified
        ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单 · 开仓时间待核实`
        : t.verdict === "no_trigger"
          ? isVoided(t)
            ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单`
            : `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单 · 未触发`
          : `${fmtTime(entryAt)} 开仓`;
  // VOIDED legs (user, 2026-11-17): the settled template below
  // with the words swapped — the same grid skeleton, only three
  // substitutions: the first row's left cell carries just the
  // 置单 time span and the DirectionChip (no PathBadge, no
  // PlanChip); the second row's left cell carries 最终盈亏 plus a
  // literal 0.00% in accent-yellow, with a ~2px accent-yellow
  // slash over the 0.00 running top-left → bottom-right and the
  // % left outside it;
  // and where the settled card's TouchNote sits, the voided card
  // carries a same-shaped box in the accent-yellow family reading
  // ● 待下一轮 (user, 2026-11-17: 加入一个位置跟uai的已触止损同款
  // 的黄色框，里面标待处理；同日改口为待下一轮） — same grid
  // cell, same row span
  // (rows 2–3 beside 最终盈亏 and 现价）, same shell dimensions
  // and typography as TouchNote's single-line cases, only the
  // palette swapped from red/green to var(--accent). StatusChip
  // already returns the yellow 已作废 chip for these legs. The
  // priceLine slot matches the settled template (left column,
  // row 3).
  if (voided) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] gap-x-3">
        <div
          className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2 self-start"
          style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 1 }}
        >
          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
            {`${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单`}
          </span>
          <DirectionChip
            direction={t.direction}
            leverage={t.symbol === "BTC" ? BTC_LEVERAGE : undefined}
          />
        </div>
        <div
          className="self-start justify-self-end"
          style={{ gridColumn: 2, gridRow: 1 }}
        >
          <StatusChip t={t} />
        </div>
        <div
          className={`mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 self-start text-sm ${mono}`}
          style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 2 }}
        >
          <span className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">最终盈亏</span>
          <span
            className="relative inline-block text-base font-bold"
            style={{ color: "var(--accent)" }}
          >
            0.00
            <span
              aria-hidden="true"
              className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-1/2"
              style={{
                width: "130%",
                height: 2,
                borderRadius: 1,
                backgroundColor: "var(--accent)",
                transform: "translate(-50%, -50%) rotate(18deg)",
              }}
            />
          </span>
          <span
            className="-ml-3 text-base font-bold"
            style={{ color: "var(--accent)" }}
          >
            %
          </span>
        </div>
        <div
          className="mt-1.5 self-start justify-self-end"
          style={{
            gridColumn: 2,
            gridRow: priceLine ? "2 / span 2" : "2 / span 1",
          }}
        >
          <div
            className="min-h-12 min-w-34 shrink-0 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 flex items-center"
            style={{
              backgroundColor:
                "color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 7%, transparent)",
            }}
          >
            <span className="flex items-center justify-start gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)]">
              <span
                aria-hidden="true"
                className="h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: "var(--accent)" }}
              />
              <span>待下一轮</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        {priceLine && (
          <div
            className="min-w-0"
            style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 3 }}
          >
            {priceLine}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
  // FINAL settled legs (direction not watch, verdict correct /
  // wrong, not an interim still-running snapshot): the v102
  // archive original, restored VERBATIM (user, 2026-11-17 — the
  // grid structure is unchanged v102 → v110; do not rewrite,
  // simplify or "equivalently" re-express any of it). Two-column
  // grid: left column [time][最终盈亏][现价], right column
  // [StatusChip][touch badge], and the touch badge's cell spans
  // rows 2–3 when a priceLine rides in the left column, so the
  // badge's top aligns with the 最终盈亏 row and its bottom
  // covers the 现价 row beside it.
  if (settledPosition && !isStillRunning(t)) {
    return (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] gap-x-3">
        <div
          className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2 self-start"
          style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 1 }}
        >
          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
            {t.direction === "watch"
              ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 判断`
              : t.verdict === "no_trigger"
                ? `${fmtTime(t.opened_at)} 置单 · 未触发`
                : `${fmtTime(entryAt)} 开仓`}
          </span>
          {!hidePlan && <PathBadge t={t} />}
          {!hidePlan && <PlanChip t={t} />}
          <DirectionChip
            direction={t.direction}
            leverage={t.symbol === "BTC" ? BTC_LEVERAGE : undefined}
          />
        </div>
        <div
          className="self-start justify-self-end"
          style={{ gridColumn: 2, gridRow: 1 }}
        >
          <StatusChip t={t} />
        </div>
        {showPnl && (
          <>
            <div
              className={`mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 self-start text-sm ${mono}`}
              style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: pnlRow }}
            >
              <span className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">最终盈亏</span>
              <span className={`text-base font-bold ${pctCls(pnl)}`}>
                {fmtPct(pnl)}
              </span>
              {t.verdict === "no_trigger" && (
                <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                  未触发 · 没有开仓，按 0% 记
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <div
              className="mt-1.5 self-start justify-self-end"
              style={{
                gridColumn: 2,
                gridRow: priceLine
                  ? `2 / span ${priceRow - 1}`
                  : `2 / span ${pnlRow - 1}`,
              }}
            >
              <TouchNote t={t} />
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        {priceLine && (
          <div
            className="min-w-0"
            style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: priceRow }}
          >
            {priceLine}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] gap-x-3">
      {uaiTemplate ? (
        <>
          <div
            className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2 self-start"
            style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 1 }}
          >
            <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{timeText}</span>
            {!hidePlan && !voided && <PlanChip t={t} />}
            <DirectionChip
              direction={t.direction}
              leverage={t.symbol === "BTC" ? BTC_LEVERAGE : undefined}
            />
          </div>
          <div
            className="self-start justify-self-end"
            style={{ gridColumn: 2, gridRow: 1 }}
          >
            <StatusChip t={t} />
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div
            className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2 self-start"
            style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 1 }}
          >
            <PathBadge t={t} />
            {!hidePlan && <PlanChip t={t} />}
            <DirectionChip
              direction={t.direction}
              leverage={t.symbol === "BTC" ? BTC_LEVERAGE : undefined}
            />
          </div>
          <div
            className="self-start justify-self-end"
            style={{ gridColumn: 2, gridRow: 1 }}
          >
            <StatusChip t={t} />
          </div>
          <div
            className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]"
            style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: 2 }}
          >
            {timeText}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      {showPnl && uaiTemplate ? (
        // The UAI template's second row (user, 2026-11-17 03:09:
        // 原来那种下面是水平的）: ONE horizontal flex row across
        // the full width — 最终盈亏 on the left, the touch badge
        // on the right, ends apart. (The two-column grid split
        // this row used to take stacked the badge under the P/L
        // instead.) A voided leg swaps the words on the same
        // template: 作废 · 没有开仓 stands where 最终盈亏 stands,
        // with no P/L figure and no touch badge.
        <div
          className="mt-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
          style={{ gridColumn: "1 / -1", gridRow: pnlRow }}
        >
          {voided ? (
            <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">作废 · 没有开仓</span>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div
                className={`flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 text-sm ${mono}`}
              >
                <span className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                  最终盈亏
                </span>
                <span className={`text-base font-bold ${pctCls(pnl)}`}>
                  {fmtPct(pnl)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <TouchNote t={t} />
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : showPnl ? (
        <>
          {t.verdict === "no_trigger" ? (
            // A leg that never opened (simply untriggered — voided
            // legs take the UAI template above) has no final P/L
            // to print (user, 2026-11-17): the pnl grid row
            // carries a plain state line instead of 最终盈亏
            // 0.00% — the yellow 未触发 chip at the top right
            // carries the color. The row still occupies the pnl
            // grid row, so the touch badge's span below is
            // computed exactly as before (TouchNote returns null
            // for such legs anyway). The 0 itself survives only
            // inside the statistics bookkeeping (finalPnlPct),
            // never on the face.
            <div
              className="mt-1 self-start text-xs text-[var(--dim)]"
              style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: pnlRow }}
            >
              {isVoided(t) ? "作废 · 没有开仓" : "未触发 · 没有开仓"}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div
              className={`mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 self-start text-sm ${mono}`}
              style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: pnlRow }}
            >
              <span className="font-sans text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">最终盈亏</span>
              <span className={`text-base font-bold ${pctCls(pnl)}`}>
                {fmtPct(pnl)}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div
            className="mt-1.5 self-start justify-self-end"
            style={{
              gridColumn: 2,
              gridRow: priceLine
                ? `${pnlRow} / span ${priceRow - pnlRow + 1}`
                : `${pnlRow} / span 1`,
            }}
          >
            <TouchNote t={t} />
          </div>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {priceLine && (
        <div
          className="min-w-0"
          style={{ gridColumn: 1, gridRow: priceRow }}
        >
          {priceLine}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// One card per coin. Every leg of the coin lives under this one card:
// legs still running sit on the face, each labelled with the plan it runs
// under （甲路/乙路…), so a coin traded under two plans no longer shows
// up as two separate cards. Expanding the card reveals every leg's
// rationale and the coin's full settled history.
//
// Dual-path face rule (user, 2026-11-14): within the coin's latest round,
// once one of the 甲/乙 legs has triggered, the card face shows only
