// that triggered leg and WITHOUT its 甲/乙 label — which plan fired is
// an internal record (it stays in the expanded history), not something
// the face needs to announce. If neither leg triggered, both legs are
// written on the face. While both legs are still open (trigger not yet
// settled by review), both stay on the face with their labels.
function SymbolCard({
  symbol,
  trades,
  historyTrades,
  live,
  failure,
  liveLoading,
  expanded,
  onToggle,
  winRateMode,
  gateLive,
  account,
  rankBadge,
  conditionById,
  lifecycleById,
}: {
  symbol: string;
  trades: Trade[];
  // This coin's trades across ALL rounds (unfiltered by the round
  // picker). Only the header's 共做了 count reads it — everything
  // else on the card follows the shown (selected round's) trades.
  historyTrades?: Trade[];
  live?: LivePriceWithTs;
  failure?: LiveFailure;
  liveLoading?: boolean;
  expanded: boolean;
  onToggle: () => void;
  winRateMode: WinRateMode;
  // 乙路 legs' breakout-precondition states by trade id
  // (getConditionStates); a missing id reads as 条件未满足.
  conditionById?: Map<number, boolean>;
  // Legs' lifecycle timestamps by trade id (getConditionStates):
  // when each leg's condition was met / when it really triggered.
  lifecycleById?: Map<number, LegLifecycle>;
  // Altcoins only, while a ranking mode is on: this coin's rank and
  // the ranked metric over ALL its trades （整体胜率 or 总 R), shown in
  // the header band next to the summary line.
  rankBadge?: string;
  // Majors only: this coin's independent 100U account state, shown in
  // the header band (undefined for altcoins, which have no account).
  account?: CoinAccount;
  // The browser-direct Gate live feed state for this coin: the
  // majors have their per-coin feeds, altcoins share the one
  // multi-contract feed — either way the card's 现价 ticks the same.
  gateLive?: GateLiveState;
}) {
  const byNewest = [...trades].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      new Date(b.opened_at).getTime() - new Date(a.opened_at).getTime() ||
      b.id - a.id,
  );
  const openLegs = byNewest.filter((t) => t.status === "open");
  const settledLegs = byNewest.filter((t) => t.status === "reviewed");
  const latestSettled = settledLegs[0];
  // Header counts follow the lifecycle names: an interim-reviewed
  // leg that is still running counts as 进行中 （已触发·待观察）,
  // not as settled. The header line reads 「N 笔进行中 · 共做了 M 笔 ·
  // 累计 ±xR」 (user, 2026-11-16), and the old ×N badge next to the
  // symbol is gone. M is the coin's all-history count of trades
  // actually made (user correction, 2026-11-16: 共做了几笔是历史
  // 做过单子的几笔，未触发的不记入) — it is computed over
  // historyTrades (every round), NOT over the round-filtered trades
  // this card shows, so the number stays the coin's lifetime total
  // whichever round is on screen. Only legs that actually opened a
  // position count: a leg the review confirmed triggered (settled
  // or still running), or an open leg already in position
  // (immediate entry). Untriggered legs — settled no_trigger, or
  // still waiting for their trigger — never opened and do not
  // count; watch calls are judgments, not orders, and do not
  // count either.
  // Running count (P2-27, 2026-11-17): only open legs already in
  // position count — a trigger-pending open leg （挂单中/未触发） has
  // not opened yet, the same caliber madeCount below already uses
  // （未触发不算开仓）. Interim still-running legs count as before.
  const runningCount =
    openLegs.filter((t) => !isTriggerPending(t)).length +
    settledLegs.filter(isStillRunning).length;
  const madeCount = (historyTrades ?? trades).filter(
    (t) =>
      t.direction !== "watch" &&
      (isTriggeredLeg(t) || (t.status === "open" && !isTriggerPending(t))),
  ).length;
  // Header figures are computed from the trades this card actually
  // shows (the selected round's calls), so the win rate and 累计 R
  // always match the legs on the card — in the all-rounds view this
  // reproduces the coin's overall figures. （共做了 above is the one
  // exception: it is the all-history count by the user's ruling.)
  const { rate: cardRate, totalR: cardTotalR } = coinFigures(
    trades,
    winRateMode,
  );
  // --- Dual-path face rule, scoped to the coin's newest batch ---
  // A dual-path pair is recorded as one batch: same coin, same round,
  // same recorded instant, two alternative legs （甲/乙）. The pair is
  // recognised by that batch shape (≥2 position legs at one instant,
  // labelled 甲/乙 in the plan or stored as conditional calls) — some
  // batches (e.g. the altcoin rescan pairs) carry no 甲/乙 label in
  // their plan column at all, only in the review text, so the batch
  // shape is the reliable signal. Watch calls never join a pair.
  // A batch is one recorded instant (rounds never share an instant),
  // so the instant alone keys it — sibling legs whose own rationale
  // carries no round tag （同上-style 乙路 rows) still pair correctly.
  // Pairing key (P2-13): rows recorded by recordBatch carry a
  // structured pair_key ("<batch group>|<SYMBOL>") shared by the
  // pair's two legs regardless of their opened_at instants; older
  // rows have pair_key null and keep the opened_at key.
  const batchKeyOf = (t: Trade) => t.pair_key ?? t.opened_at;
  const positionLegs = byNewest.filter((t) => t.direction !== "watch");
  const batchSizes = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const t of positionLegs) {
    batchSizes.set(batchKeyOf(t), (batchSizes.get(batchKeyOf(t)) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  const isPairBatch = (legs: Trade[]) =>
    legs.length >= 2 &&
    legs.some((t) => isDualLeg(t) || t.entry_type === "conditional");
  const newestPos = positionLegs[0];
  const latestBatch = newestPos
    ? positionLegs.filter((t) => batchKeyOf(t) === batchKeyOf(newestPos))
    : [];
  const latestBatchIsPair = isPairBatch(latestBatch);
  const batchTriggered = latestBatch.filter(isTriggeredLeg);
  // Batches (of this coin) in which one leg already triggered: the
  // sibling leg that never fired is void and leaves the face.
  const triggeredBatchKeys = new Set(
    positionLegs
      .filter(
        (t) =>
          isTriggeredLeg(t) && (batchSizes.get(batchKeyOf(t)) ?? 0) >= 2,
      )
      .map((t) => batchKeyOf(t)),
  );
  // Running legs on the face: open legs plus interim-reviewed legs
  // that already triggered and are still in position （已触发·待观察）.
  // A still-running leg's status column says "reviewed", but it has
  // NOT settled — rendering it through the settled face block left it
  // with no live row and no data bar even though the header counted
  // it as 进行中 (the IDOL 甲 / AKE 乙 repair made this visible). It
  // rides the open-leg block instead, which draws the live price, the
  // floating figure and the position bar for a position that exists.
  // The voided sibling of a triggered leg still leaves the face; the
  // triggered leg itself (open legs are never triggered, so this only
  // spares the still-running one) stays.
  const faceOpenLegs = byNewest.filter(
    (t) =>
      (t.status === "open" || isStillRunning(t)) &&
      (t.direction === "watch" ||
        isStillRunning(t) ||
        !triggeredBatchKeys.has(batchKeyOf(t))),
  );
  // Settled face blocks (used when nothing is open): a pair with a
  // triggered leg → only that leg, unlabelled; a pair where neither
  // leg fired → both legs written on the face — except a pair whose
  // BOTH legs are voided, which merges into a single block (see the
  // branch below); anything else → just the latest settled leg as
  // before.
  const faceSettled: { t: Trade; hidePlan: boolean }[] =
    latestBatchIsPair && batchTriggered.length > 0
      ? batchTriggered.map((t) => ({ t, hidePlan: true }))
      : // A pair whose BOTH legs are voided merges into ONE face
        // block (user, 2026-11-17: 两腿合成一块，只留一块）: the two
        // legs share placement time, direction and plan, so the
        // first leg's block stands for the pair. Mixed pairs (one
        // voided, one merely never triggered) do NOT merge — they
        // fall through to the both-no_trigger branch below and
        // keep two blocks. Face rendering only: the expanded
        // history still lists each leg, and no statistic reads
        // faceSettled.
        latestBatchIsPair &&
          latestBatch.length >= 2 &&
          latestBatch.every((t) => isVoided(t))
        ? [{ t: latestBatch[0] as Trade, hidePlan: false }]
        : latestBatchIsPair &&
            latestBatch.every(
              (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "no_trigger",
            )
          ? latestBatch.map((t) => ({ t, hidePlan: false }))
          : latestSettled
            ? [{ t: latestSettled, hidePlan: false }]
            : [];
  // Settled watch calls also belong on the face (user: 观望也标在上面）:
  // a round in which this coin was only watched — or watched alongside
  // a position call — must read as 观望 on the card itself, not only
  // inside the expanded history. Watch legs already on the face (open
  // ones, or the latest settled leg) are not repeated.
  const faceIds = new Set<number>([
    ...faceOpenLegs.map((t) => t.id),
    ...faceSettled.map((f) => f.t.id),
  ]);
  const extraWatchFace = settledLegs.filter(
    (t) => t.direction === "watch" && !faceIds.has(t.id),
  );
  // Every call shown on this card is a watch call — the header line
  // says so outright.
  const allWatch =
    trades.length > 0 && trades.every((t) => t.direction === "watch");
  return (
    <article className="cursor-pointer px-4 py-4" onClick={onToggle}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span
          className={`shrink-0 text-lg font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}
        >
          {symbol}
        </span>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          {runningCount > 0 ? `${runningCount} 笔进行中 · ` : ""}
          {`共做了 ${madeCount} 笔`}
          {allWatch ? " · 观望" : ""}
          {cardRate != null
            ? ` · ${winRateMode === "judgment" ? "胜率" : "盈亏胜率"} ${Math.round(cardRate * 100)}%`
            : ""}
          {cardTotalR !== 0
            ? ` · 累计 ${cardTotalR > 0 ? "+" : ""}${cardTotalR.toFixed(2)}R`
            : ""}
        </span>
        {rankBadge && (
          <span
            className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border border-[var(--accent)] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--accent)] ${mono}`}
            title="按全部轮次的整体数据排名"
          >
            {rankBadge}
          </span>
        )}
        {account && (
          <span
            className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}
            title={`${symbol} 独立账户余额（100U 起，单独结算）`}
          >
            <AccountBalanceBit account={account} />
          </span>
        )}
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={expanded}
          aria-label={
            expanded
              ? `收起 ${symbol} 的判断依据与历史盈亏`
              : `展开 ${symbol} 的判断依据与历史盈亏`
          }
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onToggle();
          }}
          className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center px-1 text-[var(--dim)]"
        >
          <svg
            aria-hidden="true"
            width="14"
            height="14"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            fill="none"
            className={`shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${expanded ? "rotate-90" : ""}`}
          >
            <path
              d="M9 5l7 7-7 7"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="2.5"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeLinejoin="round"
            />
          </svg>
        </button>
      </div>

      {faceOpenLegs.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-4">
          {faceOpenLegs.map((t, i) => (
            <div
              key={t.id}
              className={i > 0 ? "border-t border-[var(--border)] pt-4" : ""}
            >
              <OpenLegBlock
                t={t}
                live={live}
                failure={failure}
                liveLoading={liveLoading}
                gateLive={gateLive}
                conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false}
                lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : faceSettled.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-4">
            {faceSettled.map(({ t, hidePlan }, i) => (
              <div
                key={t.id}
                className={i > 0 ? "border-t border-[var(--border)] pt-4" : ""}
              >
                <SettledFaceBlock
                  t={t}
                  hidePlan={hidePlan}
                  priceLine={
                    i === faceSettled.length - 1 && gateLive ? (
                      <GatePriceLine live={gateLive} className="mt-1" />
                    ) : undefined
                  }
                  lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      ) : null}

      {gateLive && faceOpenLegs.length === 0 && faceSettled.length === 0 && (
        <GatePriceLine live={gateLive} />
      )}

      {extraWatchFace.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--border)] pt-4">
          {extraWatchFace.map((t) => (
            <SettledFaceBlock
              key={t.id}
              t={t}
              lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {expanded && (
        <div className="mt-4" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          <div className="text-xs font-medium text-[var(--dim)]">
            {symbol} 全部记录（新 → 旧）
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 divide-y divide-[var(--border)] rounded-md bg-[var(--surface-alt)]">
            {byNewest.map((t) => (
              <HistoryLegDetail
                key={t.id}
                t={t}
                conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false}
                live={live}
                gateLive={gateLive}
                lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </article>
  );
}

// Placeholder card for a mainstream coin that has no position call in
// the round being viewed (user: 主流币在每一轮都显示，做没做都显示；
// a major that isn't traded is on watch — 主流币不应该在观望吗）. The
// majors section always lists BTC/ETH/SOL; a major with no trade this
// round shows as 观望 (a recorded watch call still renders as a normal
// card with its own 观望 terms — this placeholder is only for a round
// with no record of the coin at all). The card is not expandable —
// there is nothing of this round to expand — but it does say how many
// calls the coin has in other rounds.
function EmptyMajorCard({
  symbol,
  roundLabel,
  allTimeCount,
  gateLive,
  account,
}: {
  symbol: string;
  roundLabel: string | null;
  allTimeCount: number;
  // Majors only: a placeholder majors card also carries the live
  // price line, so the ticking price is visible in rounds with no call.
  gateLive?: GateLiveState;
  // The coin's independent 100U account state, shown in the header
  // band like on a normal card.
  account?: CoinAccount;
}) {
  return (
    <article className="px-4 py-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span
          className={`shrink-0 text-lg font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}
        >
          {symbol}
        </span>
        <span className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border border-[var(--border)] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--dim)]">
          观望
        </span>
        {account && (
          <span
            className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}
            title={`${symbol} 独立账户余额（100U 起，单独结算）`}
          >
            <AccountBalanceBit account={account} />
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[var(--dim)]">
        {roundLabel
          ? `${roundLabel}没有给 ${symbol} 下单——本轮观望，未开仓。`
          : `到现在还没有 ${symbol} 的模拟单记录——一直在观望，从未开仓。`}
        {roundLabel && allTimeCount > 0
          ? `其他轮次共 ${allTimeCount} 笔，切到「全部轮次」可看。`
          : ""}
      </p>
      {gateLive && <GatePriceLine live={gateLive} className="mt-2" />}
    </article>
  );
}

type StatsData = ApiResponse<typeof api, "getStats">;

// One row inside a scoreboard detail window: the trade's identity
// (coin / plan / direction / time), its terms, and — once reviewed —
// its verdict, final P/L % and the R it contributed. Open rows show
// their waiting state instead of inventing a result.
function DetailTradeRow({
  t,
  conditionMet = false,
  totalRView = false,
  winrateView = false,
  pnlView = false,
  lifecycle,
}: {
  t: Trade;
  conditionMet?: boolean;
  // The leg's live lifecycle timestamps (getConditionStates), the
  // same source the card face reads: a triggered leg's 开仓 time
  // resolves lifecycle → stored triggered_at → placement, so the
  // modal's rows print — and the modal's lists sort by — exactly
  // the instant the card shows (user follow-up, 2026-11-16: 甲乙
  // 那里的时间是乱的 — the modal used only the stored value, so a
  // leg whose triggered_at had not been persisted yet fell back to
  // its placement time here while the card already showed the real
  // trigger time, and the two lists disagreed).
  lifecycle?: LegLifecycle;
  // 累计 R window only (user, 2026-11-16): its rows drop the
  // outcome-note detail paragraph （详情说明不用写）.
  // Declutter (user follow-up, 2026-11-16: 那个累计R的页面太杂乱
  // 了…这里只显示有关于r的盈亏单，无关的单子去掉）: a row in this
  // window shows ONLY its R-side facts — identity, 入场/出场， the
  // verdict badge, the R it contributed and its final P/L %. Gone
  // from this window: the side 第一止盈 / 全止盈 / 止损 guidance
  // percentages (and the 按第一止盈/全止盈/止损 sort that keyed on
  // them — the list is plain newest-first now), the 止损/止盈 price
  // entries in the terms line, the touch badge and the TP1-then-
  // stop 75% banked-profit line. The window's totals keep the
  // standing 累计 R caliber; every other window is untouched.
  // Additive extension (user follow-up, 2026-11-16): the 总判断 and
  // 胜率 (all three calibers) windows' rows now ALSO carry the card
  // face's touch annotation (same TouchNote, same cases) plus, for
  // a TP1-then-stop leg, the first take-profit's banked profit at
  // the homepage's 75% caliber — 0.75 × TP1%. In those windows this
  // is purely added content: the outcome-note paragraph, the R and
  // the final P/L % all stay exactly as they were, and no total,
  // rate or caliber changes. (Open rows never get either addition —
  // touchCase is null until a leg is finally settled.)
  // Removal (user follow-up, 2026-11-16): the 胜率 window's rows
  // drop the outcome-note paragraph too （盈亏胜率那里的明细也去掉）,
  // the same treatment as the 累计 R window — badges, the 75% line,
  // the R and the final P/L % all stay. 总判断 rows keep theirs.
  totalRView?: boolean;
  winrateView?: boolean;
  // 盈亏胜率 / 甲胜率 / 乙胜率 views only (user follow-up,
  // 2026-11-16: 还有那个盈亏胜率也是，整理一下，不需要的数据去掉）:
  // the same declutter the 累计 R window got, one caliber over.
  // A row here shows ONLY its win-rate-side facts — identity, the
  // synced 开仓 time, the verdict badge carrying THIS caliber's
  // 盈/亏 mark (winRateMark: a leg that touched TP1 reads 盈 here
  // even when its card-face mark is 亏）, the R this caliber counts
  // (winRateR) and its final P/L %. Gone from these views: the
  // 止损/止盈 price entries in the terms line, the touch badge and
  // the TP1-then-stop 75% banked-profit line (the outcome note was
  // already dropped by winrateView). The 判断胜率 view keeps the
  // winrateView treatment exactly as it was — badge, touch
  // annotation, 75% line and all — and no total, rate or caliber
  // changes anywhere.
  pnlView?: boolean;
}) {
  const r = pnlView ? winRateR(t) : settledR(t);
  // The badge's 盈/亏 mark follows the view's own caliber: inside
  // the win-rate views it is winRateMark (TP1 touched → 盈）; every
  // other window keeps the card-face pnlMark.
  const mark = pnlView
    ? winRateMark(t) != null
      ? {
          label: winRateMark(t) as "盈" | "亏",
          cls:
            winRateMark(t) === "盈"
              ? "text-[var(--long)]"
              : "text-[var(--short)]",
        }
      : null
    : pnlMark(t);
  const pnl = finalPnlPct(t);
  const exit = exitPriceOf(t);
  const watch = t.direction === "watch";
  // Same drawing rule as the card face: only an open 乙路 leg still
  // at 条件未满足 shows its trigger price only — no stop, no targets.
  // Every other leg, untriggered ones included, lists its full terms.
  const hideLevels = levelsHidden(t, conditionMet);
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className={`text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}>
              {t.symbol}
            </span>
            <PathBadge t={t} />
            <PlanChip t={t} />
            <DirectionChip direction={t.direction} />
            <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
              {/* Synced 开仓时间 (user follow-up, 2026-11-16): a
                  triggered position leg shows its REAL open time —
                  the same chain the card face uses (live lifecycle
                  triggeredAt → stored triggered_at → placement) —
                  not the shared placement instant. Untriggered and
                  watch legs never opened, so they keep the
                  placement / judgment time unlabelled. */}
              {isTriggeredLeg(t) && t.direction !== "watch"
                ? `开仓 ${fmtTime(lifecycle?.triggeredAt ?? t.triggered_at ?? t.opened_at)}`
                : fmtTime(t.opened_at)}
              {roundOf(t) ? ` · ${roundOf(t)}` : ""}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}
          >
            <span>
              {watch ? "判断时" : hideLevels ? "触发价" : "入场"}{" "}
              <span className="text-[var(--text)]">{fmtPrice(t.entry_price)}</span>
            </span>
            {!hideLevels && !totalRView && !pnlView && (
              <span>
                {watch ? "下沿" : "止损"}{" "}
                <span className="text-[var(--short)]">{fmtPrice(t.stop_loss)}</span>
              </span>
            )}
            {!hideLevels && !totalRView && !pnlView && (
              <span>
                {watch ? "上沿" : "止盈"}{" "}
                <span className="text-[var(--long)]">
                  {fmtPrice(t.take_profit)}
                  {!watch &&
                    t.take_profit2 != null &&
                    ` / ${fmtPrice(t.take_profit2)}`}
                </span>
              </span>
            )}
            {t.status === "reviewed" && exit != null && (
              <span>
                出场 <span className="text-[var(--text)]">{fmtPrice(exit)}</span>
              </span>
            )}
            {t.status === "open" && (
              <span>
                {watch
                  ? `已过去 ${heldText(t.opened_at, null)}`
                  : LEG_STATE_LABEL[legState(t, conditionMet)]}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          {touchCase(t) === "tp1_then_sl" && !totalRView && !pnlView && (
            <p className={`mt-1.5 text-xs ${mono}`}>
              <span className="font-sans text-[var(--dim)]">
                第一止盈盈利（75% 仓位）
              </span>{" "}
              <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--long)]">
                {fmtPct(0.75 * tp1PctOf(t))}
              </span>
            </p>
          )}
          {t.status === "reviewed" && t.outcome_note && !totalRView && !winrateView && (
            <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[var(--dim)]">
              {t.outcome_note}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="shrink-0 text-right">
          {t.status === "reviewed" ? (
            <>
              <span
                className={`inline-block shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${verdictMeta(t.verdict).cls}`}
              >
                {isVoided(t) ? "已作废" : verdictMeta(t.verdict).label}
                {mark && (
                  <span className={mark.cls}> · {mark.label}</span>
                )}
              </span>
              <>
                {r != null && (
                  <div
                    className={`mt-1.5 text-base font-bold ${mono} ${
                      r > 0
                        ? "text-[var(--long)]"
                        : r < 0
                          ? "text-[var(--short)]"
                          : "text-[var(--dim)]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {r > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                    {r.toFixed(2)}R
                  </div>
                )}
                {pnl != null && (
                  <div className={`text-xs ${mono} ${pctCls(pnl)}`}>
                    {fmtPct(pnl)}
                  </div>
                )}
              </>
              {touchCase(t) != null && !totalRView && !pnlView && (
                // 左文字右止盈止损： the badge stays in this row's
                // right-hand column under the verdict/R figures and
                // hugs the right edge — the row's text （币名/条款）
                // is the left side.
                <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end">
                  <TouchNote t={t} />
                </div>
              )}
              {watch && (
                <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">观望 · 不计 R</div>
              )}
            </>
          ) : (
            // P2-17: the chip must see the same conditionMet /
            // dataStatus the row's own state text uses — without
            // them it defaulted to false and an 乙路 leg whose
            // break had happened read 条件满足·未触发 in the text
            // while its badge said 条件未满足.
            <StatusChip
              t={t}
              conditionMet={conditionMet}
              dataStatus={lifecycle?.dataStatus}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const DETAIL_TITLES: Record<
  DetailKind,
  { title: string; sub: string }
> = {
  totalR: {
    title: "累计 R · 逐笔明细",
    sub: "每一笔已结算仓位单贡献的 R，加起来就是看板上的累计 R（未触发按 0 计、不列明细；观望不计）；累计 R 与胜率同步：摸到过第一止盈的单整笔按第一止盈的 R 计，没摸到的照旧；每笔时间是同步后的真实开仓时间，按时间从新到旧排；每笔只列它贡献的 R 和最终盈亏，不再附止盈止损百分比、触线标注和第一止盈盈利行",
  },
  winrate: {
    title: "胜率 · 逐笔明细",
    sub: "胜率 = 判断对 ÷（判断对 + 判断错）；未触发没开仓，不进胜率；已结算单另带与卡面相同的触线标注，先触第一止盈后回踩止损的另列第一止盈盈利（75% 仓位）；每笔不附实际走势说明",
  },
  total: {
    title: "总判断 · 全部明细",
    sub: "所有记录过的判断，按币种和方案拆开，再附全部逐笔（未触发的不列）；已结算单另带与卡面相同的触线标注，先触第一止盈后回踩止损的另列第一止盈盈利（75% 仓位）",
  },
  open: {
    title: "待复盘 · 逐笔明细",
    sub: "还没复盘结算的每一笔",
  },
};

// A foldable secondary section inside a detail window (user
// follow-up, 2026-11-16: 该收起来就收起来，简洁美观就行了):
// breakdown blocks that support the headline figures （分方案 /
// 分币种） start folded behind their heading so the window opens
// on its main content; tapping the heading expands them in place.
// The per-trade lists themselves are the windows' main content and
// always stay open.
function DetailFold({
  title,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div className="mt-4">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-expanded={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className="flex min-h-8 w-full items-center gap-1 text-left"
      >
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
          {title}
        </span>
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={`text-[var(--dim)] transition-transform ${open ? "rotate-90" : ""}`}
        >
          ›
        </span>
      </button>
      {open && <div className="mt-2">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

// The detail window behind every scoreboard cell. Which cell was
// tapped decides the content: 累计 R lists every settled trade's R so
// the headline number can be re-added by hand; 胜率 shows the
// 对/错 breakdown plus per-plan expectancy; 总判断 shows the full
// ledger split by coin and plan; 待复盘 lists every open call.
function StatsDetailModal({
  kind,
  trades,
  stats,
  onClose,
  winRateMode,
  onWinRateModeChange,
  conditionById,
  lifecycleById,
}: {
  kind: DetailKind;
  trades: Trade[];
  stats: StatsData | undefined;
  onClose: () => void;
  winRateMode: WinRateMode;
  onWinRateModeChange: (m: WinRateMode) => void;
  conditionById?: Map<number, boolean>;
  // Live lifecycle timestamps by trade id (getConditionStates) —
  // the modal's sort and its rows' 开仓 times resolve through the
  // same chain the card face uses, so a leg reads the same instant
  // in both places (see DetailTradeRow's lifecycle prop).
  lifecycleById?: Map<number, LegLifecycle>;
}) {
  const meta =
    kind === "winrate" && (winRateMode === "jia" || winRateMode === "yi")
      ? {
          title: `${winRateMode === "jia" ? "甲" : "乙"}胜率 · 逐笔明细`,
          sub: `只看${winRateMode === "jia" ? "甲路" : "乙路"}这一路的单：大字是这一路的胜率，下面一行是判断胜率（对 ÷（对 + 错）)；胜率口径只管胜率——只要摸到过第一止盈就算盈、按第一止盈的 R 计，没摸到的照旧（亏就减掉），卡面最终盈亏不变；未触发没开仓不计，单路单和观望不属于甲乙、不计入；每笔只列胜率口径的盈亏标记、按第一止盈口径计的 R 和最终盈亏，不带止盈止损价、触线标注和第一止盈盈利行，也不附实际走势说明；逐笔按开仓时间从新到旧排，开仓时间与卡面同步`,
        }
      : kind === "winrate" && winRateMode === "pnl"
        ? {
            title: "盈亏胜率 · 逐笔明细",
            sub: "盈亏胜率 = 盈利笔数 ÷（盈利 + 亏损）；只算真正开仓并已最终结算的仓位单；胜率口径只管胜率——只要摸到过第一止盈就算盈、按第一止盈的 R 计，没摸到的照旧（判断对但最后亏了钱的算亏），卡面最终盈亏不变；未触发、观望和仍在进行中的不计；每笔只列胜率口径的盈亏标记、按第一止盈口径计的 R 和最终盈亏，不带止盈止损价、触线标注和第一止盈盈利行，也不附实际走势说明；逐笔按开仓时间从新到旧排，开仓时间与卡面同步",
          }
        : DETAIL_TITLES[kind];
  // The 累计 R per-trade list is plain newest-first (user
  // follow-up, 2026-11-16: the page was too cluttered — the
  // 第一止盈 / 全止盈 / 止损 sort options that keyed on the removed
  // guidance percentages are gone with them).
  // The window subtitle is a long caliber note; it starts folded to
  // two lines so the window opens on its figures （该收起来就收起来）,
  // with the full text one tap away. Switching windows or win-rate
  // calibers folds it again.
  const [subOpen, setSubOpen] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    setSubOpen(false);
  }, [kind, winRateMode]);
  // "Newest" runs on the SYNCED time (user follow-up, 2026-11-16):
  // a triggered leg's real 开仓时间 through the card face's chain
  // (live lifecycle triggeredAt → stored triggered_at → placement)
  // — the same instant DetailTradeRow prints, so every list in
  // these windows is plain time new→old and its printed times
  // agree with the cards. (The modal once read only the stored
  // triggered_at; a leg the server had not persisted yet sorted
  // and printed by its placement time here while its card already
  // showed the real trigger time — the 甲/乙 lists read as jumbled.)
  const syncTimeOf = (t: Trade) =>
    new Date(
      lifecycleById?.get(t.id)?.triggeredAt ??
        t.triggered_at ??
        t.opened_at,
    ).getTime();
  const byNewest = [...trades].sort(
    (a, b) => syncTimeOf(b) - syncTimeOf(a) || b.id - a.id,
  );
  // Untriggered trades book exactly 0R and never opened a position, so
  // they are left out of every per-trade list in these windows — the
  // totals are unchanged (they contributed 0) and only their count is
  // still mentioned where it explains a denominator.
  const settled = byNewest.filter(
    (t) => settledR(t) != null && t.verdict !== "no_trigger",
  );
  const settledSum = settled.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (settledR(t) ?? 0), 0);
  const correctTrades = byNewest.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "correct",
  );
  const wrongTrades = byNewest.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "wrong",
  );
  const noTriggerTrades = byNewest.filter(
    (t) => t.status === "reviewed" && t.verdict === "no_trigger",
  );
  const openTrades = byNewest.filter((t) => t.status === "open");
  const decided = correctTrades.length + wrongTrades.length;
  // The win-rate caliber's per-trade lists, classified by winRateMark
  // (a leg that touched TP1 counts as 盈 here even when its card-face
  // mark is 亏）, so the window's counts match its own headline rate.
  const winTrades = byNewest.filter((t) => winRateMark(t) === "盈");
  const lossTrades = byNewest.filter((t) => winRateMark(t) === "亏");
  const pnlDecided = winTrades.length + lossTrades.length;
  const pnlRate =
    stats?.pnl_win_rate ??
    (pnlDecided > 0 ? winTrades.length / pnlDecided : null);
  // The 甲胜率 / 乙胜率 views: the SELECTED path's figures plus its
  // settled per-trade list (untriggered legs book 0 and, per the
  // standing rule for these windows, are counted in the header line
  // but never listed). The two paths are separate options now — only
  // the selected one renders.
  const selectedPath: "甲路" | "乙路" | null =
    winRateMode === "jia" ? "甲路" : winRateMode === "yi" ? "乙路" : null;
  const pathSection =
    selectedPath == null
      ? null
      : {
          path: selectedPath,
          fig: pathFigures(trades, selectedPath),
          list: byNewest.filter(
            (t) =>
              pathOf(t) === selectedPath &&
              settledR(t) != null &&
              t.verdict !== "no_trigger",
          ),
        };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center sm:items-center sm:p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={meta.title}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label="关闭明细"
        onClick={onClose}
        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-default bg-black/50"
      />
      <div className="relative flex max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-2xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-t-2xl bg-[var(--surface)] shadow-xl sm:rounded-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text)]">
              {meta.title}
            </h2>
            <p
              className={`mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[var(--dim)] ${subOpen ? "" : "line-clamp-2"}`}
            >
              {meta.sub}
            </p>
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-expanded={subOpen}
              onClick={() => setSubOpen((v) => !v)}
              className="mt-0.5 min-h-0 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--dim)]"
            >
              {subOpen ? "收起说明" : "展开说明"}
            </button>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="关闭明细窗口"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[var(--border)] text-lg leading-none text-[var(--dim)]"
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Same floating-nav clearance as the round picker: the last
            detail rows must scroll clear of the app's bottom capsule. */}
        <div className="overflow-y-auto px-4 pt-4 pb-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom)+104px)] sm:pb-4">
          {kind === "totalR" && (
            <div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3 text-center">
                  <div
                    className={`text-xl font-bold ${mono} ${
                      settledSum > 0
                        ? "text-[var(--long)]"
                        : settledSum < 0
                          ? "text-[var(--short)]"
                          : "text-[var(--text)]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {settledSum > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                    {settledSum.toFixed(2)}R
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    逐笔合计
                    {stats && Math.abs(stats.total_r - settledSum) > 0.005
                      ? ` · 看板 ${stats.total_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}${stats.total_r.toFixed(2)}R`
                      : ""}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3 text-center">
                  <div className={`text-xl font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}>
                    {settled.length}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    已结算仓位单
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3 text-center">
                  <div className={`text-xl font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}>
                    {settled.length > 0
                      ? `${settledSum / settled.length >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${(settledSum / settled.length).toFixed(2)}R`
                      : "—"}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    平均每笔
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              {settled.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="pt-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                  还没有已结算的仓位单，累计 R 暂时是 0。
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
                  {settled.map((t) => (
                    <DetailTradeRow key={t.id} t={t} totalRView conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false} lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {kind === "winrate" && (
            <div className="mb-4">
              <WinRateModeToggle
                mode={winRateMode}
                onChange={onWinRateModeChange}
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {kind === "winrate" && winRateMode === "pnl" && (
            <div>
              <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                  <span
                    className={`text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}
                  >
                    {pnlRate != null ? `${Math.round(pnlRate * 100)}%` : "—"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    {winTrades.length} 盈 / {lossTrades.length} 亏
                  </span>
                </div>
                {pnlDecided > 0 ? (
                  <div
                    className="mt-3 flex h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--border)]"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    <div
                      className="bg-[var(--long)]"
                      style={{
                        width: `${(winTrades.length / pnlDecided) * 100}%`,
                      }}
                    />
                    <div
                      className="bg-[var(--short)]"
                      style={{
                        width: `${(lossTrades.length / pnlDecided) * 100}%`,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    还没有最终结算出盈亏的仓位单，盈亏胜率暂无。
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              {stats && stats.by_plan.length > 0 && (
                <DetailFold title="分方案盈亏胜率与期望">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {stats.by_plan.map((p) => (
                      <div
                        key={p.plan}
                        className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-2.5 text-sm"
                      >
                        <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text)]">
                          {p.plan}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}>
                          {p.total} 笔 · {p.pnl_win} 盈 / {p.pnl_loss} 亏
                        </span>
                        <span className={`ml-auto text-xs ${mono}`}>
                          盈亏胜率{" "}
                          <RateText
                            mode="pnl"
                            judgment={p.win_rate}
                            pnl={p.pnl_win_rate}
                          />
                          {" · "}累计{" "}
                          <span
                            className={
                              p.total_r > 0
                                ? "text-[var(--long)]"
                                : p.total_r < 0
                                  ? "text-[var(--short)]"
                                  : ""
                            }
                          >
                            {p.total_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                            {p.total_r.toFixed(2)}R
                          </span>
                          {` · 期望 ${
                            p.expectation_r != null
                              ? `${p.expectation_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}${p.expectation_r.toFixed(2)}R`
                              : "—"
                          }`}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </DetailFold>
              )}

              {[
                { label: `盈利的（${winTrades.length} 笔）`, list: winTrades },
                { label: `亏损的（${lossTrades.length} 笔）`, list: lossTrades },
              ].map(
                (sec) =>
                  sec.list.length > 0 && (
                    <div key={sec.label} className="mt-4">
                      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
                        {sec.label}
                      </h3>
                      <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                        {sec.list.map((t) => (
                          <DetailTradeRow key={t.id} t={t} winrateView pnlView conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false} lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)} />
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ),
              )}
              {pnlDecided === 0 && (
                <p className="pt-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                  还没有最终结算出盈亏的仓位单。
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {kind === "winrate" && winRateMode === "judgment" && (
            <div>
              <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                  <span className={`text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}>
                    {stats?.win_rate != null
                      ? `${Math.round(stats.win_rate * 100)}%`
                      : "—"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    {correctTrades.length} 对 / {wrongTrades.length} 错
                    {noTriggerTrades.length > 0
                      ? ` · ${noTriggerTrades.length} 笔未触发不计`
                      : ""}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {decided > 0 ? (
                  <div
                    className="mt-3 flex h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--border)]"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    <div
                      className="bg-[var(--long)]"
                      style={{
                        width: `${(correctTrades.length / decided) * 100}%`,
                      }}
                    />
                    <div
                      className="bg-[var(--short)]"
                      style={{
                        width: `${(wrongTrades.length / decided) * 100}%`,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                    还没有判定对错的单，胜率暂无。
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              {stats && stats.by_plan.length > 0 && (
                <DetailFold title="分方案胜率与期望">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {stats.by_plan.map((p) => (
                      <div
                        key={p.plan}
                        className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-2.5 text-sm"
                      >
                        <span className="font-medium text-[var(--text)]">
                          {p.plan}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}>
                          {p.total} 笔 · {p.correct} 对 / {p.wrong} 错
                          {p.no_trigger > 0 ? ` / ${p.no_trigger} 未触发` : ""}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`ml-auto text-xs ${mono}`}>
                          胜率{" "}
                          {p.win_rate != null
                            ? `${Math.round(p.win_rate * 100)}%`
                            : "—"}
                          {" · "}累计{" "}
                          <span
                            className={
                              p.total_r > 0
                                ? "text-[var(--long)]"
                                : p.total_r < 0
                                  ? "text-[var(--short)]"
                                  : ""
                            }
                          >
                            {p.total_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                            {p.total_r.toFixed(2)}R
                          </span>
                          {` · 期望 ${
                            p.expectation_r != null
                              ? `${p.expectation_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}${p.expectation_r.toFixed(2)}R`
                              : "—"
                          }`}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </DetailFold>
              )}

              {[
                { label: `判断对的（${correctTrades.length} 笔）`, list: correctTrades },
                { label: `判断错的（${wrongTrades.length} 笔）`, list: wrongTrades },
              ].map(
                (sec) =>
                  sec.list.length > 0 && (
                    <div key={sec.label} className="mt-4">
                      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
                        {sec.label}
                      </h3>
                      <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                        {sec.list.map((t) => (
                          <DetailTradeRow key={t.id} t={t} winrateView conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false} lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)} />
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ),
              )}
              {decided === 0 && (
                <p className="pt-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                  还没有判定对错的单。
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {kind === "winrate" && pathSection != null && (
            <div>
              {pathSection.fig.total === 0 ? (
                <p className="pt-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                  还没有{pathSection.path}的单。
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div>
                  <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3">
                    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                        <span className="inline-block rounded bg-[var(--accent)] px-2 py-0.5 text-sm font-bold text-black">
                          {pathSection.path}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                          胜率
                        </span>
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                        {pathSection.fig.wins} 盈 / {pathSection.fig.losses} 亏
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div
                      className={`mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}
                    >
                      {pathSection.fig.pnlRate != null
                        ? `${Math.round(pathSection.fig.pnlRate * 100)}%`
                        : "—"}
                    </div>
                    {pathSection.fig.wins + pathSection.fig.losses > 0 ? (
                      <div
                        className="mt-3 flex h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[var(--border)]"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      >
                        <div
                          className="bg-[var(--long)]"
                          style={{
                            width: `${(pathSection.fig.wins / (pathSection.fig.wins + pathSection.fig.losses)) * 100}%`,
                          }}
                        />
                        <div
                          className="bg-[var(--short)]"
                          style={{
                            width: `${(pathSection.fig.losses / (pathSection.fig.wins + pathSection.fig.losses)) * 100}%`,
                          }}
                        />
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
                        这一路还没有最终结算出盈亏的单，胜率暂无。
                      </p>
                    )}
                    <p className={`mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}>
                      判断胜率{" "}
                      {pathSection.fig.judgmentRate != null
                        ? `${Math.round(pathSection.fig.judgmentRate * 100)}%`
                        : "—"}
                      （{pathSection.fig.correct} 对 / {pathSection.fig.wrong} 错
                      {pathSection.fig.noTrigger > 0
                        ? ` · ${pathSection.fig.noTrigger} 未触发不计`
                        : ""}
                      ） · 累计{" "}
                      <span
                        className={
                          pathSection.fig.totalR > 0
                            ? "text-[var(--long)]"
                            : pathSection.fig.totalR < 0
                              ? "text-[var(--short)]"
                              : ""
                        }
                      >
                        {pathSection.fig.totalR > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                        {pathSection.fig.totalR.toFixed(2)}R
                      </span>
                      {pathSection.fig.expectation != null &&
                        ` · 期望 ${pathSection.fig.expectation > 0 ? "+" : ""}${pathSection.fig.expectation.toFixed(2)}R`}
                      {` · 共 ${pathSection.fig.total} 笔`}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  {pathSection.list.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="mt-4">
                      <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
                        {pathSection.path}逐笔（{pathSection.list.length} 笔）
                      </h3>
                      <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                        {pathSection.list.map((t) => (
                          <DetailTradeRow
                            key={t.id}
                            t={t}
                            winrateView
                            pnlView
                            conditionMet={
                              conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false
                            }
                            lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)}
                          />
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="mt-3 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                      这一路还没有结算出结果的单。
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {kind === "total" && (
            <div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-2 text-center">
                {[
                  { v: stats ? String(stats.total) : String(trades.length), l: "总判断" },
                  { v: stats ? String(stats.reviewed) : "—", l: "已复盘" },
                  { v: stats ? String(stats.open) : "—", l: "待复盘" },
                  {
                    v: stats
                      ? `${stats.correct} / ${stats.wrong}`
                      : "—",
                    l: "对 / 错",
                  },
                ].map((c) => (
                  <div
                    key={c.l}
                    className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3"
                  >
                    <div className={`text-xl font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}>
                      {c.v}
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">{c.l}</div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {stats && stats.by_symbol.length > 0 && (
                <DetailFold title="分币种">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {stats.by_symbol.map((b) => (
                      <div
                        key={b.symbol}
                        className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 rounded-lg border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-2.5 text-sm"
                      >
                        <span className={`font-bold text-[var(--text)] ${mono}`}>
                          {b.symbol}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`text-xs text-[var(--dim)] ${mono}`}>
                          {b.total} 笔 · 已复盘 {b.reviewed} · {b.correct} 对 /{" "}
                          {b.wrong} 错
                          {b.no_trigger > 0 ? ` / ${b.no_trigger} 未触发` : ""}
                        </span>
                        <span className={`ml-auto text-xs ${mono}`}>
                          {winRateMode === "judgment" ? "胜率" : "盈亏胜率"}{" "}
                          <RateText
                            mode={winRateMode}
                            judgment={b.win_rate}
                            pnl={b.pnl_win_rate}
                          />
                          {" · "}累计{" "}
                          <span
                            className={
                              b.total_r > 0
                                ? "text-[var(--long)]"
                                : b.total_r < 0
                                  ? "text-[var(--short)]"
                                  : ""
                            }
                          >
                            {b.total_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                            {b.total_r.toFixed(2)}R
                          </span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </DetailFold>
              )}

              <div className="mt-4">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--text)]">
                  全部逐笔（新 → 旧）
                </h3>
                {byNewest.filter((t) => t.verdict !== "no_trigger").length ===
                0 ? (
                  <p className="pt-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                    还没有模拟单。
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                    {byNewest
                      .filter((t) => t.verdict !== "no_trigger")
                      .map((t) => (
                        <DetailTradeRow key={t.id} t={t} conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false} lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)} />
                      ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {kind === "open" && (
            <div>
              <div className="rounded-lg bg-[var(--surface-alt)] px-3 py-3 text-center">
                <div className={`text-xl font-bold text-[var(--accent)] ${mono}`}>
                  {openTrades.length}
                </div>
                <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">待复盘</div>
              </div>
              {openTrades.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="pt-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--dim)]">
                  没有待复盘的单，全部都已结算。
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
                  {openTrades.map((t) => (
                    <DetailTradeRow key={t.id} t={t} conditionMet={conditionById?.get(t.id) ?? false} lifecycle={lifecycleById?.get(t.id)} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AddTradeForm({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const [symbol, setSymbol] = useState("BTC");
  const [direction, setDirection] = useState<"long" | "short" | "watch">("long");
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState("");
  const [conditional, setConditional] = useState(false);
  const [entry, setEntry] = useState("");
  const [sl, setSl] = useState("");
  const [tp, setTp] = useState("");
  const [tp2, setTp2] = useState("");
  const [rationale, setRationale] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const add = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () =>
      await api.addTrade({
        symbol,
        direction,
        entry_type:
          direction !== "watch" && conditional ? "conditional" : "immediate",
        plan: plan.trim() ? plan.trim() : undefined,
        entry_price: Number(entry),
        stop_loss: Number(sl),
        take_profit: Number(tp),
        take_profit2:
          direction !== "watch" && tp2.trim() ? Number(tp2) : undefined,
        rationale,
      }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      setEntry("");
      setSl("");
      setTp("");
      setTp2("");
      setPlan("");
      setRationale("");
      setError(null);
      onDone();
    },
    onError: (e) => setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)),
  });

  function submit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!symbol.trim() || !entry || !sl || !tp || !rationale.trim()) {
      setError("币种、入场、止损、止盈和判断依据都要填。");
      return;
    }
    add.mutate();
  }

  const inputCls =
    "w-full rounded-md border border-[var(--border)] bg-[var(--surface)] px-2.5 py-2 text-sm text-[var(--text)]";

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-3 px-4 py-4">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="f-symbol" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
            币种
          </label>
          <input
            id="f-symbol"
            className={inputCls}
            value={symbol}
            onChange={(e) => setSymbol(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="BTC"
            autoComplete="off"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">方向</span>
          <div className="mt-1 grid grid-cols-3 gap-1" role="group" aria-label="方向">
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={direction === "long"}
              onClick={() => setDirection("long")}
              className={`min-h-9 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold ${
                direction === "long"
                  ? "bg-[var(--long)] text-white"
                  : "border border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--dim)]"
              }`}
            >
              做多
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={direction === "short"}
              onClick={() => setDirection("short")}
              className={`min-h-9 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold ${
                direction === "short"
                  ? "bg-[var(--short)] text-white"
                  : "border border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--dim)]"
              }`}
            >
              做空
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              aria-pressed={direction === "watch"}
              onClick={() => setDirection("watch")}
              className={`min-h-9 rounded-md text-sm font-semibold ${
                direction === "watch"
                  ? "bg-[var(--accent)] text-black"
                  : "border border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--dim)]"
              }`}
            >
              观望
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="f-entry" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
            {direction === "watch" ? "判断时价" : "入场价"}
          </label>
          <input
            id="f-entry"
            className={`${inputCls} ${mono}`}
            inputMode="decimal"
            value={entry}
            onChange={(e) => setEntry(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="0.00"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="f-sl" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
            {direction === "watch" ? "区间下沿" : "止损价"}
          </label>
          <input
            id="f-sl"
            className={`${inputCls} ${mono}`}
            inputMode="decimal"
            value={sl}
            onChange={(e) => setSl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="0.00"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="f-tp" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
            {direction === "watch" ? "区间上沿" : "止盈价"}
          </label>
          <input
            id="f-tp"
            className={`${inputCls} ${mono}`}
            inputMode="decimal"
            value={tp}
            onChange={(e) => setTp(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="0.00"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
      {direction !== "watch" && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="f-tp2" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
              第二止盈价（选填）
            </label>
            <input
              id="f-tp2"
              className={`${inputCls} ${mono}`}
              inputMode="decimal"
              value={tp2}
              onChange={(e) => setTp2(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="0.00"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {direction !== "watch" && (
        <label className="flex min-h-9 items-center gap-2 text-sm text-[var(--text)]">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={conditional}
            onChange={(e) => setConditional(e.target.checked)}
            className="h-4 w-4"
          />
          条件单：价格到达入场价才算触发，触发前不算持仓
        </label>
      )}
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="f-plan" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          方案（选填，用于分方案统计胜率，如「甲路·反弹空」「乙路·破位回踩空」）
        </label>
        <input
          id="f-plan"
          className={inputCls}
          value={plan}
          onChange={(e) => setPlan(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="甲路·反弹空"
          autoComplete="off"
        />
      </div>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="f-rationale" className="text-xs text-[var(--dim)]">
          判断依据（盘面状态、均线结构、关键位，写清了复盘才有的对）
        </label>
        <textarea
          id="f-rationale"
          className={`${inputCls} min-h-20`}
          value={rationale}
          onChange={(e) => setRationale(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="例：4h 趋势向下、均线空头发散，1h 反弹到密集区下沿受阻…"
        />
      </div>
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-[var(--short)]">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={add.isPending}
        className="min-h-10 w-full rounded-md bg-[var(--text)] text-sm font-semibold text-[var(--bg)] disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {add.isPending ? "记录中…" : "记下这笔模拟单"}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

// The round picker window: opened from the compact round bar, it
// lists every round (with its call count) plus 当天这轮（默认）and
// 全部轮次. Picking one applies it to the card list and closes the
// window, so the homepage itself carries no per-round clutter.
function RoundPickerModal({
  options,
  todayRoundLabel,
  totalCount,
  selection,
  onPick,
  onClose,
}: {
  options: { label: string; count: number }[];
  todayRoundLabel: string | null;
  totalCount: number;
  selection: string | null;
  onPick: (selection: string | null) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const rows: {
    key: string;
    label: string;
    sub: string;
    active: boolean;
    value: string | null;
  }[] = [
    ...(todayRoundLabel
      ? [
          {
            key: "__today__",
            label: `当天这轮 · ${todayRoundLabel}`,
            sub: "默认显示",
            active: selection === null,
            value: null as string | null,
          },
        ]
      : []),
    ...options.map((r) => ({
      key: r.label,
      label: r.label,
      sub: `${r.count} 笔`,
      active: selection === r.label,
      value: r.label as string | null,
    })),
    {
      key: "__all__",
      label: "全部轮次",
      sub: `共 ${totalCount} 笔`,
      active: selection === "__all__",
      value: "__all__" as string | null,
    },
  ];
  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center sm:items-center sm:p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label="选择要看的轮次"
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label="关闭轮次选择"
        onClick={onClose}
        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full cursor-default bg-black/50"
      />
      <div className="relative flex max-h-[88vh] w-full max-w-2xl flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-t-2xl bg-[var(--surface)] shadow-xl sm:rounded-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 border-b border-[var(--border)] px-4 py-3">
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[var(--text)]">
              选择要看的轮次
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[var(--dim)]">
              主页的单子按这里选的轮次显示；不选时默认看当天这轮。
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="关闭轮次选择窗口"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[var(--border)] text-lg leading-none text-[var(--dim)]"
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
        {/* Bottom padding clears the Muse app's floating nav capsule
            (~104px + the device safe area), so the last round option
            can scroll fully into view instead of hiding under it. */}
        <div className="overflow-y-auto px-4 pt-4 pb-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom)+104px)] sm:pb-4">
          <div className="space-y-2" role="group" aria-label="轮次选项">
            {rows.map((row) => (
              <button
                key={row.key}
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={row.active}
                onClick={() => onPick(row.value)}
                className={`flex min-h-11 w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 text-left ${
                  row.active
                    ? "bg-[var(--text)] text-[var(--bg)]"
                    : "border border-[var(--border)] text-[var(--text)]"
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold">{row.label}</span>
                <span
                  className={`ml-auto text-xs ${row.active ? "opacity-80" : "text-[var(--dim)]"}`}
                >
                  {row.sub}
                  {row.active ? " · 正在看" : ""}
                </span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function App() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false);
  // The long rules note under the list heading starts collapsed to its
  // first sentence; the chevron beside it expands the full text.
  const [noteOpen, setNoteOpen] = useState(false);
  // Page-wide single-open accordion, keyed by coin: only one coin's
  // detail panel is open at a time; opening another coin closes the
  // previous one.
  const [expandedSymbol, setExpandedSymbol] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // Which scoreboard cell's detail window is open (null = closed).
  const [detailKind, setDetailKind] = useState<DetailKind | null>(null);
  // Win-rate caliber: P/L by default, per the user's request.
  const [winRateMode, setWinRateMode] = useState<WinRateMode>("pnl");
  // 山寨币 section ordering: default (most recent call first) or a
  // ranking by the coin's overall win rate / total R across all rounds.
  const [altRankMode, setAltRankMode] = useState<AltRankMode>("default");
  // Round filter for the card list. null = "follow the default", which
  // is today's round (the round recorded on today's Shanghai date),
  // falling back to the latest round when nothing was recorded today.
  // "__all__" shows every round; any other value is a round label.
  const [roundSelection, setRoundSelection] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // Whether the round picker window is open — the per-round options
  // live in that popup so the homepage stays uncrowded.
  const [roundPickerOpen, setRoundPickerOpen] = useState(false);

  // Full-ledger load over listTrades' cursor pagination (P2-05):
  // the page's grouping, per-coin figures and detail windows are all
  // computed from the complete row set, so every page is walked via
  // next_cursor (500 rows per page) and merged before anything
  // renders from it — there is no row-count ceiling anymore. If a
  // LATER page fails, the rows already loaded are kept and
  // `incomplete` flags the shortfall: the page shows a visible
  // 加载不完整 notice instead of silently presenting partial data
  // as the whole ledger. A failure on the FIRST page still throws,
  // so the existing 读取失败 state with its retry button shows.
  const trades = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["trades"],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<{ trades: Trade[]; incomplete: boolean }> => {
      const merged: Trade[] = [];
      let cursor: number | undefined;
      for (;;) {
        let page: ApiResponse<typeof api, "listTrades">;
        try {
          page = await api.listTrades(
            cursor == null ? { limit: 500 } : { limit: 500, cursor },
          );
        } catch (e) {
          if (merged.length === 0) throw e;
          return { trades: merged, incomplete: true };
        }
        merged.push(...page.trades);
        if (page.next_cursor == null || page.trades.length === 0) {
          return {
            trades: merged,
            incomplete: page.next_cursor != null,
          };
        }
        cursor = page.next_cursor;
      }
    },
  });
  const stats = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["stats"],
    queryFn: () => api.getStats({}),
  });
  // Per-major independent 100U account states (BTC/ETH/SOL), written by
  // the settlement flow; the card headers show each coin's balance.
  const coinAccounts = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["coinAccounts"],
    queryFn: () => api.getCoinAccounts({}),
  });
  const accountBySymbol = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, CoinAccount>();
    for (const a of coinAccounts.data?.accounts ?? []) map.set(a.symbol, a);
    return map;
  }, [coinAccounts.data]);
  // Live prices for the symbols that still have open calls — fetched when
  // the page opens (short cache, plus a slow refresh) so each open leg
  // can show its current price, floating figure or distance-to-trigger
  // without any tapping. Symbols no source can price land in `failures`
  // with the reason, and their cards say so instead of going blank.
  const livePrices = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["livePrices"],
    queryFn: () => api.getLivePrices({}),
    staleTime: 30_000,
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  });
  // 乙路 legs' breakout-precondition states: has a 15-minute close
  // beyond the leg's breakout line happened since it was recorded?
  // The server judges it on Gate 15m candles (the settlement caliber);
  // the page only renders the resulting lifecycle name. Polled on the
  // same rhythm as the live prices.
  const conditionStates = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["conditionStates"],
    queryFn: () => api.getConditionStates({}),
    staleTime: 30_000,
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  });
  const conditionById = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<number, boolean>();
    for (const s of conditionStates.data?.states ?? []) {
      map.set(s.id, s.condition_met);
    }
    return map;
  }, [conditionStates.data]);
  // The same scan's timestamps: when each 乙路 leg's condition
  // was met and when each review-confirmed leg really triggered —
  // the card time lines read their state times from here.
  const lifecycleById = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<number, LegLifecycle>();
    for (const s of conditionStates.data?.states ?? []) {
      map.set(s.id, {
        conditionMetAt: s.condition_met_at,
        triggeredAt: s.triggered_at,
        dataStatus: s.data_status,
      });
    }
    return map;
  }, [conditionStates.data]);
  // Majors live feed: browser-direct Gate (WebSocket push with REST
  // polling fallback), one feed per major, feeding that coin's card.
  const btcLiveState = useGateLivePrice("BTC_USDT");
  const ethLiveState = useGateLivePrice("ETH_USDT");
  const solLiveState = useGateLivePrice("SOL_USDT");
  const gateLiveBySymbol: Record<string, GateLiveState> = {
    BTC: btcLiveState,
    ETH: ethLiveState,
    SOL: solLiveState,
  };

  const all = trades.data?.trades ?? [];
  const s = stats.data;
  // 甲胜率 / 乙胜率 modes: the scoreboard cell shows the SELECTED
  // path's own win rate (the same headline figure the win-rate
  // window's per-path view leads with), computed from the full
  // ledger. Both paths' figures are computed; the mode picks one.
  const jiaFigures = pathFigures(all, "甲路");
  const yiFigures = pathFigures(all, "乙路");
  // The four scoreboard values as displayed, and the one font size
  // they share (stepped down when the longest value — usually 累计 R —
  // would overflow its tile at phone width; see statValueSize).
  const totalText = s ? String(s.total) : "—";
  const openText = s ? String(s.open) : "—";
  const rateText =
    winRateMode === "jia" || winRateMode === "yi"
      ? (winRateMode === "jia" ? jiaFigures : yiFigures).pnlRate != null
        ? `${Math.round(((winRateMode === "jia" ? jiaFigures : yiFigures).pnlRate as number) * 100)}%`
        : "—"
      : (winRateMode === "judgment" ? s?.win_rate : s?.pnl_win_rate) != null
        ? `${Math.round(((winRateMode === "judgment" ? s?.win_rate : s?.pnl_win_rate) as number) * 100)}%`
        : "—";
  const totalRText = s
    ? `${s.total_r > 0 ? "+" : ""}${s.total_r.toFixed(2)}`
    : "—";
  const statSize = statValueSize([totalText, openText, rateText, totalRText]);

  // One group per coin, ordered by the coin's most recent call (the list
  // itself is newest-first, so first appearance wins).
  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Trade[]>();
    for (const t of all) {
      const arr = map.get(t.symbol);
      if (arr) arr.push(t);
      else map.set(t.symbol, [t]);
    }
    return [...map.entries()].map(([symbol, ts]) => ({ symbol, trades: ts }));
  }, [all]);

  // Altcoin live feed: the same browser-direct Gate push the majors
  // run on — one shared socket subscribed to every altcoin contract
  // in the ledger, so an altcoin card's 现价 ticks, flashes and
  // slides exactly like a major's. (Same Gate interface the polled
  // prices already came from; this just moves it browser-direct.)
  const altContracts = useMemo(
    () =>
      groups
        .filter((g) => !MAJOR_SYMBOLS.has(g.symbol))
        .map((g) => `${g.symbol}_USDT`)
        .sort(),
    [groups],
  );
  const altGateLiveBySymbol = useGateLivePrices(altContracts);

  // --- Rounds ---
  // Round key per trade, with batch inheritance: a batch is recorded
  // at one instant, and some rows of a batch carry no round tag of
  // their own (round 3's 乙路 rows say only 「同上」, and its watch
  // batch is untagged) — they inherit the round tagged on their
  // batch-mates, so a round filter never splits a 甲/乙 pair.
  const roundKeyById = useMemo(() => {
    const byInstant = new Map<string, string>();
    for (const t of all) {
      const r = roundOf(t);
      if (r && !byInstant.has(t.opened_at)) byInstant.set(t.opened_at, r);
    }
    const map = new Map<number, string>();
    for (const t of all) {
      map.set(
        t.id,
        roundOf(t) ?? byInstant.get(t.opened_at) ?? UNTAGGED_ROUND,
      );
    }
    return map;
  }, [all]);
  const roundKey = (t: Trade): string =>
    roundKeyById.get(t.id) ?? roundKeyOf(t);

  // Every distinct round label present in the ledger, newest first
  // (by round number when parseable, else by its newest call's time).
  const roundOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, { label: string; count: number; latest: number }>();
    for (const t of all) {
      const key = roundKey(t);
      const ts = new Date(t.opened_at).getTime();
      const cur = map.get(key);
      if (cur) {
        cur.count += 1;
        cur.latest = Math.max(cur.latest, ts);
      } else {
        map.set(key, { label: key, count: 1, latest: ts });
      }
    }
    return [...map.values()].sort((a, b) => {
      const na = roundNumberOf(a.label);
      const nb = roundNumberOf(b.label);
      if (na != null && nb != null && na !== nb) return nb - na;
      if ((na != null) !== (nb != null)) return na != null ? -1 : 1;
      return b.latest - a.latest;
    });
  }, [all, roundKeyById]);

  // 目前是第几轮: the round of the newest call in the ledger.
  const currentRoundLabel = useMemo(() => {
    for (const t of all) {
      const key = roundKey(t);
      if (key !== UNTAGGED_ROUND) return key;
    }
    return null;
  }, [all, roundKeyById]);

  // 当天那轮: the round of the newest call recorded today (Shanghai).
  const todayRoundLabel = useMemo(() => {
    const today = shanghaiDayKey(new Date().toISOString());
    for (const t of all) {
      if (shanghaiDayKey(t.opened_at) === today) {
        const key = roundKey(t);
        if (key !== UNTAGGED_ROUND) return key;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }, [all, roundKeyById]);

  const defaultRoundLabel = todayRoundLabel ?? currentRoundLabel;
  const effectiveRound: string | null =
    roundSelection === null
      ? defaultRoundLabel
      : roundSelection === "__all__"
        ? null
        : roundSelection;
  const showingAllRounds = effectiveRound == null;

  // The card list shows only the selected round's calls (every round
  // when effectiveRound is null); coins left with no call in the round
  // drop out of the list.
  const visibleGroups = useMemo(() => {
    if (effectiveRound == null) return groups;
    return groups
      .map((g) => ({
        symbol: g.symbol,
        trades: g.trades.filter((t) => roundKey(t) === effectiveRound),
      }))
      .filter((g) => g.trades.length > 0);
  }, [groups, effectiveRound, roundKeyById]);
  const visibleCount = visibleGroups.reduce(
    (acc, g) => acc + g.trades.length,
    0,
  );

  // Split the visible coin groups into the two sections the page
  // renders: mainstream coins (BTC/ETH/SOL) on top, altcoins below.
