    response: z.object({
      fixed: z.number(),
      jia_fixed: z.boolean(),
      yi_fixed: z.boolean(),
    }),
    async handler(ctx) {
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      const batchPlan = "第5轮山寨纸面·盘中复扫";
      let jiaFixed = false;
      let yiFixed = false;
      const [jia] = await db
        .select()
        .from(schema.paperTrades)
        .where(eq(schema.paperTrades.id, 53));
      if (
        jia &&
        jia.symbol === "ELSA" &&
        jia.plan === batchPlan &&
        jia.direction === "short" &&
        jia.entryPrice === 0.0677 &&
        jia.stopLoss === 0.069
      ) {
        // Truth-level CAS (P1-31, 2026-11-17): the guard values read
        // above are re-checked in the UPDATE's WHERE.
        await db
          .update(schema.paperTrades)
          .set({ plan: "甲路·反弹空" })
          .where(
            and(
              eq(schema.paperTrades.id, 53),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.symbol, jia.symbol),
              jia.plan == null
                ? isNull(schema.paperTrades.plan)
                : eq(schema.paperTrades.plan, jia.plan),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.direction, jia.direction),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.entryPrice, jia.entryPrice),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.stopLoss, jia.stopLoss),
            ),
          );
        jiaFixed = true;
      }
      const [yi] = await db
        .select()
        .from(schema.paperTrades)
        .where(eq(schema.paperTrades.id, 54));
      if (
        yi &&
        yi.symbol === "ELSA" &&
        yi.plan === batchPlan &&
        yi.direction === "short" &&
        yi.entryPrice === 0.0658 &&
        yi.stopLoss === 0.067
      ) {
        // Truth-level CAS (P1-31, 2026-11-17): the guard values read
        // above are re-checked in the UPDATE's WHERE.
        await db
          .update(schema.paperTrades)
          .set({ plan: "乙路·破位回踩空" })
          .where(
            and(
              eq(schema.paperTrades.id, 54),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.symbol, yi.symbol),
              yi.plan == null
                ? isNull(schema.paperTrades.plan)
                : eq(schema.paperTrades.plan, yi.plan),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.direction, yi.direction),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.entryPrice, yi.entryPrice),
              eq(schema.paperTrades.stopLoss, yi.stopLoss),
            ),
          );
        yiFixed = true;
      }
      if (jiaFixed || yiFixed) ctx.invalidateQueries();
      return {
        fixed: (jiaFixed ? 1 : 0) + (yiFixed ? 1 : 0),
        jia_fixed: jiaFixed,
        yi_fixed: yiFixed,
      };
    },
  }),

  // One-time data-repair rule (P2-22, see MIGRATION-NOTES.md §4 at
  // the space root): every one-time repair / backfill action MUST
  // carry per-row identity guards (symbol + key prices), MUST write
  // the guard values into the update's WHERE clause (truth-level
  // CAS, as repairTriggeredAtV104/V106 do), MUST be safe to re-run
  // with already-repaired rows skipped, and MUST return a per-row
  // before/after report. Bare-id-only updates are forbidden.
  backfillTakeProfit2: defineAction({
    description:
      "One-time backfill (2026-11-16, whole-page check the user ordered after the ELSA path-badge fix): the take_profit2 column exists only since migration 0006, which backfilled just the round-7 legs (ids 71–84). Every dual-path leg of rounds 2, 3, 5 and 6 (ids 4–7, 9–14, 41–66) also carries a recorded second target — written as 止盈第二档 in its own rationale (rounds 2/5/6) and in the paper ledger's per-leg terms (records.md, all four rounds) — but its column stayed null, so those legs render a single 止盈 everywhere (card triplet, expanded records, detail rows) and the two 已盈利 legs among them (HYPE 乙 id 56, ELSA 乙 id 66) price their badge off the first target instead of the full one. This action writes each leg's ledger-recorded TP2 into the column. Every value was cross-checked three ways before shipping: the ledger line's entry/stop/TP1 equal the row's stored entry/stop/TP1 exactly, and where the rationale names the second target it agrees (round 3's rationales are shared 同上 text, so there the ledger line is the per-leg source; id 44's note mentions 0.1010, which is its sibling 甲 leg's TP2 — the ledger gives 乙 0.0990, matching its own rationale). Each row is guarded by its exact recorded snapshot (symbol / direction / entry / stop / TP1) and only fills a still-null take_profit2, so an already-backfilled or differently-recorded row is left untouched. No price, verdict or review is modified, and no statistic reads this column (display only). Safe to re-run.",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: z.object({
      fixed: z.number(),
      fixed_ids: z.array(z.number()),
    }),
    async handler(ctx) {
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      // [id, symbol, direction, entry, stop, takeProfit1, takeProfit2] —
      // TP2 from the paper ledger's per-leg terms (see description).
      const rows: Array<
        [number, string, "long" | "short", number, number, number, number]
      > = [
        [4, "BTC", "short", 72550, 73050, 71850, 71200],
        [5, "BTC", "short", 71900, 72450, 71200, 70600],
        [6, "ETH", "short", 2012, 2045, 1960, 1920],
        [7, "ETH", "short", 1992, 2028, 1950, 1915],
        [9, "HYPE", "short", 71.45, 72.65, 69.42, 67.5],
        [10, "HYPE", "short", 69.7, 70.9, 67.5, 66],
        [11, "INIT", "short", 0.1347, 0.1415, 0.1096, 0.1],
        [12, "INIT", "short", 0.111, 0.1175, 0.1, 0.092],
        [13, "UAI", "short", 0.03645, 0.0387, 0.03384, 0.0315],
        [14, "UAI", "short", 0.0342, 0.0362, 0.0315, 0.0295],
        [41, "HYPE", "long", 72.9, 71.8, 74.4, 76.8],
        [42, "HYPE", "long", 74.1, 73.2, 75.8, 77.5],
        [43, "INIT", "short", 0.115, 0.119, 0.106, 0.101],
        [44, "INIT", "short", 0.108, 0.111, 0.103, 0.099],
        [45, "UAI", "short", 0.0419, 0.0435, 0.037, 0.0345],
        [46, "UAI", "short", 0.0384, 0.0402, 0.0358, 0.034],
        [47, "MAGMA", "long", 0.3035, 0.294, 0.3229, 0.345],
        [48, "MAGMA", "long", 0.318, 0.308, 0.332, 0.345],
        [49, "AKE", "short", 0.0067, 0.0073, 0.0058, 0.0049],
        [50, "AKE", "short", 0.0053, 0.00585, 0.00455, 0.0038],
        [51, "IDOL", "short", 0.0228, 0.0245, 0.0205, 0.0187],
        [52, "IDOL", "short", 0.021, 0.0223, 0.0192, 0.018],
        [53, "ELSA", "short", 0.0677, 0.069, 0.0654, 0.063],
        [54, "ELSA", "short", 0.0658, 0.067, 0.063, 0.0605],
        [55, "HYPE", "short", 70, 71, 68, 66.5],
        [56, "HYPE", "short", 67.8, 68.6, 66.4, 65.2],
        [57, "INIT", "short", 0.102, 0.1055, 0.097, 0.0935],
        [58, "INIT", "short", 0.0975, 0.1, 0.0945, 0.092],
        [59, "UAI", "short", 0.038, 0.0398, 0.035, 0.0325],
        [60, "UAI", "short", 0.0355, 0.037, 0.0335, 0.032],
        [61, "MAGMA", "long", 0.31, 0.301, 0.322, 0.335],
        [62, "MAGMA", "long", 0.322, 0.313, 0.332, 0.345],
        [63, "IDOL", "short", 0.0228, 0.0245, 0.0205, 0.0187],
        [64, "IDOL", "short", 0.019, 0.0205, 0.017, 0.0155],
        [65, "ELSA", "short", 0.0655, 0.067, 0.0635, 0.0615],
        [66, "ELSA", "short", 0.0642, 0.0654, 0.0625, 0.0605],
      ];
      const fixedIds: number[] = [];
      for (const [id, symbol, direction, entry, stop, tp1, tp2] of rows) {
        const [row] = await db
          .select()
          .from(schema.paperTrades)
          .where(eq(schema.paperTrades.id, id));
        if (
          row &&
          row.symbol === symbol &&
          row.direction === direction &&
          row.entryPrice === entry &&
          row.stopLoss === stop &&
          row.takeProfit === tp1 &&
          row.takeProfit2 == null
        ) {
          // Truth-level CAS (P1-31, 2026-11-17): the guard values
          // read above are re-checked in the UPDATE's WHERE.
          await db
            .update(schema.paperTrades)
            .set({ takeProfit2: tp2 })
            .where(
              and(
                eq(schema.paperTrades.id, id),
                eq(schema.paperTrades.symbol, symbol),
                eq(schema.paperTrades.direction, direction),
                eq(schema.paperTrades.entryPrice, entry),
                eq(schema.paperTrades.stopLoss, stop),
                eq(schema.paperTrades.takeProfit, tp1),
                isNull(schema.paperTrades.takeProfit2),
              ),
            );
          fixedIds.push(id);
        }
      }
      if (fixedIds.length > 0) ctx.invalidateQueries();
      return { fixed: fixedIds.length, fixed_ids: fixedIds };
    },
  }),

  repairTriggeredAtV104: defineAction({
    description:
      "One-time repair (2026-11-17, P1 follow-up): three stored triggered_at values disagree with the paper ledger's candle evidence. id 64 (IDOL 乙）: the derivation stored the 01:45 candle's OPEN (2026-11-14T13:45Z) but the ledger confirms the retest on that candle's CLOSE — the 01:15 candle closed below the trigger line and the 01:45 candle closed at 0.018001 back inside the retest band, so the confirmed open is that candle's close, 02:00 CST = 2026-11-14T18:00Z. id 5 (BTC 乙）: stored 2026-11-12T14:25Z (the placement instant) but the ledger's first in-band 15m close is the 00:00 candle closing 71,966.8 inside the 71,850–72,000 retest band, confirmed at its close 00:15 CST = 2026-11-12T16:15Z. id 95 (IDOL 甲）: stored triggered_at equals its placement instant (2026-11-16T13:16Z) — false precision; the ledger only knows the real touch fell in the 21:25–21:30 window, so the row is switched to the unverified display (triggered_at=null, triggered_at_unverified=true). Each row is guarded by its exact current stored value (id 95 also requires unverified=false), so already-repaired rows are skipped and nothing else is touched — id 85 is deliberately NOT handled. Safe to re-run; returns per-row before/after values.",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: repairTriggeredAtResponse,
    handler: async (ctx): Promise<z.infer<typeof repairTriggeredAtResponse>> =>
      runRepairTriggeredAtV104(ctx),
  }),

  repairTriggeredAtV106: defineAction({
    description:
      "One-time repair (2026-11-17, follow-up to repairTriggeredAtV104; RGB ruled both rows follow the paper ledger — 都按台账改）: two stored triggered_at values are corrected to the ledger's confirmation instants. id 85 (round-8 HYPE 甲空）: 2026-11-16T14:15Z (22:15 CST first touch) → 2026-11-16T15:00Z (23:00 CST, the 15m trigger confirmation recorded in the row's outcome_note and the ledger). id 66 (round-6 ELSA 乙空）: 2026-11-14T14:00Z → 2026-11-14T16:00Z (2026-11-15 00:00 CST, the 23:45 candle's close = the ledger's retest confirmation). Each row is guarded by its exact current stored value, re-checked in the update's WHERE clause, so a row that no longer holds the expected value is skipped and its actual value reported. Only triggered_at is written — no other field, statistic, or page behavior changes. Safe to re-run; returns per-row before/after values and whether each row was written.",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: repairTriggeredAtResponse,
    handler: async (ctx): Promise<z.infer<typeof repairTriggeredAtResponse>> =>
      runRepairTriggeredAtV106(ctx),
  }),

  backfillExitPricesV104: defineAction({
    description:
      "One-time backfill (2026-11-17, P1-02 follow-up): twelve finally-settled legs predate the exit_price/exit_reason columns (migration 0008), so their real exit prices live only in the paper ledger / settlement notes even though each one is exactly the review_price already stored on the row. This action copies each leg's ledger-recorded actual exit into exit_price with its exit_reason: id 5 → 70921.7 expiry (10:00 market settlement), ids 32/34/35/37/39 → OKX take-profit fill (average) prices 70503.77 / 1946.48 / 68848.34 / 1932.539375 / 79.81 as tp1, id 48 → 0.31437 expiry (21:10 market settlement), ids 56/66 → 65.2 / 0.0605 as tp2 (second target exit per terms), ids 64/81/84 → 0.01936 / 0.018495 / 0.05721 expiry (21:10 market settlement). Guard per row: exit_price still null AND review_price and first_touch exactly the expected snapshot (first_touch is tp for all twelve — every one of these legs touched its first take-profit first; the expiry rows then ran past it to their expiry market-price settlement) — a row already backfilled or since re-settled differently is skipped. Only exit_price and exit_reason are written. Safe to re-run; returns per-row written values.",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: backfillExitPricesResponse,
    handler: async (ctx): Promise<z.infer<typeof backfillExitPricesResponse>> =>
      runBackfillExitPricesV104(ctx),
  }),

  applyV104DataRepairs: defineAction({
    description:
      "One-time combined runner for the two V104 data repairs (2026-11-17): runs repairTriggeredAtV104 (three triggered_at corrections) and backfillExitPricesV104 (twelve exit_price/exit_reason backfills) back to back and returns both per-row reports. It exists so a single post-submit invocation lands BOTH repairs on the durable ledger; each underlying repair keeps its own hard-coded guards and remains independently callable, so this runner is safe to call any number of times — already-repaired rows simply report skipped.",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: applyV104RepairsResponse,
    handler: async (
      ctx,
    ): Promise<z.infer<typeof applyV104RepairsResponse>> => {
      const triggered_at = await runRepairTriggeredAtV104(ctx);
      const exit_prices = await runBackfillExitPricesV104(ctx);
      return { triggered_at, exit_prices };
    },
  }),

  saveReview: defineAction({
    description:
      "Writes the review of one paper trade: verdict (correct/wrong/no_trigger), price at review time, the window high/low since entry, which level was touched first, what price actually did, and a critique of the original reasoning. Optionally also records the leg's REAL exit price and exit reason (tp1/tp2/sl/expiry/manual) when the settlement flow knows the actual fill; fields not provided keep their stored values. Finality guard (P1-16): once a row carries a FINAL settlement, a late interim review is rejected outright, an identical final re-submit is an idempotent no-op, and a changed final settlement overwrites only with an audit entry appended to review_audit (pass correction_reason to say why). This is the ONLY review write path — the artifact's built-in self-review was removed (2026-11-16); settlement is done by the external ledger flow, which calls this action.",
    request: z.object({
      id: z.number().int().positive(),
      verdict: z.enum(["correct", "wrong", "no_trigger"]),
      review_price: z.number().positive(),
      window_high: z.number().positive(),
      window_low: z.number().positive(),
      first_touch: z.enum(["tp", "sl", "none"]),
      outcome_note: z.string().min(1),
      reasoning_review: z.string().min(1),
      exit_price: z.number().positive().optional(),
      exit_reason: z.enum(["tp1", "tp2", "sl", "expiry", "manual"]).optional(),
      // Why a final settlement is being replaced by another final
      // settlement (P1-16); recorded in review_audit. Optional.
      correction_reason: z.string().nullable().optional(),
    }),
    response: z.object({ ok: z.literal(true) }),
    async handler(ctx, args): Promise<{ ok: true }> {
      // Consistency guards (P2-10, 2026-11-17): a contradictory
      // review is rejected BEFORE anything is written, so a bad
      // settlement call can never land in the ledger half-checked.
      // review_price deliberately carries no window constraint —
      // it is the price at review time, not a window extreme.
      if (args.window_high < args.window_low) {
        throw new Error(
          `saveReview: window_high (${args.window_high}) 小于 window_low (${args.window_low})，未写入`,
        );
      }
      if (args.verdict === "no_trigger" && args.first_touch !== "none") {
        throw new Error(
          `saveReview: verdict=no_trigger 与 first_touch=${args.first_touch} 矛盾（未触发的单不应有触价），未写入`,
        );
      }
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      // Finality guard (P1-16, 2026-11-17): read the current row
      // first. The settlement flow is sequential (interim snapshots
      // first, the final settlement last), but a late-arriving stale
      // interim must never push an already-final row back to
      // un-final and corrupt the statistics.
      const [current] = await db
        .select()
        .from(schema.paperTrades)
        .where(eq(schema.paperTrades.id, args.id));
      if (!current) {
        throw new Error(`saveReview: no paper trade with id ${args.id}`);
      }
      const currentFinal =
        current.status === "reviewed" && !isInterim(current);
      const incomingInterim = noteIsInterim(args.outcome_note);
      let nextReviewAudit: string | undefined;
      if (currentFinal) {
        if (incomingInterim) {
          throw new Error(
            `saveReview: 记录 #${args.id} 已终局结算，拒绝迟到的临时复盘覆盖，未写入。如需纠错，请提交终局复盘并填写 correction_reason。`,
          );
        }
        // Final over final. The effective exit fields are what this
        // submit would store (an omitted field keeps the stored
        // value, per the P1-02 rule below).
        const effExitPrice = args.exit_price ?? current.exitPrice ?? null;
        const effExitReason = args.exit_reason ?? current.exitReason ?? null;
        const identical =
          current.verdict === args.verdict &&
          current.reviewPrice === args.review_price &&
          current.firstTouch === args.first_touch &&
          (current.exitPrice ?? null) === effExitPrice &&
          (current.exitReason ?? null) === effExitReason &&
          current.windowHigh === args.window_high &&
          current.windowLow === args.window_low &&
          (current.outcomeNote ?? null) === args.outcome_note;
        if (identical) {
          // Duplicate submit of the same final settlement: succeed
          // idempotently — no write, no audit entry.
          return { ok: true };
        }
        // A real correction: append the audit entry.
        let audit: unknown[] = [];
        if (current.reviewAudit) {
          try {
            const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(current.reviewAudit);
            if (Array.isArray(parsed)) audit = parsed;
          } catch {
            audit = [];
          }
        }
        audit.push({
          at: new Date().toISOString(),
          kind: "final_overwrite",
          reason: args.correction_reason ?? null,
          prev_verdict: current.verdict ?? null,
          new_verdict: args.verdict,
          prev_reviewed_at: current.reviewedAt
            ? current.reviewedAt.toISOString()
            : null,
        });
        nextReviewAudit = JSON.stringify(audit);
      }
      // Optimistic concurrency: the UPDATE only lands if reviewed_at
      // is still exactly what was read above (IS NULL when the row
      // had never been reviewed). Zero affected rows means another
      // write landed in between — fail loudly instead of silently
      // overwriting it.
      const updated = await db
        .update(schema.paperTrades)
        .set({
          status: "reviewed",
          reviewedAt: new Date(),
          verdict: args.verdict,
          reviewPrice: args.review_price,
          windowHigh: args.window_high,
          windowLow: args.window_low,
          firstTouch: args.first_touch,
          outcomeNote: args.outcome_note,
          reasoningReview: args.reasoning_review,
          // Real exit price / reason (P1-02): written ONLY when the
          // caller provided them — an omitted field keeps whatever
          // the row already stores, never overwritten with undefined.
          ...(args.exit_price !== undefined
            ? { exitPrice: args.exit_price }
            : {}),
          ...(args.exit_reason !== undefined
            ? { exitReason: args.exit_reason }
            : {}),
          ...(nextReviewAudit !== undefined
            ? { reviewAudit: nextReviewAudit }
            : {}),
        })
        .where(
          and(
            eq(schema.paperTrades.id, args.id),
            current.reviewedAt == null
              ? isNull(schema.paperTrades.reviewedAt)
              : eq(schema.paperTrades.reviewedAt, current.reviewedAt),
          ),
        )
        .returning({ id: schema.paperTrades.id });
      if (!updated[0]) {
        throw new Error(
          `saveReview: 记录 #${args.id} 已被其他写入更新（乐观并发冲突），未写入，请刷新后重试。`,
        );
      }
      ctx.invalidateQueries();
      return { ok: true };
    },
  }),

  getLivePrices: defineAction({
    description:
      "Fetches current prices (Gate.io futures first, Gate spot, then Binance USDT perpetual) for the symbols that still have a live position: every symbol with an open (unreviewed) paper trade, PLUS every symbol with a reviewed-but-interim holding (P2-21, 2026-11-17 — status reviewed, outcome note still interim per isInterim, verdict correct/wrong: a real position that has not finally settled and still needs the fallback price). Finally settled symbols and no_trigger rows are not priced. Symbols no source can price come back in `failures` with each source's own error, never silently dropped.",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: z.object({
      prices: z.array(
        z.object({
          symbol: z.string(),
          price: z.number(),
          change_pct_24h: z.number().nullable(),
          source: z.string(),
        }),
      ),
      failures: z.array(
        z.object({
          symbol: z.string(),
          errors: z.array(z.string()),
        }),
      ),
      fetched_at: z.string(),
      error: z.string().nullable(),
    }),
    async handler(ctx) {
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      // Price set = open rows ∪ reviewed interim holdings (P2-21).
      // isInterim reads the outcome note, so full rows are loaded
      // rather than just the symbol column.
      const rows = await db.select().from(schema.paperTrades);
      const symbolSet = new Set<string>();
      for (const row of rows) {
        if (row.status === "open") {
          symbolSet.add(row.symbol);
        } else if (
          row.status === "reviewed" &&
          isInterim(row) &&
          (row.verdict === "correct" || row.verdict === "wrong")
        ) {
          symbolSet.add(row.symbol);
        }
      }
      const symbols = [...symbolSet].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
      const prices: {
        symbol: string;
        price: number;
        change_pct_24h: number | null;
        source: string;
      }[] = [];
      const failures: { symbol: string; errors: string[] }[] = [];
      await Promise.all(
        symbols.map(async (symbol) => {
          const p = await fetchSymbolPrice(symbol);
          if (p.ok) {
            prices.push({
              symbol,
              price: p.value.price,
              change_pct_24h: p.value.changePct24h,
              source: p.value.source,
            });
          } else {
            failures.push({ symbol, errors: p.errors });
          }
        }),
      );
      prices.sort((a, b) => a.symbol.localeCompare(b.symbol));
      failures.sort((a, b) => a.symbol.localeCompare(b.symbol));
      return {
        prices,
        failures,
        fetched_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        error:
          prices.length === 0 && symbols.length > 0
            ? `全部 ${symbols.length} 个币种都取不到现价：${failures
                .map((f) => `${f.symbol}（${f.errors.join("；")}）`)
                .join(" | ")}`
            : null,
      };
    },
  }),

  getConditionStates: defineAction({
    description:
      "Lifecycle timestamps for trigger-pending paper legs, all judged on Gate futures 15m candles since the leg was recorded — the same source and caliber the settlement review uses. Only CLOSED candles are ever scanned (P1-03, 2026-11-17): the still-forming candle is dropped, so a close-based judgment can never be made on a candle that has not closed yet. For every open 乙路 (breakout-retest) leg: whether its precondition has happened yet (a 15-minute close beyond the breakout line written in the leg's rationale) and, when it has, condition_met_at = that candle's close time — the moment the leg entered the 挂单中 state. For every reviewed position leg the review confirmed as triggered (verdict correct/wrong) that ran a trigger-pending plan (conditional entry or a 甲路/乙路 dual path): triggered_at = the leg's real 开仓 time by the settlement caliber — for 乙路, the close of the first post-break candle that closed inside the retest band between the breakout line and the entry price; for any other leg, the start of the first 15m candle that touched the entry price after placement — as opposed to opened_at, which is only when the call was recorded. The 开仓 time is PERSISTED on the trade row the first time it is computed (user: 把开仓时间写进空间存住）: a leg with a stored triggered_at reads it back verbatim and is never re-derived or overwritten. A leg whose line cannot be parsed, or whose candles cannot be fetched, reports condition_met=false and null timestamps: no evidence, nothing invented. Each state also carries data_status (P2-11, 2026-11-17) so a caller can tell 'checked, condition not met' apart from 'could not check': 'unavailable' when the candle fetch failed or returned nothing for a leg that needs deriving, 'insufficient_history' when the fetched candles start more than one candle after that symbol's earliest leg was placed (history does not reach back to placement), and 'ok' otherwise — including legs that simply read back their stored triggered_at. Rows flagged triggered_at_unverified are excluded from derivation entirely (no scan, no readback, no write-back).",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: z.object({
      states: z.array(
        z.object({
          id: z.number(),
          condition_met: z.boolean(),
          condition_met_at: z.string().nullable(),
          triggered_at: z.string().nullable(),
          data_status: z.enum(["ok", "unavailable", "insufficient_history"]),
        }),
      ),
      fetched_at: z.string(),
    }),
    async handler(ctx) {
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      const rows = await db.select().from(schema.paperTrades);
      type Leg = { row: TradeRow; line: number | null; kind: "yi_open" | "triggered" };
      const legs: Leg[] = [];
      // Legs whose 开仓 time is already stored read it back verbatim
      // and skip the candle scan entirely (see the description).
      const storedStates: {
        id: number;
        condition_met: boolean;
        condition_met_at: string | null;
        triggered_at: string | null;
        data_status: "ok" | "unavailable" | "insufficient_history";
      }[] = [];
      for (const row of rows) {
        // Rows whose 开仓 time the ledger flagged as unverified
        // (P1-02) are excluded from derivation entirely: no candle
        // scan, no stored-state readback, no write-back.
        if (row.triggeredAtUnverified) continue;
        if (row.direction === "watch") continue;
        const p = planOf(row);
        const pending =
          row.entryType === "conditional" ||
          p.startsWith("甲路") ||
          p.startsWith("乙路");
        if (!pending) continue;
        if (row.status === "open") {
          // Only open 乙路 legs carry a separate precondition stage.
          if (pathOfRow(row) !== "乙路") continue;
          legs.push({ row, line: breakoutLineOfRow(row), kind: "yi_open" });
        } else if (
          row.status === "reviewed" &&
          (row.verdict === "correct" || row.verdict === "wrong")
        ) {
          // The review confirmed this leg triggered; locate WHEN on
          // the same candles. no_trigger legs never opened and get
          // no entry — there is no 开仓 time to report for them.
          if (row.triggeredAt != null) {
            storedStates.push({
              id: row.id,
              condition_met: false,
              condition_met_at: null,
              triggered_at: row.triggeredAt.toISOString(),
              // Read back verbatim from storage — no candle
              // derivation was needed, so the data is fine by
              // definition.
              data_status: "ok",
            });
            continue;
          }
          legs.push({
            row,
            line:
              pathOfRow(row) === "乙路" ? breakoutLineOfRow(row) : null,
            kind: "triggered",
          });
        }
      }
      // One candle fetch per symbol, from its earliest leg's placement.
      const bySymbol = new Map<string, Leg[]>();
      for (const leg of legs) {
        const arr = bySymbol.get(leg.row.symbol);
        if (arr) arr.push(leg);
        else bySymbol.set(leg.row.symbol, [leg]);
      }
      type State = {
        id: number;
        condition_met: boolean;
        condition_met_at: string | null;
        triggered_at: string | null;
        data_status: "ok" | "unavailable" | "insufficient_history";
      };
      const states: State[] = [];
      const nowSec = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
      await Promise.all(
        [...bySymbol.entries()].map(async ([symbol, symLegs]) => {
          type Candle = {
            openAt: number;
            closeAt: number;
            high: number;
            low: number;
            close: number;
          };
          let candles: Candle[] = [];
          let fetchFailed = false;
          try {
            const earliest = Math.min(
              ...symLegs.map((l) => l.row.openedAt.getTime()),
            );
            // Paginated fetch (P2-30, 2026-11-17): Gate caps one
            // candlestick query at 2000 points, so the range from
            // this symbol's earliest placement to now is walked in
            // sequential 2000-candle (2000 × 900s) windows, at most
            // 10 windows (≈ 208 days). The old single-window cap
            // silently cut off breakout evidence older than ~20.83
            // days. Windows accumulate and then go through the same
            // normalization below (ascending, deduped, closed only).
            const startSec = Math.floor(earliest / 1000);
            const windowSec = 2000 * 900;
            for (
              let winFrom = startSec, win = 0;
              winFrom <= nowSec && win < 10;
              winFrom += windowSec, win++
            ) {
              const winTo = Math.min(winFrom + windowSec - 1, nowSec);
              const url = `https://api.gateio.ws/api/v4/futures/usdt/candlesticks?contract=${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}_USDT&interval=15m&from=${winFrom}&to=${winTo}`;
              // One retry per window: with ~25 symbols fetched in
              // parallel, a single slow response would otherwise
              // leave that symbol's legs timestamp-less until the
              // next poll.
              let data: unknown = null;
              for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
                try {
                  data = await fetchJson(url);
                  break;
                } catch {
                  if (attempt === 1)
                    throw new Error("candlestick fetch failed");
                }
              }
              if (Array.isArray(data)) {
                for (const c of data) {
                  const o = (c ?? {}) as {
                    t?: unknown;
                    h?: unknown;
                    l?: unknown;
                    c?: unknown;
                  };
                  const openAt = Number(o.t) * 1000;
                  const candle = {
                    openAt,
                    closeAt: openAt + 900 * 1000,
                    high: Number(o.h),
                    low: Number(o.l),
                    close: Number(o.c),
                  };
                  if (
                    Number.isFinite(candle.openAt) &&
                    candle.close > 0 &&
                    candle.high > 0 &&
                    candle.low > 0
                  ) {
                    candles.push(candle);
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // No candles → no evidence; the legs below report
            // condition_met=false and null timestamps, and the next
            // poll retries. The failure is still REPORTED through
            // data_status so a caller never mistakes it for a
            // checked-and-unmet condition (P2-11).
            candles = [];
            fetchFailed = true;
          }
          // Normalize before any scan (P1-03): ascending by open
          // time, one candle per open time, and CLOSED candles
          // only — the still-forming candle (closeAt in the
          // future) is dropped, so the close-confirmation, retest
          // and touch scans below can only ever read candles that
          // have actually closed.
          {
            const nowMs = Date.now();
            const seenOpenAt = new Set<number>();
            candles = candles
              .filter((c) => c.closeAt <= nowMs)
              .sort((a, b) => a.openAt - b.openAt)
              .filter((c) => {
                if (seenOpenAt.has(c.openAt)) return false;
                seenOpenAt.add(c.openAt);
                return true;
              });
          }
          // This symbol's data status for every leg that needs
          // deriving (P2-11): a failed or empty fetch is
          // "unavailable"; candles that start more than one candle
          // (900000 ms) after the earliest leg's placement cannot
          // cover the placement moment, so any judgment would rest
          // on partial history — "insufficient_history".
          const dataStatus: State["data_status"] =
            fetchFailed || candles.length === 0
              ? "unavailable"
              : (() => {
                  const firstCandle = candles[0];
                  if (!firstCandle) return "unavailable" as const;
                  const earliestLegOpenedMs = Math.min(
                    ...symLegs.map((l) => l.row.openedAt.getTime()),
                  );
                  return firstCandle.openAt >
                    earliestLegOpenedMs + 900_000
                    ? ("insufficient_history" as const)
                    : ("ok" as const);
                })();
          for (const leg of symLegs) {
            const { row, line } = leg;
            const openedMs = row.openedAt.getTime();
            // The break close （乙路 precondition): the first 15m
            // candle that CLOSED beyond the line after placement.
            let breakCloseAt: number | null = null;
            if (line != null) {
              for (const c of candles) {
                if (
                  c.closeAt > openedMs &&
                  (row.direction === "long"
                    ? c.close > line
                    : c.close < line)
                ) {
                  breakCloseAt = c.closeAt;
                  break;
                }
              }
            }
            if (leg.kind === "yi_open") {
              states.push({
                id: row.id,
                condition_met: breakCloseAt != null,
                condition_met_at:
                  breakCloseAt != null
                    ? new Date(breakCloseAt).toISOString()
                    : null,
                triggered_at: null,
                data_status: dataStatus,
              });
              continue;
            }
            // A confirmed-triggered leg's 开仓 time, by the same
            // caliber the settlement notes record:
            //  · 乙路： the entry is booked when a candle after the
            //    break CLOSES inside the retest band between the
            //    breakout line and the entry price （收在回踩带内
            //    确认入场） — the position opens at that candle's
            //    close. A literal touch test fails these legs:
            //    the booked entry is the band's edge, which price
            //    may never exactly reach (ELSA 乙 id 84: entry
            //    0.0600, post-placement high 0.05959, yet the leg
            //    triggered on the 09:15 candle's in-band close).
            //  · Any other leg （甲路 / single conditional): price
            //    reaching the entry IS the trigger — the first
            //    candle at or after placement whose range touches
            //    the entry price; its open is the earliest moment
            //    the touch is known to have happened inside it,
            //    and a touch inside the placement candle itself
            //    reads as the placement time.
            // Either way the trigger must precede the review that
            // confirmed it. If the 乙路 band scan finds nothing
            // (unparseable line, candle gap), it falls back to the
            // touch test after the break close.
            const startMs = breakCloseAt ?? openedMs;
            const endMs = row.reviewedAt
              ? row.reviewedAt.getTime()
              : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
            let triggeredAt: number | null = null;
            if (leg.line != null && breakCloseAt != null) {
              const bandLo = Math.min(leg.line, row.entryPrice);
              const bandHi = Math.max(leg.line, row.entryPrice);
              for (const c of candles) {
                if (c.closeAt <= breakCloseAt) continue;
                if (c.openAt >= endMs) break;
                // The retest must be confirmed by a candle that
                // had CLOSED by the review instant (P1-03) — a
                // candle still open at endMs cannot confirm
                // anything; candles are sorted, so stop there.
                if (c.closeAt > endMs) break;
                if (c.close >= bandLo && c.close <= bandHi) {
                  triggeredAt = c.closeAt;
                  break;
                }
              }
            }
            if (triggeredAt == null) {
              for (const c of candles) {
                if (c.closeAt <= startMs) continue;
                if (c.openAt >= endMs) break;
                if (c.low <= row.entryPrice && row.entryPrice <= c.high) {
                  triggeredAt = Math.max(c.openAt, openedMs);
                  break;
                }
              }
            }
            if (triggeredAt != null) {
              // Persist the 开仓 time on first computation (user:
              // 把开仓时间写进空间存住）. The isNull guard keeps an
              // already-stored value authoritative, and a failed
              // write must not fail the read — the next poll retries.
              try {
                await db
                  .update(schema.paperTrades)
                  .set({ triggeredAt: new Date(triggeredAt) })
                  .where(
                    and(
                      eq(schema.paperTrades.id, row.id),
                      isNull(schema.paperTrades.triggeredAt),
                    ),
                  );
              } catch {
                // Read path stays up; persistence retries next poll.
              }
            }
            states.push({
              id: row.id,
              condition_met: false,
              condition_met_at: null,
              triggered_at:
                triggeredAt != null
                  ? new Date(triggeredAt).toISOString()
                  : null,
              data_status: dataStatus,
            });
          }
        }),
      );
      states.push(...storedStates);
      states.sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id);
      return { states, fetched_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    },
  }),

  getSymbolPrices: defineAction({
    description:
      "Fetches current USDT prices for arbitrary symbols (Gate.io futures first, Gate spot, then Binance USDT perpetual), so a call on any coin — including small caps with no Gate spot pair — can be recorded against a live reference price. Symbols no source lists come back with a null price and the per-source failure reasons in `error`.",
    request: z.object({
      symbols: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(20)).min(1).max(50),
    }),
    response: z.object({
      prices: z.array(
        z.object({
          symbol: z.string(),
          price: z.number().nullable(),
          change_pct_24h: z.number().nullable(),
          source: z.string().nullable(),
          error: z.string().nullable(),
        }),
      ),
      fetched_at: z.string(),
    }),
    async handler(_ctx, args) {
      const symbols = [
        ...new Set(args.symbols.map((s) => s.trim().toUpperCase())),
      ];
      const prices = await Promise.all(
        symbols.map(async (symbol) => {
          const p = await fetchSymbolPrice(symbol);
          return p.ok
            ? {
                symbol,
                price: p.value.price,
                change_pct_24h: p.value.changePct24h,
                source: p.value.source as string | null,
                error: null,
              }
            : {
                symbol,
                price: null,
                change_pct_24h: null,
                source: null,
                error: p.errors.join("；"),
              };
        }),
      );
      return { prices, fetched_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    },
  }),

  getCoinAccounts: defineAction({
    description:
      "Returns the per-major coin account states (BTC/ETH/SOL): each major runs an independent 100U paper account from round 7 on — balance (= 100 + that coin's cumulative realized P/L), pnl_total, and whether it is busted (balance ≤ 0). Busted is a statistical marker only (the user is push-notified separately); paper testing keeps running for the coin and R scoring is unaffected, and the balance may go negative and keeps being recorded. A major with no stored row yet returns the initial state (100 / 0 / not busted).",
    request: z.object({}),
    response: z.object({
      accounts: z.array(
        z.object({
          symbol: z.string(),
          balance: z.number(),
          pnl_total: z.number(),
          busted: z.boolean(),
        }),
      ),
    }),
    async handler(ctx) {
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      const rows = await db.select().from(schema.coinAccounts);
      const bySymbol = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.symbol, r]));
      return {
        accounts: COIN_ACCOUNT_SYMBOLS.map((symbol) => {
          const row = bySymbol.get(symbol);
          return row
            ? {
                symbol,
                balance: row.balance,
                pnl_total: row.pnlTotal,
                busted: row.busted,
              }
            : { symbol, balance: 100, pnl_total: 0, busted: false };
        }),
      };
    },
  }),

  setCoinBalance: defineAction({
    description:
      "Upserts one major coin's account state after settlement: {symbol (BTC/ETH/SOL), balance, pnl_total, busted}. The payload must satisfy the account invariants (balance = 100 + pnl_total within 0.01, and busted exactly when balance ≤ 0) or the whole write is rejected. Called by the external settlement flow; the coin card headers read the stored values back via getCoinAccounts.",
    request: z.object({
      symbol: z.enum(["BTC", "ETH", "SOL"]),
      balance: z.number(),
      pnl_total: z.number(),
      busted: z.boolean(),
    }),
    response: z.object({ ok: z.literal(true) }),
    async handler(ctx, args): Promise<{ ok: true }> {
      // Invariant check (P2-15, 2026-11-17): the schema defines
      // balance = 100 + pnl_total and busted ⇔ balance ≤ 0. A
      // payload that contradicts either relation is rejected whole —
      // nothing is written — instead of storing a self-inconsistent
      // account state.
      if (Math.abs(args.balance - (100 + args.pnl_total)) > 0.01) {
        throw new Error(
          `setCoinBalance: ${args.symbol} balance (${args.balance}) 与 100 + pnl_total (${100 + args.pnl_total}) 不一致，未写入。`,
        );
      }
      if (args.busted !== (args.balance <= 0)) {
        throw new Error(
          `setCoinBalance: ${args.symbol} busted (${args.busted}) 与 balance (${args.balance}) 的爆仓判定（balance ≤ 0）不一致，未写入。`,
        );
      }
      const db = ctx.db<typeof schema>();
      await db
        .insert(schema.coinAccounts)
        .values({
          symbol: args.symbol,
          balance: args.balance,
          pnlTotal: args.pnl_total,
          busted: args.busted,
        })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({
          target: schema.coinAccounts.symbol,
          set: {
            balance: args.balance,
            pnlTotal: args.pnl_total,
            busted: args.busted,
          },
        });
      ctx.invalidateQueries();
      return { ok: true };
    },
  }),
} satisfies ActionsModule;
```

### drizzle/0001_initial.sql

  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  text TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
```

### drizzle/0002_paper_trades_ledger.sql

```sql
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS entries;
--> statement-breakpoint
CREATE TABLE paper_trades (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  symbol TEXT NOT NULL,
  direction TEXT NOT NULL,
  entry_price REAL NOT NULL,
  stop_loss REAL NOT NULL,
  take_profit REAL NOT NULL,
  rationale TEXT NOT NULL,
  opened_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
  reviewed_at INTEGER,
  verdict TEXT,
  review_price REAL,
  window_high REAL,
  window_low REAL,
  first_touch TEXT,
  outcome_note TEXT,
  reasoning_review TEXT
);
```
